# Progress Tracker — SocietyLedger Docs Site, Admin Console & Mobile

> **Living document.** Updated after every feature ships. A new session reads this first to know exactly where things stand. Build the **next unchecked item in the Master sequence** whose dependencies are checked (order and details in `build-plan.md`). Docs track (D) comes first, then the admin track (A), then the mobile track (F), with D7.x after mobile milestones.

## Current status
- **Track / phase:** Mobile Phase 0 — Backend enablement **complete** (F0.1–F0.6, all shipped 2026-10-19). Phase 1 — Foundation **complete** (F1.1–F1.8, all shipped — F1.1/F1.2 on 2026-10-19 ahead of Phase 0 per owner's explicit request, see Decisions log; F1.3–F1.8 on 2026-10-20). Phase 2 — Authentication & member core underway (F2.1–F2.9 shipped).
- **Latest (2026-10-27):** App Downloads. The landing page offers the Android APK behind a form (first name, last name, email, phone, required consent). The admin console gets an App Downloads section (Overview analytics, Releases, Leads with CSV/Excel export). New tables app_releases, download_leads, download_events (migration 20261106); API in apps/api/src/modules/app-downloads; 14 rules tests pass; flow smoke-tested against the local DB. Also: splash wordmark no longer clipped on Android 12+ (splash images rebuilt to sit inside the circular mask). Owner: upload the APK to Cloudflare, add the release in the admin console and make it current.
- **Planned (2026-10-27):** Track T — Thrift (Ajo) add-on. Specification written (`thrift-ajo-spec.md`), build plan added (`build-plan.md` Track T, 55 features), architecture section §18, screens and docs pages in `project-overview.md §12`. Scope reduced by the owner the same day: one money-flow mode (the group's own bank account), no NIN/BVN or other government-ID verification, no custody of contribution money. All owner decisions (prices, levy, verification, payout order, trust score) were made 2026-10-27, so T0 is complete. Nothing built yet; T1 (foundation) is next.
- **Latest (2026-10-27):** Ajo Track T1 (foundation) built in `apps/api` and the admin Plan Builder editor: data model, module/add-on catalogue with the `ajoContributors` quota and read-only expiry, standalone thrift-group tenant kind, Ajo permissions and role templates, append-only ledger, platform settings. 69 new tests in `__tests__/ajo/` plus 5 in `quote.test.ts`. Nothing user-visible to members yet; next is T2 (member approval, verification, payout account). Not run against a production copy; DB-backed billing suites need `BILLING_TEST_DATABASE_URL`.
- **Latest (2026-10-27, later):** Ajo Track T2 built in `apps/api`: membership approval and activation, optional SMS phone code, encrypted payout accounts with a cool-off, and SocietyLedger's verification step with its payout guard. 159 tests in `__tests__/ajo/`, including a database suite (`ajo-members.integration.test.ts`, 37 tests against real Postgres: transactions, the contributor-limit lock under real concurrency, audit rows, a check that no full account number reaches a response, audit row, notification or log). Run it with `AJO_TEST_DATABASE_URL` pointing at a database whose name contains "test"; add `--forceExit` because an imported module keeps Jest alive. Next is T3 (groups, rules, schedules).
- **Latest (2026-10-28):** Ajo Track T3 built in `apps/api`: groups, rules, schedules, shares, the payout-order draw, swaps and exceptions, guarantors and collector assignments, with migration `20261110_ajo_groups_schedules` (applied to the development and scratch databases). 289 tests in `__tests__/ajo/` including a 46-test database suite (`ajo-groups.integration.test.ts`) and 31 route tests; two mutation checks (removing the paid-installment protection, and forgetting which positions are paid) were each caught by the tests. Next is T4 (contributions and collections), which must call `assertCanContribute`, `businessDay` and read the member's `ruleVersionInForce`.
- **Latest (2026-10-28, later):** Ajo Track T4 built in `apps/api`: contributions, allocation, confirmation and reversal, bank-transfer proofs with duplicate checks, the collector's day and line-by-line handover reconcile, and receipts, with migration `20261111_ajo_contributions` (applied to the development and scratch databases). 380 tests in `__tests__/ajo/` including a 46-test database suite (`ajo-contributions.integration.test.ts`) and 20 route tests; three mutation checks (newest-first allocation, no single-winner guard on confirm, no separation of duties) were each caught. Next is T5 (payouts): it must call `assertPayoutEligible` (T2), write `round` = the slot's position on `AjoPayout`, and read confirmed money from the ledger. T6 must treat a payment awaiting confirmation (REPORTED, or CASH_RECEIVED) as "being verified" and pause late fees while it is.
- **Latest (2026-10-28, latest):** Ajo Track T5 built in `apps/api`: payout calculation and preview, prepare → approve → record, failure, voiding and reversal, supplementary payouts, closing a group, and the platform levy with its staff view and its line on the billing checkout, with migration `20261112_ajo_payouts_levy` (applied to the development and scratch databases). 477 tests in `__tests__/ajo/` including a 40-test payout suite, a 17-test levy suite and a 6-test suite that drives a real billing checkout; the existing 141 billing tests (database ones included) still pass; four mutation checks (skipping the covered-by-confirmed-money check, letting the preparer approve, removing the 60-day rule, and not holding a slot with an open payout still) were each caught. The Ajo money-boundary test is unchanged: the Ajo module still never touches the subscription tables, it asks billing (`held-addon.service.ts`). Next is T6 (defaults and risk): reminders and the grace pause for a payment being verified, late fees (from `ruleVersionInForce`), default cases, trust score, disputes.
- **Latest (2026-10-28, newest):** Ajo Track T6 built in `apps/api`: overdue detection and reminders, late fees with disclosure and a 48-hour notice, disputes, default cases and repayment plans, the trust score, and the hourly and daily jobs, with migration `20261113_ajo_defaults_risk` (applied to the development and scratch databases). Verified: 574 Ajo tests pass together, 141 billing tests pass, the wider run shows only the two known `multi-tenancy` failures, typecheck and lint are clean, six mutation checks were each caught. Found and fixed while testing: job-opened cases and plans wrote an audit row with user id 0 and failed on the foreign key. Not verified: a live server run, real SMS or push delivery, the migration on a production copy. No screens yet, so `ui-registry.md` is unchanged.
- **Latest (2026-10-28, web phase):** Ajo Track T7 built in `apps/web` and `apps/api`: member side, group admin, members and payout order, collections and collectors, payouts and cases, and the standalone sign-up. **Not yet looked at in a browser.** API additions: `GET /api/ajo/me`, `GET /api/ajo/people`, `GET /api/ajo/collector-candidates`, `tenantKind` on `/api/ajo/status`, and `password` required on payout approve, payout reverse and case close. Verified: 581 Ajo tests pass together (twice), 145 billing tests pass, both apps typecheck, lint shows only the existing warnings. Fixed along the way: the Ajo menu entries sat in a sidebar members never see; an unexplained "Test suite failed to run" in the Ajo suites was TypeScript merging the top-level names of non-module test files, now fixed by making every Ajo test file a module.
- **Latest (2026-10-29, thrift onboarding):** Standalone thrift-group onboarding built (`thrift-ajo-spec.md` §15): `/onboarding/thrift` with five steps, a shared `GroupWizard`, step 3 with typed rows and CSV (200 rows), a new `POST /ajo/groups/:id/members/bulk`, team roles `admin`/`ajo_officer`/`ajo_collector`, a review that opens the circle, routing by kind both ways, and a resume card. The trial now includes Growth features through a hidden `ajo-trial` add-on (run the Ajo seed on every database). Also fixed while testing: `thrift-signup` was blocked by the tenant check, platform settings could not be saved (the table required an association; migration `20261114_platform_settings_nullable_association`; the insert named a column that does not exist), the admin Settings page sent its whole reply as the settings, verify-email checked the code against the wrong account when one email exists in several associations, and the admin console now has the standalone sign-up switch. Verified: 605 Ajo tests, 146 billing, 84 auth and tenant tests, both typecheck, lint clean. **Not looked at in a browser; the whole sign-up to onboarding to open-circle path has not been run end to end.**
- **Latest (2026-10-29, how often):** A circle can now collect daily, weekly, monthly, quarterly, annually, or on its own step ("every N days / weeks / months": bi-weekly, every two months, every 10 days). Migration `20261115_ajo_flexible_frequency` (applied to the development and test databases; existing groups are unchanged). The schedule engine, the group rules, reminders, repayment plans, the create wizard (so onboarding too) and every screen that names the frequency use it. "Bi-monthly" is deliberately not a label: the hint says "twice a month: choose 15 days or 2 weeks". Verified: 622 Ajo tests (17 new), both apps typecheck, lint clean, two mutation checks caught. **Not looked at in a browser.**
- **Latest (2026-10-29, mobile phase):** Ajo Track T8 built in `apps/mobile`, in full parity with the web except three things the owner removed on purpose (below). Contributor screens under Money → My thrift (`money/thrift/*`: circles, circle detail with schedule, payments, late fees, trust score, claims, receipts, phone code, swap request, leave; pay with proof; rules and acceptance; payout account and verification status). Collector screens (`manage/thrift/today`), online only: today's list, record cash, end-of-day handover, a "needs internet" banner, and no offline queue (Ajo mutations use `networkMode: 'always'` so they fail at once instead of waiting). Staff screens under Manage → Thrift (`manage/thrift/*`): circles list, six-step create wizard, circle overview with open/start/cancel/edit, members (approve, guarantor link or recorded agreement, shares), payout order (draw, swaps, emergency change), payments (confirm, turn down, reverse, slip), cash handovers (count and reconcile), collectors, payouts (preview, prepare, approve, record paid, fail, void, reverse, complete), arrears (late fees, claims, default cases with plans). Standalone thrift: sign-up (`(auth)/create-thrift-group`, linked from Welcome), the five-step onboarding wizard (`onboarding/thrift`, resumable, skippable), trimmed tabs (no Home or Community; Money becomes Thrift) and a resume card. Push and deep links: `src/lib/ajoPush.ts` (every `ajo.*` push type, by role) and `societyledger://thrift/…`. **Removed from mobile by the owner:** the guarantor's consent screen (the link opens in the browser for everyone), CSV bulk upload of contributors (people are typed in one at a time; bulk stays on the web), offline collection. **Not verified:** nothing has been run on a device or emulator, and the new Maestro flows (`08-thrift-record-cash`, `09-thrift-reconcile`) are written from the code and not yet run. `tsc` shows only the 6 errors that were already there; lint has no new warnings; 329 jest tests pass in the Ajo, lib, api, session, onboarding and sign-up areas. Four screen-render suites fail to start or fail in this environment: `manage/index` (checked: it fails the same way with the Ajo change taken out), and `money/donations/index`, `NeedsAttentionList` and `createAssociation.screen`, none of which touch Ajo code (not individually re-checked against a clean tree).
- **Latest (2026-10-29, transparency phase A):** Track T13 phase A built: the Circle board for rotating pots (spec `thrift-ajo-spec.md` §16). API: `memberVisibility`/`nameDisplay` on rule versions (migration `20261117_ajo_member_visibility`, on by default for a new rotating pot, always off for collector thrift), one status service `ajo-status.ts` (21 tests), and eight member-safe board endpoints under `/api/ajo/groups/:id/board` built from explicit field lists (16 database tests, including the leak test: no phone, email, address, bank account, guarantor, note or proof link in any view). Web: `Circle board` on the member's circle page and the officer's group workspace. Mobile: a `Circle` tab for a standalone rotating pot, and a `Circle board` button on the circle page for association members. Verified: 679 Ajo API tests pass, web and mobile typecheck (mobile shows only its 6 old errors), lint adds no warnings. **Not looked at in a browser or on a device.** Phases B (collector-thrift cycles), C and D are not started.
- **Latest (2026-10-29, transparency phase B):** Track T13 phase B built: collector-thrift cycles (spec §16.5). Each saver chooses one amount (at least the group's smallest) and it stays the same for the whole cycle; at the end they withdraw or continue, and **no answer means withdraw**. The collector's fee is one of that saver's contributions (the existing "days of contribution" fee, set to 1). A continuing saver's savings, less that fee, carry into the next cycle; the admin starts it once the cycle is over and every withdrawing saver has been paid. Migration `20261118_ajo_collector_cycles` (a cycle row per saver, `cycle_no` on groups, installments and payouts; existing groups become one cycle at the group's amount, so no schedule changes). API: `PUT /memberships/:id/amount`, `GET /memberships/:id/cycle`, `POST /memberships/:id/cycle/decision`, `GET /groups/:id/cycle`, `POST /groups/:id/cycles/next`, a daily "your cycle is ending" question (once per saver). Web: a Your savings cycle card on the member's circle page, a Savings cycle tab for officers, an amount field when adding a saver. Mobile: the same card, a Savings cycle screen under the circle, an amount field in Add a person. Verified: 16 new database tests and 15 pure tests pass, web and mobile typecheck (mobile only its 6 old errors). **The whole Ajo suite is not cleanly green on this machine:** the full run passed 703 of 711, and the 8 failures were Prisma 5-second transaction timeouts on simple queries (a loaded machine and a slow scratch database); a different set failed each run, and the ones re-run alone passed (schedule repair, the daily job, the cycles and contributions suites). The other failing tests were not each re-run alone. **Not looked at in a browser or on a device.**
- **Latest (2026-10-29, transparency phase C):** Track T13 phase C built: a collector-thrift saver's own view (spec §16.6). API (`ajo-savings.service.ts`, `ajo-savings-statement.ts`): `GET /memberships/:id/savings` (saved, reported-apart, progress, behind or up to date, "what you will receive" with the fee, payout status and why it is held, and the calendar), `/payments` (who took each payment, when it was confirmed, receipt number), `/cash` ("Where my cash is": recorded, handed in, office confirmed, with late steps flagged and an instalment to ask about), `/collector`, `/record` (trust band, streak, past cycles), `/statement?format=pdf|csv` and `GET /contributions/:id/receipt.pdf`. Only the saver or an officer can read them; a collector assigned to the saver cannot. Web: a "My savings" page with six tabs; mobile: the same as a "My savings" screen with a share sheet for statements and receipts. Verified: 12 new database tests, web and mobile typecheck (mobile only its 6 old errors). **Not looked at in a browser or on a device; the share sheet needs the build that includes expo-sharing.**
- **Latest (2026-10-29, transparency phase D):** Track T13 phase D built, which completes the transparency work (spec §16). (1) **Circle statement**: `GET /groups/:id/board/statement.pdf` builds a one-page PDF from the same board views a member sees (so it matches the board and shows nothing the board does not), with a random code; `GET /api/public/ajo/statement/:code` and the page `/verify/circle/[code]` show when it was made and whether the circle's figures still match, with totals only and never a name. (2) **Notifications** (`ajo-circle-notices.service.ts`): round fully paid, payout recorded, your turn in 2 rounds, and an opt-in weekly summary, each switchable off at `/ajo/notification-preferences`, sent once (a claim row per notice), only for circles with the board on, naming a person only if they consented; run from the daily job and after a payment, handover or payout is confirmed. (3) **Officer accountability** (`ajo-accountability.service.ts`): who prepared, approved and recorded each payout (one person doing both is flagged), the confirmation backlog by age against the 48-hour target, cash by collector and day, and exceptions with reasons, plus a CSV. (4) **Docs page** "What members can see in a circle". Web and mobile screens for all of it. Migrations `20261119_ajo_statement_codes` and `20261120_ajo_circle_notices`. Verified: 8 statement, 6 accountability and 10 notification database tests pass; docs `check:frontmatter` and `check:links` pass; web, API and docs typecheck, mobile only its 6 old errors. **Not looked at in a browser or on a device.**
- **Latest (2026-10-29, two fixes found by using the app):** (1) The rules screens crashed ("is not iterable") on web and mobile because the API sends `{ versions, acceptance? }` and both clients read it as a bare list; fixed in the web and mobile rules pages and both onboarding steps, the rules page now says "14 of 20 people have accepted version 3", and a mobile test uses the real reply shape. This had been wrong since the first web and mobile builds, which were never run against a real browser or device. (2) Starting a circle with nobody approved gave a refusal (`NOT_ENOUGH_PARTICIPANTS`) shown at the top of the page, away from the button; now the panel says "Nobody is approved yet. Approve at least one person on Members, then start the circle." with a Go to Members button and Start disabled, and any refusal from Start or Open for joining appears under its own button, with an icon and words. The server still enforces the rule. `startReadiness` has a unit test. **Not looked at in a browser or on a device.**

## How to update (after each feature)
1. Tick the box, add the date and a one-line note (what shipped, anything notable).
2. Update **Current status** above.
3. Add any Backend gaps, Decisions or Known issues discovered.
4. Commit with the feature: `docs(context): progress F<x.y>`.

Format for a completed item:
`- [x] F1.2 Theme, tokens & NativeWind — 2026-10-15 — NativeWind 4.2.x; dark vars via .dark:root; gallery added`

---

That caught two real bugs fixed along the way: `turbo prune --docker` doesn't carry a plain relative-path `tsconfig.json` `extends` across the workspace boundary (needed an explicit `COPY tsconfig.base.json`), and nginx's template `envsubst` crashes the container if a templated var is merely *absent* rather than empty (needed `ENV ROBOTS_TAG=""` in the Dockerfile). Verified serving correctly: security/cache headers, `X-Robots-Tag` present only when `ROBOTS_TAG` is set (confirms the noindex-on-staging design works), 404 page, `/api/search`, `/robots.txt`, `/sitemap.xml`. `docker-compose.dokploy.yml` gained the `docs` service (same one-Compose-stack model as `api`/`web`) and — while here — finally added the explicit Traefik `priority=1` to `web`'s wildcard router that `architecture.md §17.3` had flagged as a latent gap since `web` shipped alone; `library-docs.md`'s Dokploy section (its last remaining stale "one Dokploy Application per app" wording, deliberately deferred since D1.2) is corrected to match. `.github/workflows/docs.yml` built to the exact pipeline `architecture.md §14.9` specifies (install → `gen:reference` → fail-on-uncommitted-diff → type-check → lint → build → `check:links` → `check:frontmatter` → coverage PR comment); validated clean with `actionlint` via Docker (zero issues) — the preview-URL half of the DoD is Dokploy's own PR-preview feature, not something this workflow produces, so it's left to the owner to enable later. Three new scripts: `gen:reference.ts` (stub until D6.1, per the build-plan's explicit allowance), `coverage.ts` (per-section published/draft/review counts + a web-route-coverage proxy from `covers` frontmatter, reusing `sitemap-data.ts`), `check-links.ts` (regex-based internal link checker against the known-URL set — not full MDX/AST parsing, so a link built from a JS expression like `href={variable}` won't be caught; revisit if that turns out to matter). All three run clean locally (0 broken links, as expected — no real content with links exists yet beyond the kitchen-sink demo). `type-check`/`lint`/`build` all still pass. **Dokploy-side setup itself (creating the staging/production Compose projects in the Dokploy UI, DNS, the production app's "kept stopped until D6.4" state) is an operational step for the owner** — nothing in git encodes "stopped," that's a dashboard action.

  - **Module/plan gating is not actually enforced anywhere today**: `requireModule()` middleware defaults to `shadow` mode (logs only, never blocks) via `FEATURE_FLAG_MODE`, which isn't set in any env file including the Dokploy compose — and the frontend has zero handling of the `MODULE_DISABLED` error code. The "Enterprise"/"Pro"/"Beta" badges seen in the sidebar (`AdminSidebar.tsx`) are hardcoded per nav item, not computed from the association's actual plan — so `plans-and-modules.mdx` is deliberately worded to describe the badge as a hint, without claiming it blocks anything (because it currently doesn't).
  - "Push" notifications don't exist yet (mobile-only, not built) — title/content changed from the sitemap's "in-app, email, SMS and push" to "in-app, email and SMS", with a note that push arrives with the mobile app.
  - Notification preferences (`notifications.controller.ts`) are stored in an **in-memory `Map`**, not a database table — real for the user in a normal session, but lost on every server restart. Not disclosed to end users (not their concern), but logged as a Backend gap below.

Two sitemap corrections found by checking the real source instead of assuming it:
  - `changing-your-password`'s real location is `/dashboard/member/my-profile` (a "Change Password" button opening a modal), **not** `/dashboard/member/settings` as the sitemap's covers note claimed — that page has no password field at all. `covers` updated to the real route.
  - Login failure modes are more specific than the sitemap's generic "pending, rejected or suspended" — the API returns four distinct codes (`EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED`, `PENDING_APPROVAL`, `ACCOUNT_REJECTED`, `ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED`), and "suspended" is actually two different things: an individual account deactivated after previously working, or the *whole association* suspended by a platform admin (`tenant.middleware.ts`, a 403 before the login check even runs) — different people to contact for each. `account-approval-status.mdx` distinguishes all four.
  - Found (not fixed, logged below): `/dashboard/member/my-profile` fires a real, reproducible "Member profile not found" error toast (twice) for the `member@demo.com` test account on every page load, unrelated to the password-change feature itself (which works correctly regardless).

  - **RBAC gap found and fixed (demo-scoped only):** the `member` role's default permission array (`rbac-seed.ts`) has never included any permission for voting, welfare or cooperatives — confirmed by direct API calls returning `403 Permission denied` for `/api/voting/active`, `/api/welfare/dashboard` and `/api/cooperatives/my-profile` even though the member-facing pages for all three (My Voting, Welfare, My Savings/My Loans) are fully built and clearly intended for member self-service. The permissions these routes require (`ledger.view`, `ledger.create`, `members.view`) are the same **broad, officer-level** keys used to view *all* members' ledgers and the full member directory — there's no narrower `_own`-scoped permission for cooperatives/welfare/voting self-service anywhere in the codebase, unlike `ledger.view_own`/`payments.view_own`/`members.view_own` which already exist for finance. Rather than broadening the shared `member` role template (which would over-privilege every real member in every association), only the **demo association's own `member` Role row** got these 3 permissions added directly (a `RolePermission` data change, `rbac-seed.ts` untouched) — scoped to `demo` only, reversible, no production impact. See G16 below for the real fix this papers over.

A **third** bug in the same feature was found but **not fixed** — see G16.

  - **Local stack needed a restart before verification could start**: the `apps/web` dev server (running ~6 hours across prior sessions) was serving stale/broken JS chunk references, causing every page to hang on load. Restarted cleanly (`next dev -p 3002`); not a product bug, an environment issue from a long-lived dev process — worth remembering if a future session hits the same "page never loads" symptom.
  - **Real, significant security finding — meeting minutes and attendance have almost no real backend authorization, confirmed live.** `apps/api/src/lib/permissions.ts` maps `MEETING_VIEW`, `MEETING_ATTENDANCE` and `MEETING_MINUTES` all to `members.view_own` — the baseline permission every single authenticated user has in every association (member, treasurer, financial_secretary, admin alike), not an officer-only key. Confirmed live with a real `member@demo.com` token (no demo-scoped extra permissions involved — this is the **default** `member` role in **every** association): it successfully created and self-approved official minutes for a real AGM via `POST /meetings/:id/minutes` and `/minutes/approve`, and separately initialized and marked attendance for a meeting via `POST /meetings/:id/attendance/initialize` and `/attendance`. The web UI only shows these controls on the officer-facing `/dashboard/governance/meetings` pages and gates nothing further — someone who found the right URL or called the API directly could already do all of this today, in any real association, not just demo. Test data was created and then deleted immediately after confirming (not left in the database). **Not disclosed in the published docs** — `minutes.mdx`/`taking-attendance.mdx` describe the intended Administrator/secretary workflow as normal, same precedent as G25. Logged as **G26**, upgrading the old G3 finding (which only flagged the API/web constant mismatch, not that the API side is nearly unauthenticated-equivalent).
  - **Real, confirmed finding — a meeting can never be edited, and never leaves "Scheduled" through the interface.** (1) There is no `/dashboard/governance/meetings/[id]/edit` route anywhere in `apps/web` — the **Edit** button on a scheduled meeting's detail page points at a URL that doesn't exist. Live-tested twice: once the click hung the page for several minutes before the test was abandoned, once it silently did nothing — both outcomes confirm the same root cause (no matching route), not two different bugs. (2) Nothing in `meetings.service.ts` ever sets a meeting to `IN_PROGRESS`, and the only button that sets `COMPLETED` (**Complete Meeting**) only renders when status is already `IN_PROGRESS` — so a meeting scheduled through the UI can never be marked in-progress or completed by any button that exists; it stays **Scheduled** until cancelled. (3) Agenda items can only be set at creation time — the meeting detail page's Agenda panel is read-only, with no add/edit/delete control, even though the backend's `/agenda` endpoints work fine. `scheduling-a-meeting.mdx`/`building-an-agenda.mdx`/`cancelling-or-postponing.mdx` describe the real, narrower capability (set the agenda when you create the meeting; cancel rather than "postpone," since no distinct postponed state is ever set either) rather than inventing edit/in-progress/postpone steps that don't exist. Logged as **G27**.

There is no `secretary` system role anywhere in `rbac-seed.ts`'s `ROLE_PERMISSION_MAPPING` — only `admin`, `treasurer`, `financial_secretary` and `member`, and `MEETING_CREATE`/`MEETING_EDIT` both require `members.create`/`members.edit`, which only `admin` has. Documented as Administrator-only, with a note that a custom role can be created to delegate it (same pattern as D3.2's correction to `adding-a-member.mdx`).
- [x] D4.2 Elections & polls (9 pages) — 2026-10-13 — All 9 pages `published`: `index` (sibling `elections.mdx`), `how-elections-work`, `creating-an-election`, `nominations-and-candidates`, `running-the-vote`, `publishing-results`, `voter-eligibility-and-turnout`, `creating-a-poll`, `poll-results`. Hand-authored the election lifecycle diagram (`currentColor`-only inline SVG, same convention as D2.2). Verified against the local stack as `admin@demo.com`/`member@demo.com` — created a real election with positions, registered and approved real candidates, opened and closed voting, cast a real vote as a member, published results, created and ran two real polls through to closed results — all performed live.
  - **Five real, confirmed, systemic bugs found and fixed live — election and poll creation were both 100% broken for every association, through the web UI, before this session.** All five are one-line field-name/shape corrections, each confirmed broken-then-fixed with a direct `curl`/live-click before/after, same class of fix as D2.1's `isVisible()` bug and D3.1's guarantor-request bugs:
    1. **Election creation always failed** — `elections/new/page.tsx` sent the raw `datetime-local` input value (e.g. `"2027-01-20T09:00"`) straight through for `votingStartDate`/`votingEndDate`, which the API's `z.string().datetime()` validation unconditionally rejects (it requires a full ISO datetime with a `Z`/offset, which a `datetime-local` input never produces). Every election creation attempt 400'd with `"Invalid datetime"`, for every association, always — the only reason election `#1` existed at all is that it was inserted directly by `demo-association.seed.ts`, bypassing the broken form. Fixed by wrapping the four date fields in `new Date(x).toISOString()`, the same conversion `meetings/create/page.tsx` already did correctly.
    2. **Poll creation always failed**, for the identical reason (`polls/new/page.tsx`'s `startDate`/`endDate`) — same fix.
    3. **Poll creation also always failed on the *options* field even after the date fix** — the form sent `options: string[]` but `createPollOptionSchema` requires `{ optionText: string, orderNumber?: number }[]`. Fixed in `polls/new/page.tsx` (map to the real shape) and `lib/api/voting.ts`'s `createPoll()` type signature.
    4. **Candidates never appeared on an election's page, for any election, ever** — `elections/[id]/page.tsx` read `candidatesData?.data?.candidates`, but `GET /elections/:id/candidates` returns the candidate array directly at `.data` (no `.candidates` wrapper), so the property access was always `undefined`, silently falling back to an empty array. Every position showed "No candidates yet" regardless of real data — confirmed live both before the fix (4 real seeded candidates on election `#1`, UI showed zero) and after (fixed, same candidates render correctly with name, manifesto and status). One-line fix: `candidatesData?.data || []`.
    5. **Closing or reloading a poll with real responses crashed the page outright**, and poll option labels never rendered at all even before that — `polls/[id]/page.tsx` expected `results.options[].option.id`/`.percentage`/`.responseCount` and `option.text`, but the real API returns flat `optionId`/`votePercentage`/`voteCount` on results and `optionText` on options (confirmed via direct `GET .../results`). The `.find(o => o.option.id === ...)` call threw `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'id')` — a real, captured Next.js dev error overlay, not a hypothetical — crashing the entire poll page for anyone who opened it after the poll closed. Fixed both the destructuring and the `PollResults`/`PollOption` TypeScript interfaces in `lib/api/voting.ts` to match the real API shape.
  - **The single most severe functional gap found this project: there is no way to ever start voting on an election through the interface, once nominations have closed.** `voting.service.ts`'s `closeNominations()` deliberately sets the election's status back to `DRAFT` (`// Back to draft until voting starts` — a real, intentional backend design, confirmed by reading the comment), and `startVoting()` does correctly accept a `DRAFT` election. But `elections/[id]/page.tsx`'s action buttons only show **Start Voting** when `status === 'NOMINATIONS_CLOSED'` — a status the real code never produces — and a `DRAFT` election only ever shows **Edit** / **Open Nominations** / **Cancel**. Reopening nominations just returns to `NOMINATIONS_OPEN`, which also has no **Start Voting** button, for the same reason. The result is a genuine dead end: once nominations close, an officer cannot reach voting through any button in the app, for any election, in any association, ever. Confirmed live (repeatedly, not a one-off) and worked around for this session's verification only by calling `POST /elections/:id/start-voting` directly — a real, valid, working endpoint with no reachable UI trigger. Logged as **G28**, the top-priority gap in this log.
  - **No candidate-management screen exists anywhere in the app.** Zero files anywhere are named anything like "candidate". The **Add Candidate** link on an election's page points at `/elections/:id/candidates/add`, which 404s (confirmed twice: once the click hung the page for minutes, once it silently did nothing — both consistent with the same missing-route cause already seen on meetings' Edit button, G27). `selfNominate`, `approveCandidate` and `withdrawCandidate` are all real, implemented API client functions in `lib/api/voting.ts` that are **never called from any component in the entire web app** — confirmed by a whole-codebase grep. The only way real candidates exist anywhere (the seed data, and this session's documentation screenshots) is a direct, valid admin API call. Logged as **G29**.
  - **Election results can never be viewed anywhere in the app**, even though publishing them works correctly and candidates carry a real, correctly-incremented `voteCount` field server-side (confirmed: cast a live vote, confirmed `voteCount: 1` via the API). `getElectionResults` is a real, working endpoint — but it is **never called from any component**, the **View Results** link on a closed/published election points at `/elections/:id/results`, which doesn't exist (404, or in one run simply didn't navigate), and the candidate cards on the election's own page never render `voteCount` even when it's non-zero. An officer can publish results but can never see who won inside the app. Logged as **G30**.
  - **Both elections and polls have the same dead "Edit" link as meetings (G27)** — `/elections/:id/edit` and `/polls/:id/edit` are both referenced by a real button but neither route exists anywhere in `apps/web`. Logged as **G31**.
  - **"Voter eligibility and turnout" isn't a real screen** — the sitemap's `covers` note for this page pointed at `/dashboard/governance/voting`, which is actually the general Voting & Elections hub (stats, active items, quick actions), not a dedicated eligibility/turnout report. `eligibleVoterCount`, `totalVotesCast` and `quorumPercentage` are real fields tracked correctly on the `Election` model (confirmed via the API — `eligibleVoterCount` populates correctly to the real active-member count on creation) but are **never rendered anywhere in the web UI**. `voter-eligibility-and-turnout.mdx` documents the real hub page instead and notes turnout isn't a report yet. Logged as **G32**.
  - **Sitemap/help-guide correction found by reading the real RBAC seed, same pattern as D4.1:** `ELECTION_VOTE`/`ELECTION_NOMINATE`/`POLL_VIEW`/`POLL_VOTE` all require `members.view` — the same broad key G16 already found missing from the default `member` role template (only added for the demo association's own role, not the shared template). This isn't a new gap, just a reconfirmation that it also governs this module; no new `G#` logged.

Verified live, end to end, as `admin@demo.com`/`member@demo.com`: created a loan product, opened a savings account and recorded a deposit, submitted a real loan application through the full 4-step wizard, accepted a real guarantor request, reviewed/approved/disbursed two loan applications, and recorded a real repayment. `docs/cooperative/README.md` (Phase 8.5, an implementation-summary doc) was accurate on schema/API/architecture and used directly; its own permissions table was not trusted without checking the real RBAC seed (same pattern as D4.3's welfare README).
  - **By far the largest bug-fixing pass this session — nine real, confirmed bugs found and fixed, all field-name/shape mismatches between `apps/web` and the real API, the same class already seen repeatedly in D4.2–D4.3 but concentrated here:**
    1. **Finally closed out G16's long-open finding from D3.1** — the Guarantors list has crashed on any populated list since this project's first cooperatives session, because `formatLoanGuarantorResponse` never included the `loanApplication` data the frontend needs. Built a dedicated `PendingGuarantorRequestResponse` type and formatter for `getPendingGuarantorRequests` (leaving the shared formatter untouched, since two *other* real consumers — the loan application detail page and the member profile's `guaranteedLoans` — already work correctly against its existing flat shape). Confirmed live: a real pending guarantor request for a real loan now renders correctly with borrower name, loan product, amount and guarantee amount.
    2. **The Savings Accounts list crashed outright for any association with any savings account** — `savings/page.tsx` reads `account.member.firstName`, but `GET /savings-accounts` only ever returned a flat `memberName` string, no nested `member` object. A real, captured Next.js runtime error (`Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'firstName')`), not hypothetical. Fixed by adding a `member: {id, firstName, lastName, membershipId}` object to `formatSavingsAccountResponse` and the three Prisma queries that feed it.
    3. **The Loans list and the member-facing My Loans page crashed the same way** (`loan.member.firstName`) — `formatLoanResponse` had the identical gap; fixed the same way. (`my-loans/page.tsx` turned out to reuse this exact formatter via `getMemberCooperativeProfile`, so fixing it once covered both pages.)
    4. **The Applications list crashed the same way again** (`app.member.firstName`) — `formatLoanApplicationResponse`, same gap, same fix.
    5. **Three member-selection dropdowns showed "(undefined)" instead of a member ID** — `savings/page.tsx`, `apply/page.tsx` and `applications/page.tsx` all fetch the general `/api/members` endpoint directly for a "choose a member" dropdown, and all three referenced a `membershipId` field that endpoint has never returned (the real field is `memberId`). Fixed all three to read the field that's actually there.
    6. **The single most significant bug in this batch: accepting or declining a guarantor request has never actually worked, for anyone, with no visible error until this session.** `respondToGuarantorRequest()` filtered by `guarantorMember: { userId: memberId }`, but the controller passes a **Member** id, not a **User** id — the exact `userId`-vs-`guarantorMemberId` bug D3.1 already found and fixed in the sibling `getPendingGuarantorRequests` query, just never applied to this one. Every accept/decline attempt silently 400'd with `"Guarantor request not found or already processed"` — confirmed live (the first live test in this session hit exactly this, with the request still `PENDING` in the database afterward). The frontend *does* show a `toast.error` on this response, so a real member clicking **Accept Request** would have seen a confusing, wrong error message, not silence. Fixed with the same one-line correction as the original D3.1 fix; confirmed live afterward that accepting now works and correctly auto-promotes the loan application to `PENDING_REVIEW` once its guarantor quota is met.
    7. **The Loans and Applications lists also crashed on the loan product name** (`loan.loanProduct.name` / `app.loanProduct.name`) — same missing-nested-object pattern as #2–4, this time for `loanProduct`. Fixed in both formatters.
    8. **The Applications list showed "Invalid Date" on every row** — `applications/page.tsx` read `app.createdAt`, a field `LoanApplicationResponse` has never had; the real field is `appliedAt`. One-line frontend fix.
    9. **The Loans list showed "Invalid Date" on every row** — `loans/page.tsx` read `loan.disbursementDate`, which doesn't exist; the real field is `disbursedAt`. One-line frontend fix (also corrected an unused `nextRepaymentDate` → `nextPaymentDate` typo in the same interface for consistency).
  - **Three new gaps found and logged, not fixed:** G35 (dead "View Details" link on a savings account — no detail route exists, same class as G27/G31), G36 (`nextPaymentDate` stays `null` on at least one real disbursed loan, showing as "Invalid Date" even after the field-name fixes above — a genuine data-completeness gap, not a naming bug), and G37 (no officer-facing unified "member cooperative profile" screen exists — `member-cooperative-profile.mdx` documents the separate Savings/Loans/Applications lists instead, same pattern as several earlier "sitemap describes a screen that was never built" findings).
- [x] D4.5 Communication (10 pages) — 2026-10-14 — **Docs Phase D4 complete (5/5).** All 10 pages `published`: `index` (sibling `communication.mdx`), `announcements`, `in-app-notifications`, `email`, `sending-sms`, `sms-campaigns`, `sms-templates`, `sms-history`, `buying-sms-credits`, `reminder-schedules`. Verified live as `admin@demo.com`: real Playwright clicks plus direct `curl` calls against the real API (bypassing the UI to isolate frontend-shape bugs from backend behaviour, the same technique used to confirm G16 at D4.4).
  - **The most severe findings of the whole D4 phase, by far — a second, parallel SMS implementation exists, and the one the sidebar actually links to is comprehensively broken:**
    1. **Two complete, independent frontend implementations of the same SMS feature exist side by side.** `/dashboard/communication/sms/*` (Tailwind, 6 pages including a working Paystack credit-purchase flow) and `/dashboard/communications/*` (Mantine, 8 pages) both call the exact same backend `/api/sms/*` routes. **Only the second one is linked from either sidebar** (`Sidebar.tsx`/`AdminSidebar.tsx` both point at `/dashboard/communications`) — the first is entirely orphaned, unreachable dead code, confirmed by a whole-codebase grep for inbound links. Logged as **G38**.
    2. **Every single SMS write action in the reachable UI fails validation, confirmed directly against the API with matching and mismatching payloads side by side:** Quick Send sends `recipients` as `[{type, value}]` objects where the API requires plain strings (`curl` reproduction: `invalid_type, expected string, received object`); Create Campaign sends lowercase `recipientType` values (`all`, `category`, `status`, `custom`) where the API's zod enum requires `ALL_MEMBERS`/`MEMBER_CATEGORY`/`SELECTED_MEMBERS`/etc. (`curl` reproduction: `invalid_enum_value`); Create Template sends the message under `message` where the API requires `content` (`curl` reproduction: `content: Required`). Three independent, confirmed, total breakages of every write path in the module. Logged as **G39** (one entry covering the pattern; each occurrence detailed in the entry).
    3. **The Create Campaign message box is unreachable for the three most common recipient types.** Reading `campaigns/new/page.tsx`'s JSX and confirming live: the message composer only renders at all when **Custom Selection** is chosen — picking All Members / By Category / By Status replaces it with a static one-line `Alert`, so there is no message field anywhere on the page for those three options. Confirmed live: `document.querySelector('#sms-composer-textarea')` returns 1 element under Custom Selection, 0 after switching to All Members. Logged as **G40**.
    4. **A real, exploitable billing bypass: the reachable "Top Up Credits" flow grants free SMS credits with no payment taken.** The SMS dashboard's "Top Up" button routes to `/dashboard/communications/reports`, whose top-up form posts `{ amount, paymentMethod }` straight to `POST /api/sms/credits/topup` — an endpoint that unconditionally increments the association's credit balance and was never designed for self-service use (it has no Paystack call at all; the real Paystack-backed purchase endpoint is a *different* one, `POST /api/sms/credits/purchase`, reachable only from the orphaned page in finding #1). **Confirmed live by direct API call, replicating the exact request the reachable UI sends: `credits/topup` with `{"amount":1000,"paymentMethod":"paystack"}` returned `200 success` and genuinely added 1,000 free credits to the demo association's balance, with zero payment of any kind.** The test credits were deleted immediately afterward (`sms_credit_balances`/`sms_credit_transactions` rows for the demo association) so no fabricated balance was left behind. This is flagged to the owner directly, not just logged quietly — it's a financial-integrity bug, not a UI inconvenience. Logged as **G41**.
    5. **Notification preferences are entirely cosmetic.** `notifications.controller.ts`'s preferences store is a bare in-memory `Map` (`// in production, this should be a database table`), and — more importantly — **nothing that sends a notification anywhere in the codebase ever reads it** (confirmed by grep: the only two references to the preferences store are its own get/update handlers). The toggles save and confirm successfully, but have zero effect on what a user actually receives, and are wiped on every server restart. Logged as **G42**.
    6. **Payment Reminders reports fake success — no email is ever sent.** `finance.controller.ts`'s `sendPaymentReminders` has the literal comment `// Send reminders (in production, this would use the email service)` and `// For now, we'll log and record the reminder` — it only `console.log`s and writes an audit-log row, then returns a normal-looking `{ sent, failed, noEmail }` summary that the frontend renders as a "Reminders Sent ✅" success screen. An officer using this page has no way to know their reminder never reached anyone. Logged as **G43**.
    7. **The "Automatic payment reminders" page is entirely manual** — no cron job or scheduled task for payment reminders exists anywhere in `apps/api/src/jobs`, confirmed by directory listing. `reminder-schedules.mdx` is retitled "Payment reminders" to match reality (same precedent as D3.2's `the-member-profile.mdx` retitling).
    8. **A second, unused "Create Announcement" page exists**, duplicating the real, reachable `/dashboard/communication/announcements` page with a different UI library, calling the identical backend endpoint, and linked from nowhere — same unreachable-duplicate pattern as finding #1, logged as part of **G44** alongside the already-known pattern (G27/G31/G35 dead links).
  - **One trivial, genuinely one-line bug fixed directly, in the orphaned page from finding #1** (`communication/sms/campaigns/page.tsx`): its `scheduledAt` field sent the raw `<input type="datetime-local">` value without ISO conversion, the same bug class fixed 6 times earlier this session. Fixed for correctness since it was touched during investigation, even though the page itself is unreachable — it does not address G39, which lives in the reachable page instead.
  - **Decided not to fix the four core SMS bugs (G38–G41) or the two reminder/preferences bugs (G42–G43) this session** — unlike the one-line date/shape fixes made throughout D4.1–D4.4, every one of these requires either a product decision (which of the two SMS implementations should be kept — deleting six working, correctly-shaped pages, or rewriting eight broken ones to match the real API, is not a docs-session call) or real backend work (wiring a payment gateway into credit top-up, wiring preference checks into every notification sender, wiring a real email send into payment reminders). All seven are documented plainly in the published pages rather than describing features that don't work.

## Docs Phase D5 — Admin
- [x] D5.1 Accounting & accounting guide (15 pages) — 2026-10-14 — All 15 pages `published`: `index` (sibling `accounting.mdx`), `what-happens-automatically`, `accounting-dashboard`, `income-statement-at-a-glance`, `balance-sheet-at-a-glance`, `budgets`; and `accounting-guide` (sibling `accounting-guide.mdx` — not pre-generated by D1.3's skeleton script, created fresh this session), `chart-of-accounts`, `how-transactions-post`, `journal-entries`, `income-statement`, `balance-sheet`, `cash-flow`, `budgets-and-variance`, `accounting-periods`, `sync-status-and-errors`. Verified live as `admin@demo.com`/`treasurer@demo.com` via a mix of direct API calls and real Playwright screenshots against the same demo stack: initialized the chart of accounts, created/submitted/approved/rejected/posted journal entries (maker-checker confirmed working both ways), generated all three financial statements, created/approved/activated a budget and read its variance, and exercised the sync pipeline (single-record sync, duplicate-sync rejection, bulk sync, sync status). Two diagrams added (`Diagram` component): the journal entry approval lifecycle (`journal-entries.mdx`) and the auto-posting flow (`how-transactions-post.mdx`). Added a cross-link and clarifying note between `accounting/index` and the already-published `reports/financial-summary.mdx` (Accounting's statements only reflect posted journal entries; Reports reads Finance transactions directly) — the one already-published page this feature touched, per the Universal DoD's "update affected docs in the same PR" rule.
  - **One real, confirmed bug found and fixed, which blocked verifying the entire feature:** `accounting.validation.ts`'s `accountQuerySchema` had `isActive: z.string().optional().transform(val => val === 'true')` — when the query param was absent (the normal case), the transform still ran on `undefined` and produced the literal boolean `false`, which the service then used as an explicit `isActive: false` filter. Every `GET /accounting/chart-of-accounts` call returned an empty list, for every association, regardless of how many accounts existed (confirmed: initializing the standard 57-account chart succeeded, a second initialize attempt correctly said "already initialized," but the list endpoint still returned `[]` before the fix). One-line fix: only coerce when the param is actually present. Confirmed fixed live and via a real screenshot (Chart of Accounts list correctly showing 57 accounts afterward).
  - **One trivial one-line typo fixed directly:** the approval endpoint's response message built `` `Journal entry ${action}d successfully` ``, which reads correctly for "approve" but produces "Journal entry **rejectd** successfully" for "reject." Fixed to map each action to its correct past tense explicitly.
  - **Fixed a real, previously-unverified bug in the docs screenshot pipeline itself, not just this feature's content:** `scripts/screenshots/auth.setup.ts`'s login selectors (`input[name="email"]`/`input[name="password"]`) never matched the real login form, which uses `id="email"`/`id="password"` with no `name` attribute at all — confirmed by reading `apps/web/src/app/auth/login/page.tsx` directly after the selector timed out. D1.6 flagged this exact selector as "unconfirmed against the actual rendered DOM" and said whoever ran it first should expect to fix it — this was that first real run. Fixed to use `#email`/`#password`; the full screenshot capture then ran successfully end to end for the first time this project.
  - **Six new backend gaps found and logged, not fixed (G45–G50) — see Backend gaps below for full detail.** The most severe: **G45**, the Accounting Integration settings page's own banner claims transactions sync automatically, which is false (nothing anywhere auto-syncs on creation — every sync is a manual click); and **G48**, a latent double-posting defect between two independent, non-deduplicating sync code paths (reproduced live — screenshotted in `journal-entries-1.webp`, which shows two identical ₦5,000 "Monthly Dues - Adaeze Okafor" entries for the same source transaction). G48 is not reachable through normal UI clicking alone (it requires calling a dead, UI-unreachable API endpoint directly), so it's a real defect but not an active live risk today.
  - **Decided not to fix any of G45–G50 this session** — each needs either a product decision (G45's wording, or which of the two sync implementations in G48 should be the one kept) or real backend feature work (G46's missing welfare/cooperatives sync entirely, G47's unbuilt accounting-periods feature), the same bar applied to every other backend gap left open this session and in D4.
- [x] D5.2 Settings, users & roles (14 pages) — 2026-10-14 — All 14 pages `published`: `index` (sibling `settings.mdx`), `association-profile`, `financial-settings`, `payment-settings`, `notification-settings`, `members-and-access`, `managing-users`, `custom-roles`, `assigning-roles`, `audit-log`, `data-and-privacy`, `integrations`, `accounting-integration`, `help-content`. Verified live as `admin@demo.com`: read every settings page's frontend source for real field/button labels, confirmed every GET/PATCH category endpoint live, and — critically — traced every stored settings field through the **entire** API codebase (not just the settings module) to check what, if anything, actually reads it. Also live-tested the full custom-role lifecycle (create → assign to a real user via `/rbac/assign` → remove via `/rbac/remove` → delete) and role permission grouping. Two existing screenshots reused from already-published pages covering the same screens (`members/self-registration-and-approvals-1.webp`, `accounting-guide/sync-status-and-errors-1.webp`) instead of re-capturing duplicates; 8 new screenshots captured for the rest.
  - **The most significant finding of this session, in scope if not in individual severity: the entire "behavioral" half of Settings is cosmetic.** Financial Settings (grace period, late fees, partial payments, payment approval), Payment Settings (enabled channels, default method, receipt auto-generation), Notification Settings (reminder days, channels, sender name), most of Members & Access (self-signup, approval requirement, email verification requirement, default role, multiple roles, invite expiry, member ID prefix/auto-generate) and most of Data & Privacy (retention months, soft delete, anonymize inactive members, GDPR flag) all save correctly and round-trip through `GET`/`PATCH` — but **none of these fields is read anywhere else in the entire `apps/api` codebase**, confirmed by grepping each one individually outside the settings module. The real Financial Settings page even carries its own banner claiming changes "will only affect new transactions" — also false, confirmed the same way (same misleading-banner pattern as D5.1's G45). Only Association Profile's name/logo (genuinely displayed app-wide) and the RBAC roles system (fully functional, live-tested) are real among this feature's pages, alongside D5.1's already-confirmed Accounting Integration sync.
  - **The single most severe instance, flagged directly: "Allow Self-Signup" cannot actually prevent anyone from registering.** `auth.controller.ts`'s public `register()` endpoint has no check of any kind against `selfSignupEnabled` — self-registration is always open on every association's subdomain regardless of this toggle. **Require Admin Approval** and **Require Email Verification** are also never checked, though both happen to always be true in the hardcoded registration flow anyway, so that half is accidentally safe. The already-published `members/self-registration-and-approvals.mdx` (D3.2) previously described the toggle as if it worked — corrected in this session's PR, since it directly documents this exact gap.
  - **One real bug found and fixed:** removing a role from a user (`POST /rbac/remove`) correctly soft-deletes the `UserRole` row (`isActive: false`, by design, for an audit trail) — but the role list's `userCount` and the delete-role guard both counted the relation with no `isActive` filter, so a removed assignment permanently inflated the displayed count and permanently blocked deleting that role ("Cannot delete role with 1 assigned users" even after the one user's role was removed). Confirmed live: created a custom role, assigned it, removed it, attempted to delete it — failed before the fix, succeeded after. One-line fix in both `rbac.controller.ts` locations (`_count: { select: { userRoles: { where: { isActive: true } } } }`).
  - **Three new backend gaps found and logged, not fixed (G51–G53) — see Backend gaps below for full detail.** G51 (Audit Log completely non-functional for every tenant admin), G52 (the self-signup finding above), G53 (the broader cosmetic-settings pattern, one entry covering every affected field across 5 settings categories).
  - **Decided not to fix G51–G53 this session** — G51 needs a real tenant-scoped audit endpoint built from scratch; G52 and G53 need a product decision on which settings are worth wiring up for real (and in G52's case, a security-conscious fix, not a quick one) — same bar as every other backend-feature-work gap left open this project.

No prices in the docs — real pricing links out to `societyledger.com.ng/pricing`. Verified live as `admin@demo.com`: the demo association had **no subscription record at all** going into this session; started a real trial via `POST /subscriptions/initialize` (Enterprise, monthly — real 14-day `trialEndsAt`, real Paystack `authorizationUrl` and a `PENDING` `PaymentHistory` row), then downgraded it to Starter, confirming the plan changed in the subscription record immediately rather than at the stated future date. Read `subscription.controller.ts`, `subscription-jobs.ts` (trial-expiry/renewal/past-due cron jobs) and the full checkout flow (`checkout/page.tsx`) end to end.
  - **Unlike D5.1/D5.2, the underlying billing mechanics here are genuinely solid** — real Paystack/Nomba checkout with correct amounts, accurate 14-day trial dates, correct proration math for upgrades, and real scheduled trial-expiry/renewal-reminder emails (3-day and 7-day warnings) via `subscription-jobs.ts`. The problems found are all about what plan/subscription status actually *controls*, not about the payment plumbing itself.

  - **Re-confirmed and sharpened G13 (already open since D2.2) with a concrete live demonstration**: the demo association had zero subscription records of any kind, yet retained full working access to Accounting and Cooperatives — both Enterprise-exclusive modules — the entire session before this feature started. This isn't a new finding, but D5.3 is the first time it was proven end-to-end rather than inferred from reading middleware code.
  - **Decided not to fix G54–G56 this session** — G54 needs a real deferred-change mechanism (a schema change, not a one-liner), G55 needs a product decision on whether a grace period is actually wanted, and G56 needs either new product work (a real no-payment trial path) or a copy change the docs session isn't positioned to make unilaterally — same bar as every other backend-feature-work gap this project.
- [x] D5.4 AI assistant & help/support (9 pages) — 2026-10-14 — **Docs Phase D5 (Admin) complete — all 46 pages across D5.1–D5.4 published.** All 9 pages `published`: `index` (sibling `ai.mdx`), `asking-questions`, `smart-reminders`, `ai-reports`, `document-questions`, `limits-and-privacy`; and `in-app-help-center`, `support-tickets`, `contacting-support` (no index for `help-support`, matching D1.3's note that this section was never meant to have one). Verified live as `admin@demo.com`: confirmed the real OpenRouter integration, rate limits (100 requests / 500,000 tokens per day, resets daily) and graceful "AI service is not configured" handling (no `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` in this local environment, so no live AI completion was captured — the same class of limitation as G24's Paystack note, not a product defect); confirmed the real report-type enum (`monthly_summary`/`quarterly_review`/`annual_report`/`treasurer_handover`) and ticket-category enum (`ACCESS`/`PAYMENTS`/`REPORTS`/`MEMBERS`/`TECHNICAL`/`BUG`/`FEATURE_REQUEST`/`OTHER`) by deliberately triggering their validation errors first; created and closed a real support ticket end to end (create → respond → close) and a real help article, both only after fixing the permission gap below.
  - **The major finding: creating a help article/FAQ or a support ticket failed with "Permission denied" for every role, including Administrator — confirmed live, and not a demo-only quirk.** `help.manage`/`support.create`/etc. are real, well-designed permissions with a real, well-designed seed script (`help-permissions.seed.ts`, correctly wired into `seed-all.ts`) — but that script assigns role permissions only to associations that exist *at the moment it runs*, and `seed-all.ts` runs before `demo-association.seed.ts` ever creates one. This is the exact same root cause already in Known Issues for `rbac-seed.ts` — just never previously noticed for this second seed file, because D5.2's `settings/help-content.mdx` was verified by reading code and screenshotting the list view, not by testing the actual create action. Fixed locally by re-running `tsx prisma/seeds/help-permissions.seed.ts` directly (same remedy as the known `rbac-seed.ts` issue); confirmed working afterward (real article and ticket created). Known Issues updated to cover both seed files under one root cause, rather than logged as a new G-numbered gap.
  - **One more wrinkle found while fixing the above, logged as a Known Issue, not a new gap:** `RBACService` caches a user's resolved permissions in memory for 5 minutes, and nothing clears that cache when a *role's own permission set* changes (only `clearUserCache` on role assign/remove to a specific user does). The permission fix above didn't take effect until the existing cache entry expired — a genuine, if minor, staleness window worth knowing about if a role's permissions are ever edited and don't seem to take effect immediately.
  - **No code bugs fixed this feature beyond the seed re-run** — Document Q&A is honestly labelled "Coming Soon" by the app itself (not described as broken — it says so), so `document-questions.mdx` was changed from `type: task` to `type: concept` to match, same precedent as every other "no real steps exist" downgrade this project (G19, G29 among others).
  - **One minor, unfixed inconsistency noted (not logged as a G-gap — too minor and unverifiable which address is actually monitored):** the support email appears as both `support@societyledger.com.ng` (contact page, privacy page, site metadata) and `support@societyledger.com` (in-app help centre, subscription success page) — docs use the `.com.ng` version since it's the more consistent one across the marketing site.

## Remediation Phase R5 — Accounting completeness
- [x] R5.1 Welfare & Cooperatives accounting sync (G46) — 2026-10-17 — rescoped after live re-verification: real-time sync was already wired (see G46's row); the actual gap was no bulk-sync/retry route or "Sync Now" button for the 5 welfare/cooperative record types, unlike Finance. Added them, matching Finance's pattern exactly.
- [x] R5.2 Accounting periods: open/close/lock (G47) — 2026-10-17 — new `accounting-periods.service.ts`/`.controller.ts`, `/api/accounting/periods*` routes (Administrator-only), `/dashboard/accounting/periods` screen. OPEN→CLOSED (reversible)→LOCKED (permanent, no reopen) lifecycle. Manual journal entry create/update hard-blocks a CLOSED/LOCKED period date (400); the auto-sync path hits the same shared check and just fails-and-logs, never blocking the underlying business action.
- [x] R5.3 Consolidate the double-posting sync paths (G48) — 2026-10-17 — deleted the dead single-record `syncTransactionToAccounting`/`syncPaymentToAccounting` path entirely rather than cross-checking two implementations; `accountingIntegrationService`'s create-and-post path is now the only one that exists.
- [x] R5.4 Delete the dead duplicate sync controller (G49) — 2026-10-17 — `accounting-sync.controller.ts` deleted (folded into the R5.3 change, same root cause).
- [x] R5.5 Fail loudly on an unmapped category type (G50) — 2026-10-17 — `onTransactionCreated`'s account-mapping lookup now throws for a type with no mapping instead of silently defaulting to `due`.
- **R5 complete — live-verified against the local stack: period overlap rejection, close→lock (irreversible)→reopen-rejected-when-locked, manual journal entry hard-blocked into a closed/locked period date and allowed outside it, dead `/sync/transaction(/payment)/:id` routes now 404, all 5 new welfare/cooperative bulk-sync endpoints reach 100% sync rate on genuinely-unsynced demo records with zero duplicate journal entries on a repeat run. Two new, pre-existing bugs found and fixed while verifying this phase's own bulk-sync reporting (not introduced by R5, but directly blocking it) — see G89/G90.**

## Remediation Phase R7 — Unbuilt R0 decisions
R0 (2026-10-15) *decided* these three but none was ever actually built through R1–R6 — each is a real, substantial feature, not hygiene, which is why they're a separate phase rather than folded into R6.
- [x] R7.1 Module/plan gating enforcement (G13) — 2026-10-18. Built the frontend's `MODULE_DISABLED`/`FEATURE_DISABLED` locked/upgrade state (`moduleLockStore.ts` + `ModuleLockedModal.tsx`, wired into both the axios interceptor and `apiFetch`), fixed three backend default-fallback/`upgradeRequired` bugs found along the way (see Known issues), added `GET /api/modules/mine` + Sidebar module badges, and flipped `FEATURE_FLAG_MODE=enforce` in local `.env` only (production stays `shadow` — explicit decision, not an oversight). Live-verified against both an Enterprise-tier association (welfare/AI unlocked) and a Starter-tier crafted-JWT association (welfare/AI correctly 403 with `upgradeRequired: true`; core `members` module still allowed). Found and fixed in the same pass: the demo association was genuinely seeded on Starter but showcasing Enterprise-only modules — upgraded its subscription to Enterprise rather than leaving the mismatch in place (owner-approved)
- [x] R7.2 Member payment proof-upload model (G17) — 2026-10-18. Built the full two-step proof-upload + officer-approval flow per the owner's explicit spec: bank-transfer proofs require a receipt file, Cash/Card/POS/other methods require a written declaration note (who was paid, how, circumstances) instead of a file; officers approve/reject either way before the payment posts. Extended the `PaymentProof` model (nullable file fields, `declarationNote`, `paymentMethod`, `transactionIds[]` for one declaration covering several outstanding items); member-side `/dashboard/my-finances/pay` page branches between file-upload and declaration-note UI; member's own pending proofs now surface on `/dashboard/my-finances`. Found and fixed in the same pass (see G92/G93 for full detail): the only two officer-side verification pages were both broken against the real backend AND unreachable from any navigation — fixed both and linked the richer one from the Sidebar. All three proof paths (cash-declare+approve, cash-declare+reject, bank-transfer-file+approve) live-verified end to end and test data cleaned up afterward
- [x] R7.3 Tenant-level self-registration approval screen (G65) — 2026-10-18. Added `GET /api/members/approvals` and `PATCH /api/members/approvals/:userId` (both gated by `members.edit_all`, associationId forced from JWT — cross-tenant isolation verified live). Reuses `updateUserApproval()` and `sendApprovalNotificationEmail()` from `auth.service.ts`. Built `/dashboard/settings/pending-approvals` page with pending/approved/rejected tabs, search, rejection-reason modal, and pagination. Added "Pending Approvals" Sidebar link under Settings (gated by `MEMBER_EDIT_ALL`). Live-verified: GET returns real pending approval, reject returns 200, cross-tenant block returns 404
- **DoD:** each of G13/G17/G65 is either built for real and live-verified, or the owner formally revisits the R0 decision (declined/deferred for v2) — Milestone RL cannot be claimed until all three are resolved one way or the other.
