Found and documented 4 distinct, incompatible list-pagination shapes across endpoints (`{entries,pagination}`, `{items}` with none, flat notification fields, bare array) and 2 "500 instead of 404" gaps (`voting/elections/:id`, `member-ledger/my-ledger/statement`) mobile's error mapping must special-case. Confirmed `architecture.md §9.3`'s paystack-initialize "no schema change needed" claim is correct. Also inventoried every real `Notification.type` write site: only `payment_received`/`system`/`announcement` are actually auto-dispatched today; `payment_due`/`approval` are declared but unreachable except via manual admin create — flagged for F0.2 to resolve. G2 (stale `packages/shared` types) confirmed and detailed with concrete field-level mismatches (`User.id`/`Member.id` are `number` not `string`; `PaginatedResponse<T>` matches none of the 4 real shapes) — stays `open`, fix deferred to F1.3 per the gap's own scope.
- [x] F0.2 Push notifications backend — 2026-10-19 — `DeviceToken` model + migration; `modules/devices/` (register/unregister, Zod, upsert-by-token with reassignment); `services/push.service.ts` (expo-server-sdk, chunked sends, 15-min receipt job via node-cron, disables tokens on `DeviceNotRegistered`); `Notification.entityId` + `NotificationPreference.pushEnabled` added. **Real architecture deviation from architecture.md §9.2**: a `dispatchNotification`/`broadcastNotification` pair already existed (`services/notification-preferences.service.ts`, built at R3.6/G14/G42 — architecture.md predates it) — extended that real, already-shipped single-dispatch-point with push/entityId/pushType rather than building a parallel, conflicting `notification-dispatch.service.ts`. `notifications.controller.ts` and `finance.controller.ts` already called `dispatchNotification`, so they get push for free with zero changes to either file. The 2 remaining writers architecture.md named (`jobs/meeting-jobs.ts`'s `createMeetingNotifications`/`notifyAttendanceMarked`, `tenant-management.service.ts`'s `notifyTenantUsers`) refactored to add push as a **strictly additive** step — their existing in-app-row-creation behavior (gated or unconditional) is byte-for-byte unchanged; push is a new, separately-gated (`pushEnabled`, new `filterPushEligible` bulk helper) capability layered on top, specifically to avoid any risk of silently changing who receives an existing notification. `KIND_TO_PUSH_TYPE` map (+ per-call `pushType` override) deliberately decouples the push payload's `data.type` from the stored `Notification.type` — meeting-jobs' 3 call sites now pass `meeting.scheduled`/`meeting.cancelled`/`meeting.reminder` as `pushType` for correct mobile deep-linking without changing their stored `type: 'announcement'` at all. 15 unit tests (mocked `expo-server-sdk`+Prisma, no real DB/network) across `__tests__/push/push.service.test.ts` + `__tests__/notifications/notification-dispatch.test.ts`, plus a live manual smoke test (`docs/mobile/f0.2-push-smoke-test.md`) against the real local dev server and a real (fake-token) Expo API round-trip — confirmed a push failure never affects the originating request's response. **Deliberately not wired this pass** (see `docs/mobile/api-contract.md §12`): the long list of *new* dispatch call sites architecture.md's bullet names (payment proof events, bank transfer events, loan/guarantor/welfare events, election/poll events, support tickets, user approvals) — each needs its own recipient-resolution verification through controllers this session didn't check live; left for the mobile feature session that first surfaces each event. **`expo-server-sdk` pinned to `5.0.0`, not npm `latest` (7.2.0)** — 7.x/6.x are ESM-only (`"type": "module"`) and require Node ≥22.12/≥20 respectively; production's Dockerfile runs `node:20-alpine`, and ESM-only breaks Jest's default CJS transform (confirmed live — importing it crashed the 2 pre-existing multi-tenancy integration tests at load time). `5.0.0` is CJS, requires Node ≥20 (matches production exactly), and has an identical API (confirmed via its `.d.ts`) — `library-docs.md` updated. **Side effects found/fixed while verifying live**: `packages/shared` had never been built (no `dist/`) — blocked `apps/api` from even booting via `tsx`, not just tests; built it (`pnpm -F @societyledger/shared build`, no source change). See G91's updated row for a second, deeper pre-existing Jest/ESM blocker (`otplib`) found but not fixed.
- [x] F0.3 Refresh-token sessions — 2026-10-19 — `RefreshToken` model + migration (`tokenHash` sha256, `familyId` groups a login's rotation chain); `services/refresh-token.service.ts` (`issueRefreshToken`/`rotateRefreshToken`/`revokeRefreshToken`/`revokeFamily`/`revokeAllForUser`/`listActiveSessions`/`revokeOtherSessions`); `lib/auth.ts` gained `generateToken({isMobile})` (15-min expiry vs. the existing 7-day default, untouched), `generateRefreshToken`/`hashRefreshToken`/`mobileAccessTokenExpiresAt`. `auth.controller.ts`'s `login()` and `member-invites.controller.ts`'s `acceptInvite()` both branch on `X-Client: mobile` — present → access+refresh pair added to the response and the access token gets the 15-min lifetime; **absent → response is byte-for-byte unchanged, confirmed live** (web never sends this header). New `POST /auth/refresh` (rotates, re-checks user/association status on every call, reuse of an already-rotated token revokes the whole family — confirmed live: replaying an old token killed the legitimate rotated one too), `GET /auth/sessions`, `POST /auth/sessions/revoke-others`; `logout`/`change-password`/`resetPassword` all now also revoke refresh tokens (best-effort/all, respectively) — a no-op for web, which never has one. `tenant.middleware.ts` allow-list and `server.ts`'s `sessionRateLimit` updated for `/auth/refresh`. 34 unit tests (`__tests__/auth/{token-generation,refresh-token.service}.test.ts`, Prisma mocked) covering exactly the DoD's named scenarios (rotation, reuse detection, revoked user, suspended association, web token lifetime unchanged), plus a full live manual run against the real local server (login web vs. mobile, rotate, replay-after-rotation, sessions list, revoke-others, logout, refresh-after-logout) — every case behaved exactly as designed. Did not live-test the `member-invites.accept` mobile branch end-to-end (needs an unlinked member + a real invite token to set up; the code path reuses the exact same `generateToken`/`issueRefreshToken` calls already proven correct by the login tests) — flagged here rather than silently assumed.
- [x] F0.4 Mobile payment return bridge — 2026-10-19 — `modules/payments/mobile-return.routes.ts`, mounted in `server.ts` **before** `tenantMiddleware` (same block as the existing webhook/cron/blog/admin-auth "must be before tenantMiddleware" routes) rather than via the tenant-exclusion-list mechanism F0.2/F0.3 used — matches architecture.md §9.3's explicit instruction literally. Always redirects to the hard-coded `societyledger://payments/return` scheme with `reference` (or `trxref`) + `status` forwarded; a `redirect` (or any other) query param is never read, so it can't become an open redirect — 5 unit tests confirm this directly (`__tests__/payments/mobile-return.test.ts`, a tiny isolated Express app + supertest, sidesteps the G91 otplib/Jest issue entirely since this router has no risky transitive imports). Live-confirmed against the real running server. **Not run through an actual Paystack test-mode browser checkout this session** — the endpoint is a new, independent, passive redirect target that doesn't touch `paystack.controller.ts`/`initialize`/`verify` at all, so this is a narrower gap than it would be for a change to the Paystack integration itself; flagged rather than silently assumed, same as F0.3's member-invites gap above.
- [x] F0.5 Machine-readable error codes — 2026-10-19 — messages unchanged, `code` added everywhere: `SUBSCRIPTION_INACTIVE` (`subscription.middleware.ts`, all 4 messages — no subscription/expired/trial ended/no plan); `TENANT_MISMATCH` (`auth.middleware.ts`'s "Access denied for this association"); `ASSOCIATION_NOT_FOUND`/`ASSOCIATION_SUSPENDED`/`ASSOCIATION_CANCELLED` (`tenant.middleware.ts`). **RBAC turned out to be 3 distinct, separately-worded functions in `rbac-enhanced.ts`, not 1** — `requireAllPermissions`'s "Insufficient permissions" (the exact string architecture.md names) plus `requirePermission`'s "Permission denied" and `requireAnyPermission`'s "Permission denied" and `requireAssociationRole`'s "Access denied", all genuinely the same NoPermission condition mobile's error-mapping table (architecture.md §6.3) needs to catch regardless of which function rejected the request — found live when the first manual test (`requirePermission`, the most commonly used of the three) came back with no code at all despite the fix "being done." All 4 now carry `code: 'INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSIONS'`; `requireOwnership`'s distinct "own resources only" message and the platform/super-admin-only functions (mobile never reaches those, rule #15) deliberately left alone. Every code live-verified against the real running server (crafted a cross-tenant request, a suspended association via a temporary direct DB flip + restore, a permission-denied RBAC write) rather than just read from source.
- [x] F0.6 Web "Open in app" links — 2026-10-19 — `apps/web/src/utils/device.ts` (`isMobileUserAgent`/`isMobileBrowser`, client-side UA sniffing only, never security-relevant) + `components/auth/OpenInAppButton.tsx` (renders nothing until mounted, then nothing on desktop — avoids an SSR/client mismatch since there's no real UA to check server-side here), wired into `/auth/activate` (`scheme="invite"`) and `/auth/reset-password` (`scheme="reset-password"`), both passing `tenant?.subdomain` from the existing `useTenant()` context into the `societyledger://invite|reset-password?token=&tenant=` link per architecture.md §8.3 exactly. Matched the two pages' existing raw-Tailwind styling rather than a token system — neither page (nor anything else in `apps/web`) uses one; AGENTS.md's no-raw-Tailwind rule reads as scoped to the mobile app's actual NativeWind/token system, not retrofitted onto web's pre-existing, un-migrated pages. `type-check`/`lint` both clean (apps/web has no `test` script at all — pre-existing, not something this feature needed to add). **No browser automation tool was available this session** (confirmed — none of the browser-capable tools this environment sometimes exposes were present; same recurring gap D1.2/D1.4/D1.5/D1.6/D6.4 all hit) — verified instead by curling both pages directly (200, no server-render error) and by code review of the mount-then-check client logic; the actual "does the button really (dis)appear on a real mobile/desktop UA" visual check is **not done** and should happen before demo, same caveat those earlier sessions left. **Mobile Phase 0 is now complete — all 6 features (F0.1–F0.6) shipped 2026-10-19.**

Full root-cause detail in `ui-registry.md`'s "Dev tooling & known gaps (F1.4)" and `library-docs.md`'s "Testing stack" — fix before any future feature needs to RTL-test an icon-rendering component. The gallery screen itself is also unverified by rendered output: `expo export -p web` bundles it with zero errors (Metro), but the static HTML came back empty for that one route — the same css-interop/`document` issue also empties Expo Router's Node-based static prerender, which is unrelated to the real iOS/Android target (no device was available to confirm there); flagged for before demo, same pattern several earlier sessions hit with no browser available.

- [x] F1.7 API client & error handling — 2026-10-20 — `src/api/{client,errors,envelope}.ts` + `src/providers/QueryProvider.tsx` + `src/stores/{tenant,modules}.ts` (new) + `src/stores/session.ts` expanded from F1.6's minimal pull-forward (`{permissions}`) to the real shape (`user`, `status: 'idle'|'authenticated'|'unauthenticated'`, `setUser`/`setStatus`/`reset`) — `useCan`'s own public API unchanged, only its internal selector updated, same precedent as every earlier pull-forward absorption. `client.ts`: single-flight token refresh (shared in-flight promise, not per-request), retried-once-then-sign-out on refresh failure, `X-Tenant`/`X-Client`/`X-App-Version` headers, 403 `MODULE_DISABLED`/`FEATURE_DISABLED` → `stores/modules`. `errors.ts`'s `ApiError` normalizes network/timeout/HTTP failures into one shape (`status`, `code?`, `fieldErrors?`, `isNetwork`, `isTimeout`). `envelope.ts`'s `unwrap()` is the one place that reads `{success,data}`/`{success:false,...}`, including the 2xx-with-`success:false` edge case. `QueryProvider.tsx`: TanStack Query v5 + MMKV-backed persister, `onlineManager`/`focusManager` wired to NetInfo/AppState, `PERSISTED_DOMAINS` allow-list. `app/_layout.tsx` wraps the tree in `QueryProvider` and calls `loadTokenCache()` once on boot. **Major finding, corrected docs:** `msw` (the originally documented mocking tool) is **not usable in this project** — confirmed by direct evidence, not assumption: no `msw/native` export in `msw@3.x`, its whole dependency tree is ESM-only, and even after fixing imports, `msw/node`'s `setupServer` never actually intercepted axios here because axios resolves to its **browser build** under `jest-expo` (confirmed via `typeof XMLHttpRequest`/`typeof window`/`typeof fetch` checks and a failed explicit `{adapter:'http'}` override) — none of the transports `msw` can intercept are the one axios actually uses in this environment. Switched to `axios-mock-adapter` (wraps the axios instance directly, transport-agnostic) and verified it works via `client.test.ts`'s 6 tests. `msw` uninstalled; `library-docs.md` and `code-standards.md §13` corrected (this affects the documented testing convention for every future feature, not just this one). **Two more real bugs found and fixed while writing tests:** (1) `useCan`'s selector `state.user?.permissions ?? []` allocated a new array every call when `user` was null, violating `useSyncExternalStore`'s referential-stability contract → "Maximum update depth exceeded"; fixed with a module-level stable `EMPTY_PERMISSIONS` fallback. (2) `@testing-library/react-native@14.1.0`'s `renderHook()` is `async` (same breaking change as `render()`, confirmed by reading `dist/render-hook.js`) — an un-awaited call destructures `result: undefined`, read as a crash rather than a missing `await`; fixed in `useCan.test.tsx`, documented in `library-docs.md`'s "Testing stack" alongside the existing `render()` note. **Architecture-doc gap, resolved pragmatically:** `architecture.md §5`'s layer-import table doesn't list `stores/modules` as an allowed import for `api/client.ts`, but the doc's own data-flow diagram and this feature's own build-plan bullet both require the 403→store update; treated the table as incomplete rather than binding, documented in `library-docs.md`. `type-check`, `lint`, full test suite (15 suites, 94 tests), and `expo export -p web` all pass clean.
- [x] F1.8 App shell & navigation skeleton — 2026-10-20 — Root `app/index.tsx` is now the real splash/redirect (`<Redirect href="/(app)/(tabs)/home" />`), replacing F1.2's design-tokens proof screen (moved to `app/(dev)/tokens.tsx`). `app/(app)/_layout.tsx` is the one guard (architecture.md §5): redirects to `/(auth)/welcome` unless `useSessionStore`'s `status === 'authenticated'`, tested directly with mocked `Redirect`/`Stack` (3 tests) and independently reconfirmed by `expo export -p web`'s own static prerender (every `(app)/*` route came back an empty shell at build time — the guard firing with no session, not a bug). `app/(app)/(tabs)/_layout.tsx`: 5 tabs via `Tabs` from `expo-router/tabs` (not the deprecated root `expo-router` export — see library-docs.md), custom `TabBar` component (ui-rules.md §5.8), Manage tab gated by a new `lib/permissions.ts` export (`MANAGE_TAB_PERMISSIONS`) via `useCan`. 5 tab-root placeholder screens + a notifications placeholder (the header bell's target) replace real content screen-by-screen as Phase 2/3 land. `lib/linking.ts` (new, 10 tests, 100% coverage) parses `societyledger://` deep links with zod, via `expo-linking` (new dependency) — not wired into `+native-intent.tsx` yet (F2.3, once Accept Invite/Reset Password exist). **Deliberately not built, documented as scope decisions, not gaps:** 11 of the 12 architecture.md `(auth)` screens (only `welcome` exists — enough for the guard's redirect target), the `(lock)` and `onboarding` groups (unreferenced by any guard here), and any modal-presented route (no form screen exists yet to need one). **Two real environment/tooling findings, both documented:** (1) `expo-linking`'s `Linking.parse()` reads `expo-constants` even for a bare parse and crashes under Jest's default auto-mock — fixed with a targeted `expo-constants` mock (`executionEnvironment: 'bare'`); (2) Expo Router's typed-routes declaration (`.expo/types/router.d.ts`) is only regenerated by the live `expo start` dev server, never by `expo export` — caused phantom `tsc` errors on every new route's `href` until a brief `expo start` run caught it up; both findings written into `library-docs.md` for future features that add routes. `type-check`, `lint`, full suite (17 suites, 107 tests), and `expo export -p web` all pass.

## Phase 2 — Authentication & member core
- [x] F2.1 Welcome & association selection — 2026-10-20 — First features built in `src/features/*`/`src/api/endpoints/*` (code-standards.md §3/§6/§7's layering, nothing built against it before this). `app/index.tsx` is now the real splash (A1): new `features/session/useBootstrap.ts` reads `lib/secure`'s token cache, calls `GET /auth/me` (new `api/endpoints/auth.ts`) when a token exists, maps the response via new `features/session/mappers.ts` into `stores/session`, and resolves `/(auth)/welcome` or `/(app)/(tabs)/home` — deliberately the simple, network-first version (no cached-`/auth/me` instant UI, no foreground re-validation, `platformRole`/`requiresOnboarding` both just route to tabs since A12 and the onboarding wizard don't exist before Phase 8/the admin cutover); all of that is F2.4's job layered on top, not duplicated here. `app/(auth)/welcome.tsx` is now the real 3-slide carousel (A2, new `features/auth/components/WelcomeCarousel.tsx` — plain paging `ScrollView`, no new carousel dependency); "Create an association" (A13, Phase 8) is a toast, not a dead route. New `app/(auth)/association.tsx` (A3): debounced lookup (new generic `hooks/useDebouncedValue.ts`) against `GET /associations/check-subdomain` (new `features/tenant/queries.ts`/`api/endpoints/associations.ts`), client-side reserved-subdomain rejection (`features/tenant/schemas.ts`, `@societyledger/shared`'s `RESERVED_SUBDOMAINS` — the same list the server checks) before ever calling the API, recent-associations list reading `stores/tenant`'s existing `recent[]`. **Real finding:** `check-subdomain` returns `success: true` with `status: 'SUSPENDED'` rather than an HTTP error (only `CANCELLED` is a 403) — `features/tenant/components/AssociationConfirmationCard.tsx` branches on `tenant.status` itself to show the suspended message instead of Continue. New `app/(auth)/login.tsx` placeholder (real content is F2.2's) built now so A3's Continue has somewhere real to land — same reasoning F1.8 used for the `welcome` placeholder it's now replacing; confirmed via a real `expo export -p web` run that both new routes resolve cleanly (35 static routes). **Backend fix, found verifying A5 ahead of F2.3:** `POST /member-invites/self-register` (the endpoint mobile will actually use for Register, since `/auth/register` creates a bare `User` with no `Member` at all) never checked the association's `selfSignupEnabled` setting, unlike `/auth/register` — fixed in `member-invites.controller.ts` with the same `403 SELF_SIGNUP_DISABLED` gate, owner-directed and in scope despite architecture.md rule #14's mobile-backend restriction, since it closes a real pre-existing correctness/security gap rather than adding new behaviour. New dependency: `expo-image` (pre-approved in `library-docs.md`, first real use — association logos via new `lib/assets.ts`'s `resolveAssetUrl()`). `type-check`, `lint`, full suite (22 suites/124 tests), and `expo export -p web` (35 routes) all pass.

Error-code routing is a lookup table on the screen itself, covering every code in architecture.md §6.3's login table: `EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED` → new `verify-email.tsx` (A6, `OtpInput`'s first real use, 60s resend cooldown), `PENDING_APPROVAL` → new `pending.tsx` (A10), `PLATFORM_LOGIN_REQUIRED` → new `web-only.tsx` (A12, `expo-clipboard`'s first real use), `ACCOUNT_REJECTED`/`ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED`/`ASSOCIATION_SUSPENDED`/`ASSOCIATION_CANCELLED`/`ASSOCIATION_NOT_FOUND` → new `blocked.tsx` (A11, one screen/5 copy variants via a new `lib/params.ts`'s `parseParams()`), `429` → a fixed rate-limit toast, anything else → the API's own (already user-facing) message as a toast. - [x] F2.3 Register, forgot/reset password, accept invite — 2026-10-20 — `app/(auth)/register.tsx` (A5) and `forgot-password.tsx` (A7) replace F2.2's placeholders; new `reset-password.tsx` (A8) and `accept-invite.tsx` (A9) are reached only via new `app/+native-intent.tsx`, which finally wires F1.8's `lib/linking.ts` into Expo Router (deferred twice, until these screens existed). New `api/endpoints/memberInvites.ts` + `types/api/memberInvites.ts`; `features/auth/{schemas,mutations,queries}.ts` extended (`registerSchema`/`newPasswordSchema`/`forgotPasswordSchema`, `useSelfRegister`/`useForgotPassword`/`useResetPassword`/`useAcceptInvite`, new `useVerifyInvite`). A5 always uses `/member-invites/self-register` (F2.1's finding) then continues to `verify-email.tsx` (F2.2, reused as-is) — self-registered accounts are never pre-verified. **Real finding:** `/auth/reset-password`'s tenant scoping is optional server-side — the reset works with no tenant resolved at all; A8 resolves one anyway (reusing F2.1's `useCheckSubdomain`) only so Login works directly afterward. A9 derives its tenant from `/member-invites/verify`'s own server-confirmed `association`, not a client param (that endpoint needs no tenant context at all — no `tenantMiddleware` on it); its `/member-invites/accept` response is too thin to build a `SessionUser` from, so it stores the returned tokens and hands off to `/` to let F2.1's `useSessionBootstrap` re-derive the full session from `/auth/me`, instead of a third duplicate mapper. **Environment finding, resolved mid-feature:** the physical-device dev client started throwing `Cannot find native module 'ExpoImage'`/`'ExpoClipboard'` after a fresh `eas build` — traced via the actual Gradle build log (not guessed) to the EAS Build dependency cache, not a code or config issue (confirmed first: `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` succeeded with the full dependency set, both packages are correctly under `"dependencies"`, and `npx expo-modules-autolinking verify --verbose` reported everything resolved cleanly locally) — a plain `eas build --clear-cache` rebuild fixed it; `app.config.ts`'s `plugins` array was briefly (and wrongly) edited to add `'expo-image'`/`'expo-clipboard'` as a first guess, verified wrong (`npx expo config` crashed — neither package ships a config plugin) and reverted before this was found. `type-check`, `lint`, full suite (27 suites/151 tests), and `expo export -p web` (51 routes) all pass.

## Phase 4 — Cooperatives & support
> **Phase 4 backend groundwork (architect session 2026-10-21) — done, verified at unit level only, not yet live-clicked:**
> - Own-scoped permissions `cooperatives.view_own` / `apply_own` / `guarantor_own` / `savings_deposit_own` (finishes R0.2 for cooperatives); routes accept officer key OR own key; loans, applications, savings transactions, repayments and deposits are server-scoped to the caller's member (`canSeeAllCooperatives`), and single-record GETs 404 someone else's. Backfill: `scripts/grant-member-cooperatives-permissions.ts` (**run on dev; still to run on every other environment**).
> - Two money holes closed: `POST /savings-transactions` and `POST /loan-repayments` were open to anyone with `ledger.view`; now officer-only.
> - Savings deposits: `SavingsDepositRequest` (migration `20261021_add_savings_deposit_requests`); `POST/GET /cooperatives/savings-deposits`, `/:id/receipt`, `/:id/approve`, `/:id/reject`. Member uploads a bank receipt (MinIO); balance moves only on officer approval (claim-then-credit, reverts if the credit fails); member notified.
> - Savings-to-loan rule + lien: reuses existing `LoanProduct.maxLoanToSavingsRatio` (2 = up to twice savings) + new `lockSavings`; `SavingsLien` (migration `20261021_add_savings_liens`) holds principal ÷ ratio at disbursement, released pro-rata as principal is repaid; rule checked on apply, approve and disburse against *available* savings (balance − liens); held savings can't be withdrawn. Profile now returns `lockedSavings` / `availableSavings`. Logic in `modules/cooperatives/savings-lien.ts`.
> - Tests: `__tests__/cooperatives/{cooperatives-scoping,savings-deposit.service,savings-lien}.test.ts` (52 pass; whole API run: 114 pass; `multi-tenancy/{subdomain,isolation}` suites fail — they import the full server against the dev DB, untouched, cause not investigated).
> - **Web groundwork (same day) — done, type-check clean, NOT yet click-tested:** member My Savings rebuilt (receipt-upload deposit, pending/rejected list, held vs available, activity); officer `/dashboard/cooperatives/savings-deposits` approval queue; loan products now editable with the Savings rule (multiplier + hold switch); apply page shows the borrowing limit and held amount; My Loans repayment buttons officer-only. Also found + fixed: members could not pick guarantors at all (`GET /members` needs `members.view`) → new names-only `GET /cooperatives/guarantor-candidates`; officer roles (admin/treasurer/fin-sec) now also get `cooperatives.savings_deposit_own`.
> - **Loan repayments by receipt (built, owner-approved 2026-10-21):** members could self-credit a repayment (money hole, now officer-only), so repayment now mirrors savings deposits: `LoanRepaymentRequest` (migration `20261021_add_loan_repayment_requests`), permission `cooperatives.repay_own` (member + admin/treasurer/fin-sec; backfill script updated and run on dev), `POST/GET /cooperatives/loan-repayment-requests`, `/:id/receipt|approve|reject`. Capped at what's still owed (checked at submit *and* approve); approval calls `processLoanRepayment` (so the held-savings release fires), claim-then-record, reverts to pending on failure. Web: My Loans "Pay Now" (receipt upload) + "My Payments" list; officer queue `/dashboard/cooperatives/loan-repayments`. Tests: `loan-repayment-request.service.test.ts` + scoping additions (cooperatives suites: 92 pass).
> - Docs pages for these web screens (cooperatives section, D4.4) not yet updated.

Loan, savings and welfare queues have no mobile screen yet, so they are counted by the API and shown on web but left off the mobile list rather than linking nowhere (F6). **Web:** the dashboard gets the same list (`NeedsAttentionCard`), hidden when nothing is waiting. **API loopholes found and closed:** (1) the dashboard's **Overdue Payments** came from the stored status, so late charges the overdue job hadn't reached were missed — it is now decided from the due date, and a late charge is no longer also counted as pending; (2) **voided charges were counted** in Outstanding Dues, the collection rate and the pending/overdue counts — now excluded; (3) the web dashboard page called `useQuery` after an early return (the hook order changed once sign-in loaded) — moved above it and not fetched for plain members. Manage hub gained a **Payments** row with the waiting count; the `payment.proof_submitted` push opens the proof. New: `types/api/paymentProofsManage.ts`, `api/endpoints/paymentProofsManage.ts`, `features/nigerianPayments/{queries,mutations,labels}` (+ tests), `ProofRow`, `DeclaredItemsCard`, `RejectProofSheet`, `ProofReceiptCard`, and the reusable `ui/ImageViewerModal` (pinch to 5x, double-tap, pan, swipe down to close; maths in `lib/imageZoom.ts`, tested). **No new library:** images use `expo-image` + gesture-handler + reanimated; a PDF opens in the in-app browser (`expo-web-browser`). **Loopholes closed (API):** (1) a proof could be decided any number of times and in any order — approving a single-charge proof twice recorded the payment twice, two officers racing did too, and a rejected proof could be approved later; now only a PENDING proof can be decided, claimed atomically (409 `ALREADY_DECIDED`), logic in `payment-proof-decision.ts`; (2) an officer could verify their own proof — now 403 `OWN_PROOF`; (3) approving a proof with no charge and no linked transfer recorded nothing but said "approved" — now 400 `NOTHING_TO_APPLY`; (4) approval paid voided or waived charges and, on the single-charge path, the full amount even if already settled another way — now every path pays each charge's remaining balance and skips voided, waived and settled ones; (5) **any member could read any other member's receipt** through `GET /proofs/:id/download` (members hold `payments.view_own`, and the handler only checked the association) — officers get any proof, a member only their own, and others get 404; (6) nobody was notified — officers who hold `payments.approve` now hear of a new proof (`payment.proof_submitted`) and the member hears the outcome with the reason (`payment.proof_verified` / `proof_rejected`), via `services/payment-proof-notifications.ts`; (7) the detail endpoint returned declared charges as bare ids — it now returns `declaredItems` (category, due date, charged, paid, balance) and `declaredBalance`; (8) the storage key (`filepath`) was sent to clients in every response — now `hasFile` only; (9) a blank rejection reason passed validation — now trimmed and required. New endpoint `GET /payments/nigerian/proofs/:id/file-url` returns the signed link as JSON. Permissions follow the API, not the spec: list and open need `payments.view`, verify and reject need `payments.approve`. **Web parity:** the receipt preview on `/dashboard/payments/verification` was built from the old stored path and could not show receipts held in storage, and `/dashboard/payments/nigerian/verification` linked `/download` without the auth token — officers could not reliably view receipts on web. Both pages now share `components/payments/{ProofQueuePanel,ProofReviewModal}`: Waiting / Verified / Rejected tabs with paging, a Review modal with the receipt (image inline, PDF link, or the written declaration), the charges to match with the total, own-proof and nothing-to-apply warnings, a confirmation before Verify, a required reason to Reject, and the API's messages on a lost race. `HelpLink` added; `DOCS_LINKS` gained PY1/PY2 and `/dashboard/payments/verification`. **Docs:** `verifying-payment-proofs.mdx` rewritten for web and mobile (it wrongly said members can't upload proofs); `check:frontmatter` 205/0 failed, `check:links` 418/0 broken, docs build passes. **Tests:** API 252 pass (new: `payment-proof-decision`, `payment-proof-notifications`, `payment-proof-file-url`); mobile 71 suites / 339 pass; API, mobile and web typecheck clean; mobile lint 0 errors. **Not device/browser-tested** — the image viewer's gestures in particular need a real phone. **Deploy checks:** the signed receipt links are made with the API's own storage address (`MINIO_ENDPOINT`); if that is an internal address (e.g. `http://minio:9000`), phones and browsers cannot reach it — the same applies to Phase 4 receipts and ticket attachments, so confirm the production storage endpoint is publicly reachable (or sign links against a public URL). Rebuild `packages/shared`. Left for later: approving only some of a proof's charges (all-or-nothing for now — reject with a reason and have the member resubmit); search in the queue; the Payments hub gains bank transfers in F5.5. Note: `nigerian/verification` and `payments/verification` are two web routes for the same job; they now share one panel but both still exist.
- [x] F5.5 Bank transfers & bank accounts — 2026-10-24 — **Mobile:** PY4 `manage/payments/transfers/index.tsx` (Waiting / Verified / Matched / Rejected, search over reference, sender and narration, FlashList), PY5 `transfers/[id].tsx` (amount, status, "Sent by", "Received into", the member and linked charge, linked proofs; **Reject** with a required reason and **Verify transfer** while waiting, both final; edit while waiting), PY6 `transfers/form.tsx` (record or edit: amount, reference, date, receiving account, sender's bank picked from the Nigerian banks list, optional member via the new `MemberPickerField`, narration, notes), PY7 `bank-accounts/index.tsx` + `form.tsx` (list with Default / Inactive markers, add, edit, make default, remove / reactivate, a "show inactive" toggle). The Payments hub (PY1) now lists Payment proofs, Bank transfers and Bank accounts; the Manage hub badge is proofs + transfers waiting; the `bank_transfer.pending` push opens the officer's transfer (it used to open the member's payments screen). **Your requirement — each association sets the accounts members pay into:** members already read `GET /members/me/payment-info` (active accounts, default first) on web (pay page, My Loans, My Savings) and mobile (pay screen, cooperative "Pay to" card), so what an officer sets is what members see; the gaps were on the officer side and are closed (below), and every member screen now says "Your association hasn't added a bank account yet. Ask an officer for the payment details." instead of silently showing nothing. **Account rules (API, `bank-rules.ts`):** the first active account is always the default; one default at all times (making another the default moves it, un-setting it directly is ignored); only an active account can be the default; **the last active account can't be removed or deactivated** (409, "Add another account first"); removing the default promotes the oldest remaining active account; an account with transfers **or statement imports** is deactivated, never deleted (before, a statement import made the delete a 500); an account number can't clash on edit (before, a 500); accounts can be reactivated (`isActive` on PATCH). **Transfer rules:** verify/reject only land on a PENDING transfer, claimed atomically (409 `ALREADY_DECIDED` — before, a transfer could be verified, rejected, re-verified, or a reconciliation-`MATCHED` one overwritten); `status` removed from the edit schema (before, anyone who could record transfers could set VERIFIED/MATCHED through PATCH and skip `payments.approve`); edits only land on a PENDING transfer; the receiving account (must be active), member and charge must belong to this association and the charge to the named member; a reference can't repeat ignoring case and spaces; the list `status` filter is validated; linked proofs' storage keys are no longer sent (`hasFile`); officers with `payments.approve` are notified of a new transfer (not the recorder). **Verify stays confirm-only** — it records no payment; payments are recorded by verifying a member's proof or confirming a reconciliation — and every screen says so. **Web — real bugs found and fixed:** Verify / Reject on bank transfers always failed (the client sent `{ verified }`, the API wants `{ status }`); editing a bank account always failed (`PUT` vs the API's `PATCH`); "Set as Default" called a route that doesn't exist; editing a transfer used `PUT`; transfer lists read `total/page/limit` from the wrong place so paging showed no count; the admin-sidebar transfers list had Verify / Reject menu items with no handler, its detail page had a Delete that only navigated away and an Edit that said "coming soon"; the bank accounts page was gated on a payments permission the API doesn't use for accounts (now `settings.view` / `settings.edit`); Verify / Reject buttons were gated on `payments.nigerian.verify` (now `payments.approve`, what the API checks). New shared `components/payments/useTransferDecision` (explainer for Verify, required reason for Reject, the API's message when refused) used by the transfers list, the admin-sidebar list and the detail page; the transfer detail page now has a working edit and Verify / Reject; `nigerian/bank-accounts` now re-exports the one fixed page; bank accounts page explains what members see, reactivates inactive accounts, disables "Remove" for the last active account and shows what happened (deleted vs deactivated). **Docs:** `association-bank-accounts.mdx` and `recording-bank-transfers.mdx` rewritten for web + mobile (the default, removing accounts, "verifying doesn't credit the member"); checks and build pass. **Tests:** API 289 pass (new: `bank-accounts` against an in-memory fake of the accounts table, `bank-transfers`, notification); mobile 75 suites / 367 pass; API, mobile and web typecheck clean; mobile lint 0 errors. `StatusChip` gained `matched` (info). **Not device/browser-tested.** Known gaps, left on purpose: transfers have no "recorded by" column, so the recorder can still verify their own transfer (needs a migration); `confirmReconciliation` can be called more than once and would record a payment twice — flagged for F6.8; an officer-attached proof on web is attributed to that officer and must be verified by another; mobile's record form doesn't attach a proof (web does); the two web transfer routes (`payments/bank-transfers`, `payments/nigerian/transfers`) still both exist. **Follow-up (2026-10-24, owner decision):** managing bank accounts is open to the **Administrator (`settings.edit`) and to anyone with `payments.nigerian.record` (Treasurer, Financial Secretary)** — the API write routes, the web page (Add button, actions menu) and the mobile screens all use that same either-or rule; viewing is `settings.view`, `payments.nigerian.record` or `payments.view`. Before this, the Add button was visible to a Treasurer but the save failed with "permission denied", then my first F5.5 pass hid it from them entirely. On web the way in is Finance → Payment Management → **Manage Bank Accounts**. **Fix after device testing (2026-10-24):** the officer's payment proof screen showed a blank receipt, an unreadable "Zoom" pill and no Reject / Verify buttons. Causes: (1) **signed receipt links pointed at `localhost:9000`** (the API's own `MINIO_ENDPOINT`), which a phone reads as itself — so the image, the viewer and the Phase 4 receipts / ticket attachments could not load on a device. `getPresignedUrl(key, expires, requestHost)` now signs for an address the caller can reach: `MINIO_PUBLIC_ENDPOINT` when set (**required in production whenever `MINIO_ENDPOINT` is internal**, e.g. `http://minio:9000`), else — in development, with storage on localhost — the host the app reached the API on (the PC's LAN IP, so it follows network changes); all six callers pass `req.hostname`; `lib/storage.ts` `resolvePublicEndpoint` is tested. (2) The "Zoom" pill and the image viewer's title, close button, spinner and error text used `inverse`, which is **dark in dark mode**, on an always-dark backdrop — now the always-light `onPassbook` token (`Spinner` gained an `onDark` tone). (3) The Reject / Verify buttons sat in a row nested inside `StickyFooter`, which is already a row, so the inner row shrank to its content and both buttons collapsed — the wrappers now sit directly in the footer (proof and transfer screens). Restart the API after pulling this; no mobile env change is needed.
- [x] F5.5b Cash collections & lodgments (additive, between F5.5 and F5.6) — 2026-10-24 — **What it is:** officers collect cash from members at meetings and later lodge it into the bank; every step is tracked. Cash is one more option — card, bank transfer and proofs are untouched. **API (`modules/cash/`, `/api/cash`):** new tables only (`cash_collections`, `cash_receipts` with a per-association idempotency key, `cash_receipt_items`, `cash_lodgments`, `cash_lodgment_allocations`; migrations `20261024_add_cash_collections` + `…_drop_cash_lodgment_overdue_notified`); permissions `cash.collect|lodge|verify|view` seeded for Administrator, Treasurer and Financial Secretary (role mapping, `rbac-seed`, backfill `scripts/grant-officer-cash-permissions.ts`, already run on the dev DB); setting `payments.cashLodgmentDueDays` (default 3). A receipt records each item through the same rules as the other paths with method `cash` — dues/levies are a faithful copy of `recordPayment`'s checks (that logic lives in a controller), savings = `processSavingsTransaction`, welfare = `recordContribution` + `confirmContribution`, donations = `recordDonation`, loan repayments = `processLoanRepayment`. Respects `enabledChannels` (cash must be enabled). **Books:** an AsyncLocalStorage flag makes only cash-collection inflows debit a new **Cash in Hand (1114)** account (created on first use); default behaviour is unchanged (tested); a verified lodgment posts bank (1112) Dr / cash in hand Cr; a voided receipt posts the mirror entry. The ordinary donation flow never posted to the books, so cash donations post Dr cash in hand / Cr 4300 themselves. **Rules:** claim-then-act on close, verify and void; safe retry via idempotency key (a half-saved receipt keeps what was credited and says so, 409 `PARTIALLY_RECORDED`); closed collections are immutable; a lodgment must cover whole closed collections, can't exceed cash held, a shortfall needs a reason; **the collector can't verify their own lodgment** and can't void their own receipt; void only while the collection is open, never for loan repayments (no safe automatic reversal), refused if the member already spent the savings / the fund paid out; overdue reminders (daily 08:00 job, first the day after the deadline then every 3rd day) to the collector and `cash.view` holders. Notifications: member (in-app/push/email) on receipt and void, verifiers on a lodgment, collector on the decision. **Mobile:** CA1 `manage/cash/index.tsx` (hero with in hand / ready / awaiting check, overdue banner, per-officer list for `cash.view`), CA2–CA4 collections (list, open, detail with receipts, close, void sheet), CA5 `collect.tsx` (member picker → what they owe / loans / savings / welfare / donation → amounts → receipt; "Not saved — Retry"), CA6 `lodge.tsx` (pick collections, account, amount, shortfall reason, slip photo), CA7–CA8 lodgments (list, detail with slip viewer, Verify / Reject). Manage hub gained a **Cash** row; push types `cash.*` route. **Web:** `/dashboard/cash` (overview), `/collections`, `/collections/[id]` (Collect cash modal, close, void), `/lodge`, `/lodgments`, `/lodgments/[id]`; sidebar **Cash Collections** under Finance; payment settings gained "Days to lodge collected cash". **Docs:** new Cash collections section (4 pages + overview), member page "Paying cash at a meeting", mobile officer-tasks, how-members-pay, payment-settings (its "nothing reads these values" callout was wrong — corrected), release notes; frontmatter 211/0, links 438/0, docs build passes. **Tests:** API 43 new (`__tests__/cash/*`, `cash-in-hand-account`), cash+payments suites 129 pass, full API suite 342 pass (2 multi-tenancy suites hang without a live server — not from this change); mobile 78 suites / 397 pass; API, mobile, web typecheck clean; mobile and web lint 0 errors. **Not device/browser-tested, and not exercised end to end against the database** (unit tests mock Prisma; the raw `FOR UPDATE` lock was run on the dev DB). Restart the API once so the permission cache picks up the new keys. **Gaps left on purpose:** no offline collecting (online only; a later feature); cash-lodgment due days is edited on web only (mobile has no settings screen yet); no link from a meeting page to its collections; a shortfall stays as unexplained cash in hand until a manual journal clears it; loan-repayment receipts can't be voided.

Overdue is now decided from the due date everywhere officers look: the reminders list and its counts, the Overdue filter on the charges list, the finance overview count and a new `overdue` figure in the charge stats; the app also labels such a charge Overdue.

No new library. **API loopholes found and closed (all platforms):** (1) **nothing ever set a meeting to Completed**, so "recent meetings" stayed empty and old meetings sat in "upcoming" — a meeting now completes by itself once its end time passes (3 hours after start when none is set), on every read and in the hourly job, and `POST /meetings/:id/complete` closes one by hand; (2) **attendance could be written for any member id, including another association's** — every member must belong to the association (bulk checks all at once, 400 otherwise); (3) attendance on a **cancelled** meeting is refused (409); (4) **approved minutes could still be edited** — now locked (409), and **the author can no longer approve their own minutes** (403) and approval is claim-then-act so two approvals can't both land; (5) **cancelling** a cancelled or held meeting is refused with the reason (409) and is atomic; (6) a meeting's status could be **PATCHed to Cancelled**, skipping the reason and the member notification — now 400 "use Cancel"; (7) a meeting with **cash collections** can't be deleted (409); (8) an end time before the start is refused; (9) new `PUT /meetings/:id/agenda/reorder` saves a whole new agenda order in one call (the service existed but had no route). Cancelling already notified members, so that stayed as it was. **Web:** unaffected by the rules except that errors now carry clearer messages; the web already marked meetings completed or in progress through the edit call. **Docs:** taking-attendance, minutes, cancelling, scheduling, building-an-agenda and attendance-history now cover web and mobile and the new rules; `DOCS_LINKS` gained GV1–GV3, GV5 and GV6; frontmatter 211/0, links 439/0. **Tests:** API 367 pass excluding the two server-dependent multi-tenancy suites (new: meeting rules and service guards), mobile 88 suites / 458 pass (new: attendance counts, roster, forms, agenda reorder, rows, endpoints), API, mobile and web typecheck clean, mobile lint 0 errors. **Not device-tested** — the attendance screen's tap-and-rollback behaviour in particular wants a real phone in a room. **Left on purpose:** no "start meeting" (In progress) button on mobile; no apology reason entry when marking Excused; a meeting can still be postponed only by editing its date (the API has no postpone action); the agenda is built on the meeting's page, not inside the form. Causes: (1) **My Donations treated a pledge as money given** — the API sent no paid amount or balance and the app summed the pledged amount into "Total donated"; (2) the mobile pay screen only knew levies and dues, so a pledge (a pending donation charge) resolved to nothing. **API:** `GET /members/me/donations` now returns `amountPaid`, `balance`, `isPledge` and `dueDate` (rules in `donation-rules.ts`) and leaves out cancelled (voided) pledges; `GET /members/me/outstanding` no longer lists voided charges. **Loophole closed:** **Paystack and Nomba `initialize` did not check the charge belonged to the caller** — any signed-in member could start an online payment against another member's charge; now only that member can (404 otherwise, `payments/charge-ownership.ts`; voided charges refused too). **Mobile:** My Donations shows **Total donated** (paid only, including the paid part of a part-paid pledge) and **Pledged, still to pay**; a pledge row shows what is left and its due date and opens the pay screen; the pay screen now finds a pledge (online when Paystack is on, or bank transfer with a receipt) and the list re-reads when you return to it. **Web:** the same totals and a PLEDGED badge, still-to-pay amount and due date on My Donations; the pay page labels it a pledge. **Docs:** "Making a donation" now covers mobile and what to do about a pledge. **Tests:** API 397 pass excluding the two server-dependent suites (new: pledge figures, charge ownership), mobile 93 suites / 472 pass (new: donation totals, My Donations screen), typecheck clean on all three, lint 0 errors. **Not device/browser-tested.** **Left open — your decision:** a member's own "Make a donation" still records the gift as already paid with no money or receipt checked; it should probably go through the same pay-and-verify flow.

## Phase 6 — Officer advanced
- [x] F6.1 Categories, dues, levies — 2026-10-26 — **Build order for Phase 6 (agreed):** F6.1, F6.2, F6.5 (cooperatives), F6.4 (welfare), F6.3 (elections), F6.6, F6.7, F6.8 — the last two wait for the file-handling library decision. **Mobile (Manage → Finance):** FN6 `categories/index.tsx` (search, kind and active filters, each row shows default amount, how often and how many charges/campaigns use it) + `form.tsx` (add, edit, switch off); FN7 `dues.tsx` (**Generate dues**, same as the web's Due Generation: category fills the default amount, due date, note, all active members or a chosen set, summary; also takes `?categoryId=` to charge a levy type); FN8 `levies/index.tsx` (Campaigns | Levy types, stat tiles, status chips; a levy type opens Assign to members / Edit); `levies/campaign-form.tsx` (start or edit a campaign); FN9 `levies/[id].tsx` (progress bar, assigned / still owed / members paid, closed banner, every member with what they paid, remove a wrong assignment, Open their charge, ⋮ Edit and Close campaign) and `levies/assign.tsx` (amount per member, all active or chosen). Finance overview gained Generate dues, Levies and Categories rows. New `ProgressBar`, `allActiveMemberIds`, picker `excludeIds`. No new library. **Rule agreed with the owner: a levy is a debt.** Closing a campaign only stops new members; charges already raised stay owed, reminded and on the ledger until paid, waived or voided one member at a time. There is no campaign-level cancel. **API (all platforms):** (1) campaign status is forward-only — officers can start a draft and Close an active campaign (new `CLOSED` status, migration `20261026_add_levy_campaign_closed`); Completed is worked out when everyone assigned has paid; the old free `PATCH` to any status (including cancelling with debts left) is refused (409 `INVALID_CAMPAIGN_STATUS`); (2) **removing an assignment hard-deleted the charge without taking the debit off the ledger** — it now voids the charge, rebuilds the member's ledger and clears caches, and is refused once the member has paid (409 `ASSIGNMENT_HAS_PAYMENTS`); (3) assigning members is refused on a closed campaign (409 `CAMPAIGN_NOT_ACTIVE`), accepts `allActive: true`, and clears caches; (4) a deadline in the past is refused (400 `DEADLINE_IN_PAST`); (5) a category's **type is locked once a charge or campaign uses it** (409 `CATEGORY_IN_USE`); (6) **switching a category off with `PATCH isActive:false` skipped the open-charges check that Delete had** — both now count live (not voided) unpaid and part-paid charges (400 `CATEGORY_HAS_OPEN_CHARGES`); (7) category list and single read now return `usageCount`; campaign list/detail return `stillOwed`, `paidCount` and the effective status; (8) levy errors now return their real status and code instead of a blanket 500. **Web:** campaign page gets Close campaign (with a confirm that says the debt stays) and a closed banner; remove-assignment wording; the assign list now loads every active member (it stopped at 200); the Dues generator loaded only the first 100 members so Select all missed the rest — now every page; the levy type's **Assign to Members** link opened a dues page that only listed due categories, so nothing was preselected — it now preselects the levy; campaign deadline can't be in the past; category type is disabled with a reason when in use. **Left as found:** the category schema accepts grace period and late-fee fields the database has no columns for (silently dropped), so neither web nor mobile offers them; generating dues twice charges twice (documented, no duplicate guard). **Docs:** financial-categories, setting-up-dues and levy-campaigns now cover web + mobile and the debt rules; `DOCS_LINKS` gained FN6, FN7, FN9 and the web routes; frontmatter 211/0, links 450/0. **Tests:** API 420 pass excluding the two server-dependent multi-tenancy suites (new: levy rules, levy service), mobile 98 suites / 505 pass (new: labels, schemas, endpoints, rows, campaign detail), API, mobile and web typecheck clean, lint 0 errors. **Not device/browser-tested.** Restart the API and run `pnpm prisma migrate deploy`.
- [x] F6.2 Donations & penalties admin — 2026-10-26 — **Mobile (Manage → Finance):** FN10 `donations/index.tsx` (Received / Pledged-unpaid / Donations tiles; tabs Causes | Funds | Recent; a cause opens Record a donation / Edit), `record.tsx` (donor, Paid now or Pledge, amount, how paid or expected date, fund, message, anonymous; paid-now goes straight to the receipt), `allocation-form.tsx` (add or edit a fund with an optional target), `receipt.tsx` (shared as text through the share sheet, like statements; a PDF waits for the file-library decision); FN11 `penalties/index.tsx` (Charged / Still owed / Waived tiles; tabs Types | Rules | Charged | Waivers; Apply late fees now), `rule-form.tsx` (fixed or percentage fee with a cap, grace period, repeat), `late-fees.tsx` (**preview first**: who and how much, then Charge n late fees), `waive.tsx` (amount up to what is owed, reason). Causes and penalty types reuse the Categories screens; a penalty charge's menu also has Waive. Finance overview gained Donations and Penalties rows. New `PagedList`. No new library. **Agreed rules:** a waiver lowers the penalty's amount by what is waived (a full waiver marks it Waived), credits the member's ledger and keeps the reason in the history; late fees only go to real overdue dues and levies; applying them is previewed first. **Money bugs found and fixed (API, all platforms):** (1) **waiving did nothing to what the member owed** — `createFromWaiver` existed but was never called, a partial waiver only wrote a note, a full one left the ledger debit, and the same penalty could be waived again and again; now the amount drops, the ledger is credited, over-waiving / voided / already-waived / non-penalty are refused with codes (`WAIVER_TOO_LARGE`, `PENALTY_VOIDED`, `ALREADY_WAIVED`, `NOT_A_PENALTY`), and it is claim-then-act so two officers can't both waive (409 `CONFLICT`); (2) **a recorded donation never reached the member's ledger or the books** — an instant donation now credits the ledger (donations are not debts, so a pledge reaches the ledger when paid, as the ledger already intended), syncs accounting, and refreshes caches; (3) **late fees were charged on voided charges, donation pledges and levies/penalties alike** — now only dues and levies, never voided ones, unless a rule names a category on purpose; (4) donation validation: amount above ₦0, active donor, no past pledge date, plain messages when the cause or fund is missing; (5) rule fee checks also on update (a percentage above 100 was accepted); (6) donation stats count voided donations out and now return `totalReceived` and `totalPledged`; the list says which are pledges; (7) real status codes and plain messages instead of blanket 500s; (8) new `GET /penalties/late-fees/preview` (a dry run that changes nothing, including not saving the Financial Settings fallback rule). **Web:** Apply Late Fees now shows the preview and asks first; categories, rules, funds and waivers lists loaded only the first 20 (members 100) — now all; the donor list and the waive/record screens follow. **Docs:** donations-and-causes, penalties-and-late-fees and waiving-a-penalty now cover web + mobile (the waiving page wrongly said the web had no Waive button — it is on the Applied Penalties tab); - [x] F6.3 Elections & polls admin — 2026-10-28 — **Added after review (owner request): optional rules for who can vote and who can stand.** Default unchanged: with no rule every active member can vote and stand, on web and mobile, and nothing new is shown. An association opts in. **Rules are data:** per election (`elections.eligibility_rules`, copied from an optional association template in `settings` when the election is created, so a later change never touches an election that exists), for voters and candidates separately, from a menu in one place (`MEMBER_CONDITIONS` in `eligibility-rules.ts`): financial standing (no overdue charges, or at most ₦X, counting only what is still owed), minimum months a member, member categories (the association's own, plus the four the app has always had); for candidates also "must also be allowed to vote" and a limit on positions per person. Adding a condition is one entry in that table. **Exceptions** (`election_eligibility_exceptions`): the committee can allow or bar a named member for voting or standing with a reason on record, until voting closes; a member who has voted can't be barred. **Checked live** when someone votes, is nominated or is added (403 `NOT_ELIGIBLE`, with the reason in plain words); the count of who can vote is "eligible now plus anyone who already voted", used for turnout, the minimum turnout and the progress chart. **API:** `GET …/elections/:id/my-eligibility`, `GET|PUT …/elections/:id/eligibility`, `POST …/eligibility/exceptions`, `DELETE …/eligibility/exceptions/:id`, `POST …/eligibility/preview` (how many a rule would let in and who it would turn away), `GET|PUT …/eligibility/template`, `GET …/eligibility/options`; new permission `elections.rules.manage` (Administrator and Election Committee; migration `20261031`). **Web:** rules editor with preview on the new-election form and the election page, an exceptions manager, a Voting Rules page (sidebar for the Administrator and the committee), requirements and the reason shown to members, vote and nominate buttons hidden for someone the rules turn away. **Mobile:** the same on Manage → Voting → Voting rules, the election form and control screen, and the member election screens. **Not covered on purpose:** a status condition (active is always required), conditions beyond the menu (add them to the table as real cases arise), rules on polls. Migration `20261030_add_election_eligibility_rules`. Tests: API 647 (new: rules engine, service), mobile 693 (new: form mapping, cards, endpoints). **Original entry:** **Added after review (owner request): live turnout for every member.** New `GET /voting/elections/:id/progress` (any member who can view elections; from the moment voting opens) returns members voted of eligible, turnout %, the minimum-turnout line, a running-total timeline (slices of at least an hour, at most 48) and how many voted on each position — never who anyone voted for or who is ahead (owner chose turnout-only over live candidate counts). Web: `ElectionProgress` (bar with the minimum marked, area chart, per-position bars, "Live", refreshes every 10 seconds) on My Voting and the election page. Mobile: `TurnoutCard` (bar, line chart, per-position bars) on the member election screen and, compact, under each active election. Also fixed during review: web My Voting read `elections`/`polls` while the API sends `activeElections`/`activePolls` (it was empty for everyone); the dashboard pages sent anyone who wasn't a plain member to the officer dashboard (403 for the committee); re-adding a rejected or withdrawn candidate hit the unique constraint (now reopens the old row). Tests: API 87 voting tests, mobile 48. **Original entry:** **A new role, the Election Committee (agreed with the owner):** elections are run by people chosen outside the officers. Six new permissions — `elections.manage`, `elections.candidates.manage`, `elections.run`, `elections.results.view`, `elections.results.publish` and `polls.manage` — held by the new per-association system role `election_committee` (everything a member has, plus those six). **The Administrator can appoint the committee and run polls, but can no longer run elections.** Before, `ELECTION_MANAGE` and `POLL_MANAGE` were both an alias of `members.create`, so any officer with it ran elections. Migration `20261028_add_election_committee_role` (permissions, a role per association, role permissions, `polls.manage` for admins; applied locally and the RBAC seed re-run), `ROLE_PERMISSION_MAPPING`, signup's `rolesToCreate` and a test that the SQL grants exactly the mapping. **Mobile (Manage → Voting, only with the module on):** GV7 `elections/index.tsx` (voting now, elections held, usual turnout; what is waiting for the committee — nominations to decide, voting past its end, results to publish; status filters), GV8 `elections/form.tsx` (type, nomination and voting dates, secret votes, abstaining, minimum turnout, positions on create), GV9 `elections/[id].tsx` (stage line, the one next step with a confirm, turnout, dates, each position with its candidates — Approve / Reject with a reason, Add a candidate by searching members, change or remove a position — cancel with a reason, delete) and `elections/results.tsx` (turnout, minimum turnout met, bars per position, abstentions, winner or "level"), GV10 `polls/index.tsx`, `form.tsx`, `[id].tsx` (answers, live results, start / close / cancel, add or remove an answer). The Manage hub gained a **Voting** section, and Home's attention list gained election nominations, voting to close and results to publish. New: `features/electionsManage` (labels, schemas, queries, mutations, `Rows`, `Sheets`), endpoints and types; `PagedList` takes an optional scrolling `header`. No new library. **Integrity bugs fixed (API, all platforms):** (1) **closing nominations sent the election back to DRAFT**, so it looked unstarted and members could be registered again — new `NOMINATIONS_CLOSED` status (migration `20261029_add_election_nominations_closed`); (2) **no lifecycle step was claimed**, so a double tap or two committee members could open, close or publish twice — every step now moves from one status only, once (409 `WRONG_STATUS`, `ALREADY_DONE`); (3) **voting could be opened with an empty position, a nomination still waiting, or an end date already past** — refused (409 `NO_CANDIDATES`, `PENDING_CANDIDATES`, `VOTING_WINDOW_PASSED`); (4) **votes were checked then written with nothing between**, so two taps counted twice and a vote could land after the close — vote, close, publish and nomination decisions now take the election's row lock; a blank ballot (the validation let `undefined` through), a candidate from another position, and a pending or withdrawn candidate are refused; (5) **an officer could approve or reject their own nomination**, and approvals weren't claimed or limited to before voting — refused (403 `OWN_NOMINATION`), claim-then-act (409 `ALREADY_DECIDED`, `CANDIDATES_LOCKED`); a candidate can no longer withdraw once voting is open; (6) **turnout counted ballots, not people** (a member voting in three positions was three) — now counts members once, and a quorum is compared exactly (49.6% no longer meets 50%); **results below the quorum can't be published** (409 `QUORUM_NOT_MET`); (7) **results were readable by anyone with view permission as soon as voting closed, a tie "won" for whichever candidate came first, and `GET /elections/:id` and the candidate list handed every member each candidate's live vote count (and email)** — results are now committee-only until published, nobody sees a tally while voting is open, vote counts are hidden until the results are visible, a tie has no winner, abstentions are counted; (8) polls: **start, close and cancel weren't claimed; "allow multiple votes" let a member answer again; live answers were on the poll itself for every member; a poll could start with its end date past** — now claimed, one answer per member under the poll's row lock, answers hidden until the poll shows them (managers always see them), several answers follow the poll type (the dead "change their response" setting is gone from the web forms); (9) an election or poll with votes or answers can't be deleted, cancelling needs a reason, dates and quorum are validated, and rule failures return their real status and code instead of a blanket 500. **Web:** the Election Committee gets a sidebar section; every election and poll button is shown only to the people who can use it (it showed them to every member); the Add Candidate member search no longer needs the members list; Cancel asks for a reason; Nominations Closed status and a Start Voting step after it; results page shows ties, abstentions and "not published yet"; quorum fields on the election form (the API had them, the form did not); the polls list's View Results link went to a page that didn't exist. **Docs:** a new "The Election Committee" page; how-elections-work, creating-an-election, nominations-and-candidates, running-the-vote, publishing-results, voter-eligibility-and-turnout, creating-a-poll, poll-results and the governance FAQ rewritten for web + mobile (they still said there was no screen to add candidates, no Start Voting button and no results); `DOCS_LINKS` gained GV7, GV8, GV10 and the web routes; frontmatter 212/0, links 461/0. **Tests:** API 594 pass excluding the two server-dependent multi-tenancy suites (new: committee role, voting rules, voting service, election attention), mobile 118 suites / 670 pass (new: labels, schemas, endpoints, rows, election control screen, Voting hub), API, mobile and web typecheck clean, lint 0 errors on changed code. **Not device/browser-tested.** **Left on purpose:** a tie leaves the election at Voting Closed — the committee cancels and runs it again (no run-off flow); the member voting screens are unchanged; rating polls aren't offered on the phone; "number of winners" per position is fixed at one. older ones stay open to any allowed officer) and the recorder is refused (403 `OWN_RECORDING`); (6) only active members can contribute or ask for a payout, the minimum is enforced, a bulk record collapses duplicate members, cancelling a request is claim-then-act; (7) rule failures return their real status and code instead of a blanket 500. **Web:** the contribution member list now loads every active member (it stopped at 500 and included inactive ones); the approved-amount field states the limits and caps at what was asked. **Docs:** welfare-funds, recording-contributions, payout-requests and disbursing-payouts now cover web + mobile and the rules (the disburse page also stopped telling officers to type "cash" into a prompt); (3) **a loan could be paid out twice** (the approved check wasn't tied to the update) — now claimed first (409 `ALREADY_DISBURSED`); (4) **applications weren't claimed before they were decided**, so two officers could approve and reject at once — now claim-then-act (409 `ALREADY_DECIDED`); (7) a loan product's limits could be edited into nonsense and a 0% rate couldn't be saved; (8) rule failures now return their real status and code instead of a blanket 400. **Web:** the Disburse dialog can now credit a loan to the borrower's savings account (the API always supported it; the web never offered it). **Docs:** approving-savings-deposits, approving-loan-repayments, reviewing-and-disbursing-loans, deposits-and-withdrawals, repayments-and-schedules, loan-products and savings-accounts now cover web + mobile and the rules; closing or dormancy of a savings account from the app; a loan's late fees are charged by the schedule but there is no screen to waive one; the product's savings rules are checked against the product as it stands at review and payout (the safe direction). New `features/reports` (labels, drill, queries, mutations, share, `PeriodPicker`, `DrillFigure`, rows), `api/endpoints/reports.ts`. **Libraries (approved):** `expo-file-system`, `expo-sharing` (not `expo-print`). **Drill-down wired beyond reports:** Home officer tiles (members, revenue, pending, overdue), revenue bars, payment-method slices and recent transactions; member Home tiles, upcoming obligations and recent activity; Finance overview (received, billed, still owed, billed-by-kind legend, latest payments); web dashboard tiles, revenue chart, payment-method pie and recent transactions; every web report page. **API (all platforms) — bugs found by the audit and fixed:** (1) **voided and forgiven (waived) charges were counted as owed for ever** in every report, the member summary and the dashboards — now excluded everywhere (`COUNTED_CHARGE`); (2) **income was taken from when a charge was raised, not when money came in** — money received is now every payment dated in the period; (3) **penalties were shown as expenses** — they are income; the report has no spending source, so expenses read 0 and Accounting holds spending; (4) **a custom date range was silently ignored** (a named period always won) — now honoured, and refused if the end is before the start or longer than five years; (5) **periods were cut on the server's clock** — now Lagos time (UTC+1); (6) **aging buckets didn't add up**: a member's whole debt was put in the bucket of their oldest charge, and a charge not yet due was shown as "0–30 days late" — now each bucket is the sum of its own members and there is a **Not yet due** band; (7) **member balances left out suspended and inactive members who owe** and added up only the rows on the page (web) — now every non-disabled member and totals over the whole list; (8) **"overdue" followed the stored status** — now the due date, the same rule as the overdue list; (9) `groupBy` quarter/year was ignored on collection rate — now works; (10) reports were **cached up to 15 minutes**, so a figure could disagree with its list — reports are now live; (11) Home: outstanding dues counted forgiven charges, "this month" used the server's clock, the revenue trend double-counted, payments with no method were missing from the method split — all fixed and aligned with the reports; (12) the member's own "Outstanding balance" counted voided and waived charges — fixed; (13) the finance overview's billed/outstanding/overdue figures aligned the same way; (14) export responses leaked the server file path (removed), export dates are validated, and an expired download answers 410 `EXPORT_EXPIRED`. New API: `GET /reports/charges` and `GET /reports/payments` (same filters as the reports, totals over the whole list). **Money** is shown in full (₦10,000.00) on the new screens, and the web report pages and dashboard now show two decimals. **Web:** new `/dashboard/reports/charges` and `/payments` list pages; `DrillLink` on every figure of the five reports and the dashboard; the aging page gained the Not-yet-due band. **Docs:** financial-summary, member-balances, aging-analysis, collection-rate, income-by-category, finding-the-report-you-need, exporting-reports and export-history now cover web + mobile and the rules; new page "Opening a figure to see what makes it up"; `DOCS_LINKS` gained RP1–RP7 and the drill routes; frontmatter 214/0, links 476/0. **Tests:** API 750 pass (excluding the server-dependent multi-tenancy suites; new: report rules, report services, Home figures), mobile 126 suites / 734 pass (new: endpoints, labels and links, drill figure and rows), API, mobile and web typecheck clean, lint 0 errors. **Not device/browser-tested** — in particular the share sheet and file download are native and untested. **Left on purpose:** spending is not in these reports (Accounting has it); the member's own Home balance card opens through its Pay button, not a list; web export pages were not changed; the dashboard's member count opens the members list unfiltered. Restart the API; nothing to migrate.

No new library (document picker was installed). **API (all platforms):** new `statement-parser.ts` (finds the table below account details; recognises Date / Narration / Reference / Debit / Credit / Withdrawal / Deposit / Balance or one signed amount; Nigerian dates and amounts; skips balance and total lines and says why a line was skipped; fingerprints each line), `statement-file.ts` (CSV with quotes, BOM and ; or tab separators, and Excel via exceljs; PDF, .xls and renamed files refused with a reason), `statement-import.ts` (preview then import, mapping remembered per bank account), `reconcile-rules.ts` + `reconciliation-engine.ts` (ranked suggestions, propose, confirm, dispute).
