(2) **accepting an auto-match recorded ₦0.00 and marked the charge PAID** (`matchedAmount: 0`); (3) **one bank line could be matched to several charges, and several lines to one charge**, so a charge could be paid twice; (4) **importing the same file twice duplicated every line**; (5) a confirmed match marked the charge **paid** even for a part payment, was dated "now" instead of the bank's date, and wasn't checked against a charge that was cancelled or already paid; (6) the bank-transfer link was updated **without an association check**; (7) **disputing a match left its bank line stuck as matched**; (8) the officer who proposed a match could confirm it; (9) the import wasn't atomic; (10) `getReconciliations` returned no bank line, and import counters were never updated (now counted from the lines). **Web:** the four reconciliation pages were rebuilt on a correct client (they read response shapes the API never returned): import with account, preview and column dropdowns; Reconciliation with Suggested / Matches / Unmatched lines, confirm and turn down; history and unmatched-lines pages; the two older addresses redirect. **Docs:** importing-a-bank-statement, matching-and-reconciliation and reconciliation-history now cover web + mobile, how any bank's file is read, and the rules; `DOCS_LINKS` gained the statements and history routes; frontmatter 214/0, links 478/0. **Tests:** API 817 pass excluding the server-dependent multi-tenancy suites (new: statement parser, file reader, import, matching rules, reconciliation engine; the old decision test was replaced), mobile 129 suites / 749 pass (new: labels, endpoints, cards), typecheck clean on all three, lint 0 errors. The import, preview, saved mapping, duplicate refusal, suggestion, proposal and dispute were also run against the local database. **Not device/browser-tested** — in particular the phone's file picker and upload. **Left on purpose:** a bank line pays one charge only (a transfer covering two charges is matched to one and the rest by a second transfer or a recorded payment); confirmed matches can't be reversed (a wrong one is corrected by voiding the payment); PDF and old .xls statements are not read. Restart the API; `prisma migrate deploy` runs on start (applied locally).

## Phase 7 — Admin & intelligence
- [x] F7.1 Accounting (AC1–AC6) — 2026-10-24 — **Mobile (Manage → Accounting, module and `ledger.view`):** AC1 `accounting/index.tsx` (cash and bank hero with owned, owed and surplus, entries waiting for approval, records not in the books yet with **Catch up**, what could not be entered and why, the last six months, spending against the budget, shortcuts, latest entries); AC2 `accounts.tsx` + `account-form.tsx` (grouped by kind with balances, set up the standard chart, add, change, switch off, delete); AC3 `journal/index.tsx` (status filter, search); AC4 `journal/form.tsx` (header, lines with a **live balanced check**, Save draft or Send for approval) and `journal/[id].tsx` (lines, history, **Automatic** label, rejection reason, Send, Approve, Reject with a reason, **Post with the officer's password**); AC5 `statements.tsx` (Income, Balance sheet with an As-at choice, Cash flow, each shared as text through the share sheet, warnings when a sheet does not balance or cash does not reconcile); AC6 `budgets/index.tsx`, `form.tsx`, `[id].tsx` (plan, approve, put in force, income and spending against the plan kept apart); plus `periods.tsx` (add, close, reopen, lock). Manage hub row with a "n waiting" badge and a new `journalEntries` item in the attention list (also on Home). New `features/accounting` (labels, schemas, queries, mutations, `Rows`, `StatementSection`, `TrendChart`, `LinesEditor`, `PostEntrySheet`), `api/endpoints/accounting.ts`, types. No new library. **Money bugs fixed (API, all platforms):** (1) **the income statement and the dashboard ignored the period** — "this month" added up everything since the books began; it now counts what was posted within the period, and the comparison is the stretch just before; (2) **budget variance and the dashboard budget figure measured actual from the start of time** and added income to spending — actual is now inside the budget's dates, income and spending have their own totals, and a narrower range is prorated; (3) **an entry only had to balance to within a kobo, with floating point** — now exact, in whole kobo, with a difference of ₦0.01 refused; (4) **posting wasn't claimed** (two officers could post one entry twice) and **approving or rejecting could both land** — each step now moves from one status only, once (409 `ALREADY_POSTED`, `ALREADY_DECIDED`); (5) **posting never checked the period or the entry again** — an entry written before a period was closed could still be posted into it; it now re-checks the balance, the accounts and the period when sent for approval and again when posted; (6) **a draft's lines could name another association's accounts, and were replaced outside a transaction** (a failure left an entry with no lines) — accounts are checked and the change is one step; (7) **a closed period could be dodged on its last day** (a period ending at midnight left the rest of that day open) and the check used the server clock — periods now run first moment of the first day to last moment of the last day in Lagos time; closing refuses while entries in it wait (409 `ENTRIES_WAITING`); close, reopen and lock are claimed once, **and recorded** (reopening wasn't); (8) **the balance sheet dropped liabilities that were neither current nor long term**, so it could never balance — all are counted and a warning shows if it still does not; accounts switched off but still holding money now appear; (9) **cash flow** counted an expense called "Bank Charges" as a bank account, counted the first day twice (opening cash included it), and treated a move between two cash accounts as money in and out — fixed, and it reports whether it reconciles; (10) **a new Income account was created with a debit normal balance** (every type defaulted to debit) — now follows its type; a child must be the same type as its parent and can't be placed under itself or its own sub-accounts; a built-in account can't be switched off; an account with sub-accounts or a budget line can't be deleted; the standard chart is created all-or-nothing; (11) budgets: income and expense accounts only, each once, whole kobo, dates running forwards, **another officer approves** (403 `OWN_BUDGET`), activating is one step; (12) **automatic entries** are now written already posted in one step with their source (`auto_sync:Payment` …) instead of created, edited to Approved and posted separately — so one can't be left half done and the journal can tell them from a person's; (13) **the sync errors list returned raw log rows including stack traces** — now a plain reason only; (14) **the controller sent whatever message a failure carried**, which could be a database error — rule failures answer with their own status, code and plain message and anything else a plain 500; (15) the entry number retries when two entries are made at once; every step is in the audit log. **Posting now asks for the officer's password** (403 `REAUTH_FAILED` when wrong; the web Post button got a password prompt). **Tests:** API 60 new (rules, the whole entry workflow, statements, accounts, budgets, periods, attention), mobile 45 new (endpoints, labels and forms, the entry screens, dashboard, statements, budgets). **Docs:** 13 accounting pages rewritten for web + mobile — they said syncing was manual, that welfare and cooperatives could never sync, and that periods did not exist; `DOCS_LINKS` gained AC1, AC2, AC4–AC6 and the web routes. **Not device/browser-tested.** **Review follow-up (same day, `/review`):** (a) **fix (10) did not work through the API** — the create-account validation still defaulted `normalBalance` to DEBIT before the service saw it, so a new Income account was still a debit account; the default is removed and a controller test now proves it; (b) **statements now export as a PDF** (`POST /exports/accounting-statement`, a pure document builder shared with the screens, a real multi-page PDF, listed in export history) with a PDF icon on the statements screen for anyone with `exports.financial`; text sharing stays; (c) **the password check is now limited** — five wrong passwords in 15 minutes lock posting and void-and-replace for that officer (429 `REAUTH_LOCKED`), only wrong ones count, shared helper `lib/reauth.ts` on Redis with a memory fallback; (d) catch-up now needs `ledger.edit` as the spec says (the server accepts edit or create); (e) the balance sheet is only fetched while it is on screen and "today" is worked out again when it is opened; (f) an account can be taken out from under its parent; (g) a rejected-too-often password message shows on the posting sheet; (h) tests for the controller paths, the lock-out and the PDF (API 84 in the accounting folder, mobile 51); (i) `/imprint` run and the class table added to the registry; (j) the queries were run against the local database for the first time (chart with balances, all three statements, dashboard, journal list, budgets, variance, the association row lock) and all ran. **Web parity pass (owner noticed the phone lacked things the website has):** compared every web accounting page with the phone, feature by feature, and closed the gaps — account **sub-type** (it decides where an account shows on the balance sheet and how cash is sorted, so a phone-made fixed asset used to land in "other") and a chosen **normal balance** for contra accounts; the income statement's **comparison with the period before** (change in naira and percentage, coloured by whether it is good); a balance sheet **as at any date** and grouped as current and fixed assets and the kinds of liability; **itemised cash flow** (each thing in and out per activity) and the change as a percentage; a **note on every budget line** and a **year filter** on the budget list; a **Sync status** screen with the website\'s per-module breakdown and a **catch-up for each kind** of record (the website offers this for finance, welfare and cooperatives, the phone had one button); the statement PDF carries the same groups and itemised cash. Nothing on the website is now missing from the phone, apart from its charts (the phone shows the figures and the six-month trend only) and its Settings-page entry point to sync (the phone has it under Accounting). Tests: mobile 68 in the accounting folder, API 84+.

**Deliberate additions beyond the plan, kept:** the periods screen, the `journalEntries` attention count, and the stack-trace fix to `/accounting/sync/errors`. **Data to look at (not changed):** on the local database association, posted entry JE-2026-00124 (id 131, manual, 14 Oct) has a ₦20,000 debit and no credit line, so its balance sheet reads assets ₦642,500 against ₦622,500 — it predates the balance checks (most likely the old non-atomic line replacement). It needs a correcting entry or a decision from you; the app now shows the warning rather than hiding it. Entries made by the app before this change are labelled "manual" in the journal (the source was not recorded), so only new ones carry the Automatic label.  **Left on purpose:** no way to reverse or delete a posted entry (write a reversing entry); cooperatives/welfare entries are caught up by the **Catch up** button but their sync status isn't broken down per kind. Restart the API; nothing to migrate.
- [x] F7.1b Accounting drill-down (web + mobile + API) — 2026-10-25 — **Rule: every figure in Accounting opens down to the last level — figure or account → its ledger → the journal entry → the record that made it.** **API (all platforms):** new `GET /accounting/ledger` (one account, several together, or a whole section by `scope` = cash / ASSET / LIABILITY / EQUITY / INCOME / EXPENSE; optional dates, search, paging) returning opening, debits, credits, movement, closing, sub-accounts, and each posted line with the balance after it — **posted entries only, this association only, dates cut in Lagos time, opening + movement = closing** (`accounting-ledger.ts` pure rules, `accounting-ledger.service.ts`); journal entry lines open their account and a **Where this came from** card opens the record (`SourceCard`, `sourceHref`). `SummaryHero` stats can now be opened. **Web:** new `/dashboard/accounting/ledger`; Restart the API; nothing to migrate.
- [x] F7.1c Expenses — spending recorded as an expense voucher (API + mobile + web) — 2026-10-25 — **Rule agreed with the owner: spending is recorded after it is paid, with the receipt; a different officer approves it with their password; approval posts it to the books; a wrong one is voided, never deleted; budget warns, never blocks.** **API (all platforms):** new table `expense_vouchers` (migration `20261025_add_expense_vouchers`, enum `ExpenseStatus`) and module `modules/expenses` — `expense-rules.ts` (pure rules: amount, Lagos date not in the future, receipt limit, receipt sniffed from its bytes, numbering EXP-2026-00001, budget position), `expenses.service.ts`, controller, routes under `/api/expenses`: options, budget-check, list (status, search, dates, category, mine; totals), get, create, update, delete, receipt upload/remove/url, submit, withdraw, approve, reject, void. Recording needs `ledger.create`; approving, rejecting and voiding `payments.approve`. **The books are only touched on approval and on a void**: approval claims the voucher by status (two approvals at once cannot both post), posts debit category / credit paid-from as an automatic entry with the voucher as its source (the ledger's "Where this came from" card opens it), and if the books refuse (closed period) the claim is undone; a void posts the mirror entry dated today and is undone the same way. The approver cannot be the recorder (403 `OWN_EXPENSE`); approve and void need the password (the same limiter as posting a journal entry). Category must be an active expense account and paid-from an active cash or bank account of this association. A receipt above the limit is required to send (limit = new Financial setting `receiptRequiredAbove`, default ₦5,000.00; at or below it a reason is enough). Audit entries for every step. Accounting dashboard returns `pendingExpenses`; Home attention gains `expenses`. **Mobile:** Manage → Accounting → Expenses (`expenses/index` list with totals and status filter, `form` with budget note and receipt picker, `[id]` with approve / reject / void / send / take back / delete), `features/expenses`, Accounting hub row with a waiting badge, Home line, "A receipt is needed above" in Financial settings; the password sheet now carries its own words. **Web:** `/dashboard/accounting/expenses` (list, record/change modal with budget note and file receipt, one-expense view with receipt, approve with password, reject, void; opens from `?expense=`), sidebar entry, dashboard alert, setting in Financial settings. **Tests:** API 1,041 (new: rules 20, service 32, attention 2), mobile 963 (new: endpoints, form rules, words, detail/list/form screens, Home line). **Docs:** new "Recording and approving expenses"; journal, budget and financial-settings pages point to it; `DOCS_LINKS` AC7. **Not browser/device-tested; no real-database run of an approval** (the local books were not written to). **Left on purpose:** petty cash float and top-ups, reimbursements, recurring expenses, a saved payee list, exporting vouchers (all agreed as later steps); on the web the receipt is shown from a signed link, not downloaded. A voucher waiting for approval tells an officer who may not decide it why there is no button, and tells everyone when no other officer can approve it (the API returns `otherApprovers`). If file storage (MinIO) is down, attaching a receipt answers 503 `STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE` in plain words and the voucher is untouched. Restart the API; the migration is applied to the local database already.

New `features/settings` (labels, schemas, queries, mutations, `SettingsScreen`, `SaveFooter`, `LogoEditor`), `api/endpoints/settings.ts`, types. No new library. **Only settings that something reads are offered on the phone.** Left off, because nothing reads them: approval and email-verification switches (a sign-up always waits for approval), payment approval and threshold, maximum late fee, currency/decimal places/fiscal year, default payment method, manual-payment switches, SMS toggle and quiet hours. The web still shows them and the docs say so. **Security and integrity bugs fixed (API, all platforms):** (1) **any member could cancel, change or restart the association's subscription** — `/subscriptions/cancel|change-plan|initialize|start-trial|reactivate` had no permission check; they now need `settings.billing`, and `/subscriptions/current` needs `settings.view` or `settings.billing`; (2) **settings accepted any key and value, including `platform.*`** — each category now has a strict schema (unknown keys, wrong types and out-of-range values refused with 400 `INVALID_SETTINGS` in plain words; `platform` refused with 403); (3) **any of the three settings permissions could change any category** (a billing officer could switch off member sign-up) — each category now has its own write permission (integrations: `settings.integrations`; payments: edit or billing; the rest: edit); (4) **the profile PATCH could set the logo path to anything, and the next logo upload or delete then deleted that file** — the logo changes only through upload and delete, and old files are removed only inside the logo folder; (5) **a logo was accepted by the label the phone sent, and saved with the uploaded file's own extension in a folder served as-is** — the type is now read from the file's first bytes and the extension follows it; a rejected file answers 400 in plain words; (6) a stored setting could stand in for the association name or logo — the association's own fields now win; (7) values are always saved as JSON (the text "2024" stayed a number), and the change and its audit entry are written in one transaction; (8) rule failures return their real status and code. **Tests:** API settings 22 (new: schema, permissions, logo sniffing, service), mobile 28 (new: endpoints, labels and forms, hub, financial and payments screens). **Docs:** association-profile, financial-settings, payment-settings, notification-settings and members-and-access rewritten for web + mobile — they said nothing was read, which was no longer true; `DOCS_LINKS` gained ST1–ST6 and the web settings routes (ST10 deliberately not mapped). **Not device/browser-tested.** The logo upload from a phone is the main thing to try. **Web parity review (2026-10-25):** compared all seven website settings pages with the phone. Decision: keep the rule that only settings something reads are offered, and close the real gaps — an officer with only `settings.view` can now open every settings screen **read-only** (a note at the top, the form dimmed and untouchable, no Save), as the website allows, and Payment settings shows whether **text messages (SMS)** are on, because the server does read the SMS provider. Not added, because nothing reads them (checked in the API): secondary colour, timezone, currency, fiscal year, decimal places, maximum late fee, payment approval and threshold, default payment method, manual-payment switches, require approval / email verification, the association-level SMS switch, reply-to email, quiet hours, API access, and the whole Data & Privacy page. Gateway keys are deliberately not entered on a phone. Tests: mobile settings 31. Restart the API; nothing to migrate.
- [x] F7.3 Users, roles & audit (ST7, ST8, ST9) — 2026-10-24 — **Mobile (Manage → Settings, "People and records"):** ST7 `users.tsx` (search, a row per person with their role chips; tap opens `user-roles.tsx` to give or take a role, each behind a confirm); ST8 `roles/index.tsx` (roles with people count, permission count, Built in chip), `roles/form.tsx` (name and description; the role starts with no permissions), `roles/[id].tsx` (name and description for a custom role, permissions grouped by part of the app as switches, Save, Delete for a custom role nobody holds); ST9 `audit.tsx` (search, Area and When dropdowns, newest first, tap for what changed). New `features/rbac` (labels, queries, mutations, `Rows`, `PermissionEditor`), `api/endpoints/rbac.ts`, types. No new library. **A switch is shown only where it can work:** a permission the person doesn't hold can be switched off but never on, and a role they could not hold is not offered. **Deviation from the spec:** ST7 has no active toggle — the API has no endpoint for it; disabling a login stays under Members. **Security bugs fixed (API, all platforms):** (1) **anyone with `settings.edit` could edit any role, including their own, and give it any permission** (role writes were gated by `settings.edit`/`members.edit_all`, not the `roles.*` permissions) — role writes now need `roles.create`, `roles.edit` and `roles.assign`, and **a role can only carry permissions the person making the change holds** (the Administrator may also give the Election Committee set, which they hold none of by design); (2) **a platform role (no association) could be given to anyone, and was listed to every association** — roles are now this association\'s own only, and a request with no association is refused (it used to fall through to every association\'s roles); (3) **the last Administrator could be removed or swapped out**, locking the association out — refused (409 `LAST_ADMIN`), checked under a lock on the association so two changes at once can\'t both pass; (4) **the Administrator role\'s permissions could be stripped** — refused (403 `ADMIN_ROLE_LOCKED`); (5) **you could give yourself a role** — refused (403 `OWN_ROLE`); (6) a missing role name crashed with a 500, a built-in name could be reused, and an unknown permission key crashed — all are plain 400s now; (7) removing a role left the older single role field stale (still `admin`), and someone left with no role could not see their own account — the field follows what they still hold, and they go back to Member; (8) a role change and its audit entry are one transaction; (9) the users list took any page size — capped at 100; (10) **the audit log** gained date, person and area filters, a `/settings/audit-logs/filters` list, and **hides anything that looks like a secret** in the stored before/after values, with real status codes. **Tests:** API 44 new for roles/audit (rules, service, audit), mobile 23 new (endpoints, labels, giving and taking roles, permission switches, locked Administrator). **Docs:** managing-users, custom-roles, assigning-roles and audit-log rewritten for web + mobile — the audit-log page said it could not work and that the permissions gated nothing, both long untrue; `DOCS_LINKS` gained ST7, ST9 and their web routes. **Web parity pass:** compared the website's users, roles and audit-log pages with the phone and closed every gap — **permission search** (by wording or key; a search opens the groups that match), a **Turn all on/off** control per group, **role creation in one step** with its permissions (at least one, as on the website), **Joined date and platform-role badge** on each user, **Built in / Custom role** shown with a different colour, an **Action filter** on the audit log (Create, Update, Delete, Login, Settings, Role changes) and the **record number** on audit entries. Tests: mobile 29 in the rbac folder. **Not device/browser-tested.** **Left on purpose:** a role of one person\'s own cannot be edited by them to lose permissions they hold (only adding is checked); the Election Committee set is mirrored in the app to avoid offering a switch the server would refuse (the server decides); web roles page still lets an officer try to edit the Administrator role, and the server answers with the reason. Restart the API; nothing to migrate.

## Phase 8 — Association signup & onboarding
- [x] F8.1 Create association (A13) — 2026-11-03 — **Mobile:** `(auth)/create-association.tsx` (two steps: name, kind of association with **Other** + own words, address suggested from the name with live availability and a preview; then first and last name, email, phone, password, terms) → sets the new association as the current tenant → verify email → waiting for approval; welcome screen button now opens it. **API:** `POST /associations/signup` takes the founder's name and phone (creates their `Member`), the kind of association (stored in the existing association settings, one list for signup, web and mobile Settings in `packages/shared/src/association-signup.ts`), always starts on the **Enterprise** plan, and honours the platform switches (sign-ups open, email verification, admin approval, trial days) that were saved but ignored. **Free trial:** starts when the founding admin is approved (`startTrialIfPending`), 14 days by default (admin console setting); reminders at 3 and 1 day; **after it ends the association is blocked** (new gate in `authMiddleware`, `403 SUBSCRIPTION_INACTIVE`; login, `/auth/me`, billing, support and help stay open; billing overrides respected; platform switch to turn blocking off). `subscriptionAccess` is returned by login and `/auth/me`; web and mobile show a countdown banner (3 days or fewer) and a blocked screen (officers: renew on the web; members: contact the admin; no prices or pay button on mobile). **Admin console:** Approvals can mark an email verified (reason, audit log) or resend the code, shows the association kind and founder, search and a Not verified filter now work; Settings gains the blocking switch and the trial length is validated. **Web:** signup form rewritten (new fields, naming reminder, kind with Other, live address check). **Bugs fixed across platforms:** the trial/subscription block never ran anywhere; the platform signup/trial settings were ignored; the approval email linked to the wrong domain (`.com`); the trial warning email said "N-day trial ends in N days" and fired daily; the approvals search box did nothing; the web wizard saved nothing (profile, team, finance) and counted skipped steps as done; wizard finance used `annually` where the API accepts `annual`; association kind had two separate lists. Tests: API 1160+, mobile 1037.

## Phase 9 — Hardening & release
- **Professional access messages (2026-10-27):** a refusal for role or plan reasons now reads the same on mobile and web ("You don't have access to this", "Meetings isn't part of your association's plan", "Your association's subscription has ended", and so on) instead of "We couldn't load this" or the server's raw sentence. Shared wording and classification in `packages/shared/src/access-errors.ts`; mobile `ApiError` + `ErrorState`/`AccessState`; web `toast.apiError`, `ErrorDisplay`, module modal; the API's module refusals now also carry `code`. Details in `context/ui-registry.md`.
- **Donations, member ledgers and books (2026-10-27):** a donation is now a charge plus a payment on the member ledger (instant = nets to ₦0, unpaid pledge = owed), and posts Dr Donation Pledges Receivable (new account 1123) / Cr Donation Income, then Dr cash or bank / Cr 1123. Fixed: credit-only donations, double and triple posting, pledges booked as cash, online payments (Paystack, Nomba) skipping the ledger, deleted or voided charges left in ledger and books, the member's own donation action, receipt-number collisions between associations, concurrent ledger writes, wrong entry signs and captions on web and mobile. `scripts/repair-ledgers-and-books.ts` repairs every association on each start (idempotent, never edits posted entries, corrections dated today); `pnpm --filter api repair:books -- --dry-run` previews it. Details and proof: `docs/deployment/DONATIONS_LEDGER_REPAIR.md`. Tests: new rule tests, updated service tests, and a real-database test (`LEDGER_TEST_DATABASE_URL`).
- **Brand rollout (2026-10-25, part of F9.5):** the `SocietyLedger-brand/` kit now replaces the Expo template icons and the old web icons everywhere. **Mobile:** `icon.png` (RGB, no alpha, valid for iOS), Android adaptive foreground/background/monochrome (brand blue `#0B6BFF`), splash (light = white, dark = slate, `dark` block in the `expo-splash-screen` plugin), Android notification icon (white mark, blue tint, `expo-notifications` plugin), web favicon; in-app mark on the startup screen, `AuthBrandMark`, welcome carousel tile and About. **Web:** favicons (ico, svg, 16/32/96), apple-touch-icon, PWA icons incl. maskable, manifest and service worker (cache bumped to v2), offline page, Open Graph/Twitter image (`og-image.png`, 1200x630), JSON-LD logo, `components/brand/BrandLogo.tsx` (`BrandMark`, `BrandLogo`) used in Header, Footer, testimonials, 404 and the three sidebars' no-logo fallback. **Admin:** favicons, header and login lockups, middleware allows icons while signed out. **Docs:** favicons, OG image, nav logo. Needs a new native build to show on devices; iOS not device-verified; no iOS dark/tinted icon variants supplied. Palette (teal tokens) is unchanged: only logos and icons moved to the brand kit.
- **F9.1 so far:** list audit passed (FlashList on every large list); Android bundle 8.8 MB Hermes; cold start and memory not yet measured (need a release build on the Redmi). Seed for E2E and store review: `pnpm --filter api seed:oko` (`oko-youth-forum`, password from `OKO_DEMO_PASSWORD`), not yet run.

- [ ] F9.3 E2E test suite — 7 Maestro flows written in `apps/mobile/e2e/` (not yet run on a device)

# Track T — Thrift (Ajo) add-on (`apps/api`, `apps/web`, `apps/mobile`, `apps/admin`, `apps/docs`) — planned 2026-10-27

> Full specification: `thrift-ajo-spec.md`. Build order and Definitions of Done: `build-plan.md` Track T. Data model and rules: `architecture.md §18`. Screens, roles and docs pages: `project-overview.md §12`. **Nothing in Track T is built yet.** Scope: one money-flow mode (the group's own bank account), no government-ID verification, no custody of contribution money.

## Phase T0 — Decisions — complete
- [x] T0.1 Commercials — 2026-10-27 — tiers Starter ₦3,000.00 / Growth ₦8,000.00 / Business ₦20,000.00 / Enterprise from ₦40,000.00 per month (annual = 10 × monthly), 14-day trial; levy = 0.5% cycle (₦500–₦5,000) + 0.25% per participant (₦50–₦1,000), billed on the add-on invoice, Starter exempt
- [x] T0.2 Remaining product rules — 2026-10-27 — payout order hybrid, trust score on-time percentage with bands, two-step verification (no payout before SocietyLedger verifies)

## Phase T1 — Foundation
- [x] T1.1 Ajo data model & migration — 2026-10-27 — 19 `ajo_*` tables + `associations.kind` + `addons.family` (migration `20261107_ajo_foundation`, additive only; applied to the dev DB, not yet to a production copy). Money columns are `BigInt` kobo.
- [x] T1.2 Module, features & add-on catalogue (four tiers, `ajoContributors` limit, read-only on expiry — F4) — 2026-10-27 — module `ajo` + 5 features; add-ons `ajo-starter/growth/business/enterprise` in family `ajo` (`prisma/seeds/ajo-addons.seed.ts`, created once); `ajoContributors` is an add-on-only quota (0 without the add-on); `modules/ajo/ajo-access.ts` (`MODULE_DISABLED` / `ADDON_EXPIRED`) and `ajo-capacity.ts` (distinct people, per-tenant lock, `AJO_CONTRIBUTOR_LIMIT`); tier swap in quoting and lifecycle; admin Plan Builder editor fixed (see Decisions log). Mounted `GET /api/ajo/status`.
- [x] T1.3 Standalone thrift-group tenant kind — 2026-10-27 — `POST /api/associations/thrift-signup` (closed unless `ajoStandaloneSignup` is on), `Plan.audience` + base plan `thrift-group`, entitlements limit a `THRIFT_GROUP` to core + Ajo (`THRIFT_GROUP_MODULES`), 14-day trial includes a complimentary Ajo Starter add-on. No web sign-up page yet (T9).
- [x] T1.4 RBAC — permissions, Collector and Officer role templates — 2026-10-27 — 7 `ajo.*` permissions; roles `ajo_collector`, `ajo_officer` (neither approves payouts; only `admin` holds `ajo.approve_payout`); members get `ajo.view_own` + `ajo.contribute`. Seeded by `rbac-seed.ts` on deploy. The generated permissions reference page was not regenerated (`gen:reference` already fails on an unrelated spread).
- [x] T1.5 Append-only ledger & idempotency primitives — 2026-10-27 — `modules/ajo/ajo-ledger.service.ts` (record, reverse, derived balance); replay and reverse-then-reapply property tests.
- [x] T1.6 Settings (levy parameters, standalone sign-up switch) — 2026-10-27 — `PlatformSettings` gains `ajoStandaloneSignup` (default **off**) and the six levy values + exempt add-on codes; validated against the owner's ceilings (₦5,000.00 / ₦1,000.00); audited by the existing settings audit. No admin settings screen yet (T9.3).

## Phase T2 — Member approval, SocietyLedger verification & payout account
- [x] T2.1 Member approval & optional phone confirmation — 2026-10-27 — `modules/ajo/ajo-membership.service.ts` (`addMember`, `approveMembership`, `tryActivate`, `unmetRequirements`, `assertCanContribute`) and `ajo-phone-code.service.ts`. Routes: `POST /api/ajo/memberships/:id/approve`, `/phone-code`, `/phone-code/confirm`. Approval is idempotent, nobody approves their own membership, and the T1 contributor limit is checked inside the activating transaction (a membership held back by the limit completes when approved again after the plan grows). No public add-member route yet (T3).
- [x] T2.2 Payout account record & change controls — 2026-10-27 — `ajo-payout-account.service.ts`, `ajo-secrets.ts`. Encrypted at rest (AES-256-GCM, bound to the member, key derived from the platform key), 10-digit numbers, masked everywhere, full number only from an audited `reveal`. A change (or first entry) starts the cool-off (`ajoPayoutCooloffHours`, default 72), voids approved-unpaid payouts, resets verification and notifies the person and group admins in one transaction; the same details sent again change nothing. Routes: `GET/PUT /api/ajo/members/:memberId/payout-account` (`me` or an id; officers with `ajo.manage` for others), `POST …/reveal`.
- [x] T2.3 SocietyLedger verification (second step; no payout before verified) — 2026-10-27 — `ajo-verification.service.ts`, `ajo-admin.routes.ts`. Person-level, latest row wins, decided rows never edited. Staff: `GET /api/admin/ajo/verifications[?status&associationId]`, `GET …/:id`, `POST …/:id/decision` (SUPER_ADMIN and COMPLIANCE_ADMIN decide; SUPPORT_AGENT views). `assertPayoutEligible` is the guard T7 must call (`PAYOUT_ACCOUNT_MISSING`, `PERSON_NOT_VERIFIED`, `PAYOUT_COOL_OFF`). Member side: `GET /api/ajo/members/:memberId/verification`, `POST …/verification/resubmit`. No admin console screen yet (T9).

## Phase T3 — Groups, rules & schedules
- [x] T3.1 Group lifecycle — 2026-10-28 — `modules/ajo/ajo-group.service.ts`. DRAFT → OPEN → ACTIVE → COMPLETED/CANCELLED. Creating a group also writes rules version 1. Opening needs the group's bank account; starting is an explicit admin action that freezes the money fields (`GROUP_LOCKED`), fixes the rounds and generates every schedule in one transaction, behind a row lock so two admins cannot both start it. Cancelling needs a reason and is refused while confirmed money is on the books (`GROUP_HAS_MONEY`). The group's own bank account is encrypted at rest (bound to the group), shown in full only by `GET /groups/:id/bank-account` to its members and officers, and masked everywhere else; a change tells every member. Routes: `/api/ajo/groups` (list, create, detail, edit, open, start, cancel, bank-account).
- [x] T3.2 Rule versions & acceptance — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-rules.service.ts`, `ajo-consent.service.ts`. Rules are versioned and never edited. A new version takes effect at least 7 days after it is published and every member is told; one change at a time. A member is bound by the latest version **they** accepted, so a fee cannot apply to someone who never agreed to it, and not accepting never stops anyone contributing. A newcomer accepts the version in effect when they join. Acceptance comes from the member (`channel: app`) or from an officer on their behalf (`channel: officer`, with a required note), and both count; the channel is always visible. `ruleVersionInForce(membershipId)` is what T6 and T8 must call.
- [x] T3.3 Memberships & joining checks — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-membership.service.ts`. `unmetRequirements` now lists approval, phone confirmation, rule acceptance and guarantor consent, loaded in one place (`loadRequirementFacts`). A seat (`GROUP_FULL`) and a plan contributor slot (`AJO_CONTRIBUTOR_LIMIT`) are separate checks with separate codes. Who can still join: nobody in a DRAFT; anyone in an OPEN group; late joiners in a started collector-thrift group (switchable off per group, `LATE_JOIN_CLOSED`); nobody in a started rotating pot (`ROSTER_FROZEN`). Leaving removes only instalments still ahead that have no money against them. A person who left can be added again.
- [x] T3.4 Schedule engine (collector thrift + rotating pot) — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-schedule.ts` (pure) and `ajo-schedule.service.ts`. Daily, weekly and monthly (anchored on the start date's day and clamped: 31 Jan → 28 Feb → 31 Mar). A rotating-pot member with N shares pays N times the contribution in every round. Regeneration only adds missing instalments and never deletes, moves or re-prices one that has money or a contribution against it; a late joiner is never back-filled. `businessDay()` gives the date a payment counts for from the group's timezone and cutoff (T4 uses it).
- [x] T3.5 Collector assignments — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-collector.service.ts`. A collector is a user holding `ajo.collect`; a contributor has one current collector. Reassigning ends the old assignment the day before and adds a new one, so history is kept. `GET /api/ajo/collector/contributors` returns only the signed-in collector's own contributors, and a collector can open the schedule only of someone assigned to them.
- [x] T3.6 Guarantor agreements — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-consent.service.ts`. Officer-recorded only (a guarantor has no login). The wording is built on the server and stored exactly as shown; it says it is a recorded consent, not a legal guarantee. A required guarantor with no recorded consent keeps the member pending. An earlier agreement stays on file when one is replaced.
- [x] T3.7 Payout order: draw, swaps, emergency exception (hybrid) — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-draw.ts` (pure), `ajo-order.service.ts`. **Shares:** a rotating-pot participant can hold more than one (default limit 2) and each share is a numbered payout slot (`AjoPayoutSlot`). The draw is an admin action: a server-made 128-bit seed and a deterministic, unbiased shuffle, so anyone can reproduce it from the saved seed. It cannot be re-rolled unless the roster or the shares changed. Starting needs a current draw. Swaps are proposed by a participant or an officer and decided by an admin, only before any payout is paid; the admin cannot hold a slot in the swap. After the first paid payout only an admin's emergency exception with a written reason is allowed, and it moves unpaid slots only. Every step is an `AjoPayoutOrderEvent`. The optional "At risk" early-position rule is stored and enforced by the draw, but is inert until trust scores exist (T8).

## Phase T4 — Contributions & collections
- [x] T4.1 Contribution recording & statuses (reported → cash received → confirmed → reversed) — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-contribution.service.ts`, `ajo-allocation.service.ts`, `ajo-collections.ts` (pure). Only **confirmed** money counts: it alone writes a ledger entry and moves an instalment's `paidKobo`. One payment is one contribution row; at confirmation it pays the person's oldest unpaid instalment first (arrears before anything newer) and the split is stored in `AjoContributionAllocation`, so a reversal undoes exactly what that payment paid. A payment bigger than everything still owed is refused (`EXCEEDS_OWED`), at recording and again at confirmation. Every record needs an `Idempotency-Key` header (kept per association); repeating a request returns the original. Corrections are reversal rows with a reason of 10+ characters, once per payment; a report that was never confirmed is **rejected** (new `REJECTED` status), not reversed. Two payments for one person are never confirmed at the same moment (per-membership lock).
- [x] T4.2 Collector cash flow & handover — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-handover.service.ts`. A collector records cash only for people assigned to them (an officer for anyone); cash after the group's cutoff counts for the next business day. The end-of-day summary shows expected (today's instalments for their people), target, collected, in hand, handed over, difference, and arrears kept separate. One handover per collector, group and business day, with the figures frozen; once it exists the day is closed to more cash. An officer reconciles **line by line**: the counted amount must equal the ticked lines (`COUNT_MISMATCH`); ticked lines become confirmed, unticked ones stay "cash received", flagged missing from the handover with a required note, and are carried into the collector's next handover or rejected with a reason. The whole reconcile is atomic. Cash is never confirmed before this.
- [x] T4.3 Bank-transfer contributions — 2026-10-28 — A contributor (or an officer for them) reports a transfer with a proof (JPEG, PNG, WebP or PDF, up to 5 MB, stored through the existing storage helper with a hash of the file) or a bank reference. The same file or bank reference used twice is flagged when reported and checked again when confirming: if the other payment is confirmed and not reversed, confirmation is refused (`DUPLICATE_PROOF`); if it is still waiting, the officer needs an acknowledgement and a note. The officer who recorded a payment, or the person who made it, cannot confirm it, except a sole reconciler who says so with a note (`selfConfirmed`, audited). Amounts are never edited: a wrong report is rejected with a reason. The proof is shown only as a short-lived link; the storage key never leaves the API.
- [x] T4.4 Receipts & notifications — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-receipt.service.ts`. `ajo_receipts` has a unique key on (contribution, moment), written in the same transaction as the change, so there is exactly one receipt per moment (recorded, confirmed, rejected, missing from handover, reversed). Someone with a login gets an in-app notice, push and email per their preferences; someone with no login is texted when a payment is recorded and when something goes wrong, not for a routine confirmation. When SMS credits run out the receipt is queued (visible on the payment), the officers are told once, and queued receipts go out through "send queued receipts", after a credit top-up, or when another text succeeds. A receipt problem never undoes or blocks the payment. Receipts use plain words with money in full and the payment's own reference.

## Phase T5 — Payouts
- [x] T5.1 Payout calculation & preview — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-payout-calc.ts` (pure) and `previewPayout` in `ajo-payout.service.ts`. Gross is what the round (rotating pot) or the cycle (collector thrift) was scheduled to bring in; shortfall is the part not yet confirmed, shown with the names of who is short; net is the confirmed money less the collector fee. A payout can be prepared with what is confirmed (the admin decides at approval); its figures are frozen when prepared; late money becomes a **supplementary payout** (the fee is worked out on the cumulative confirmed total and taken once, never more than the money there is). The recipient's own arrears are shown, not enforced. Rotating pot: round N is preparable once its date has come and round N-1 has been paid (out-of-order only through T3's emergency exception). Collector thrift: after the last due date, one at a time or "prepare all who are ready", with the others listed and why.
- [x] T5.2 Approval workflow — 2026-10-28 — prepare (`ajo.manage`) → approve (`ajo.approve_payout`, never the preparer or the recipient) → record paid (`ajo.reconcile`, never the recipient). A sole approver may approve their own preparation or payout only with an explicit confirmation and a note; it is flagged (`selfApprovalConfirmed`) and every officer is told. `assertPayoutEligible` runs when preparing, approving and recording; a changed payout account cancels the payout; money reversed after preparing stops it (`PAYOUT_NOT_COVERED`). A slot with a prepared, approved or paid payout cannot be swapped or moved (T3's order service now uses `lockedPositions`).
- [x] T5.3 Recording payouts & failures — 2026-10-28 — SocietyLedger never sends money. An officer records the bank's reference (duplicates refused, repeats idempotent) and an optional proof; only recording writes the ledger (a PAYOUT debit and, if any, a FEE debit). Cancelled (preparer for their own, an admin for any open one) or failed (`failed`, by whoever finds out) payouts write nothing, return the round to pending and tell the admins. A recorded payout is undone only by an admin **reversal** with a reason (ledger reversal entries, status REVERSED). `completeGroup` closes a group after its cycle once nothing is waiting or still payable.
- [x] T5.4 Platform levy accounting — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-levy.ts` (pure), `ajo-levy.service.ts`. Assessed when a group starts from the schedule actually generated (cycle levy plus a levy per participant; a late joiner's own levy when they become active), with the rates frozen on each immutable record and the calculation in words; the exempt tier (Starter) gets none. The spec's worked examples (₦3,750.00 and ₦5,100.00) are tests. Own table, never in the group ledger, never deducted from a payout. **Billing (A and B):** staff list/report/waive/void it (`/api/admin/ajo-levy`), apart from subscription payments; and it rides on any payment-taking plan or add-on checkout as separate `LEVY` quote lines (itemised per group), settled in the same transaction that applies the payment, outside the plan and add-on shares. Accrued → billed (on a checkout) → paid; an abandoned checkout's levy is taken again by the next; a levy paid twice, or waived after the checkout began, becomes a credit against the next. Unpaid levy older than 60 days blocks **starting a new group** only (`LEVY_OVERDUE`).

## Phase T6 — Defaults & risk
- [x] T6.1 Overdue detection, grace & reminders — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-latefee.service.ts` (`markOverdue`), `ajo-reminder.service.ts`, `ajo-risk-cycle.ts`, `jobs/ajo-jobs.ts` (hourly cycle at :05 and a daily 02:30 job; switch off with `AJO_JOBS_DISABLED=true`; started and stopped in `server.ts`). Overdue is marked after the due date and grace is counted in the group's own days. Reminders (upcoming, overdue, last day of grace if a fee exists) go out from 08:00 group-local, at most one message per person per day; a daily group gets at most one overdue message a week; login users get in-app/push/email, SMS-only people get SMS; when SMS credits run out the reminder is skipped, not queued, and officers are told once. Unique `ajo_reminders` rows make every run safe to repeat. A daily officer digest. Only tenants with Ajo fully on are touched.
- [x] T6.2 Late-fee assessments (off by default) — 2026-10-28 — Assessed once per overdue instalment the day after grace, from the rule version the member accepted that was in force on the due date (no accepted version, no fee); the calculation is stored in words. A fee starts PENDING, the member is told, and it becomes PAYABLE only 48 hours after they were told and with no dispute open (an SMS-only member who could not be reached stays unpaid-able until an officer records they were told, with a note). Payments clear instalments first, then payable fees oldest first, as a separate FEE credit in the group ledger; it is the group's income, never in a payout, never SocietyLedger's. Waivable with a reason; a waiver is undone only by a recorded reversal. Payment limit now counts payable fees (`owedIncludingFees`).
- [x] T6.3 Default cases & repayment plans — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-default.service.ts`. Only for rotating-pot members whose payout is PAID and who are then overdue past grace. The procedure the member accepted decides the action (NOTIFY_ADMIN, REPAYMENT_PLAN, CASE_AND_PLAN). A frozen snapshot is taken at opening, then an append-only case file (`AjoCaseEvent`). A plan is a timing promise: PROPOSED until an officer records the member's agreement with a note, or the member accepts; a kept plan stops new fees on the instalments it covers and a missed date is recorded once and the admins told. An admin closes a case as RESOLVED or CLOSED_UNRESOLVED with a reason. Nothing is taken from others' savings or guarantors and no membership status changes. Automatic openings are not written to the audit log (it needs a signed-in user); the case file's OPENED event is their record.
- [x] T6.4 Trust score (on-time percentage bands) — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-trust.service.ts`, `ajo-risk.ts`. On-time % over 12 months for the PERSON across their Ajo groups in the association (a new group has no history of its own); bands Excellent 95+, Good 85–94, Fair 70–84, At risk under 70, No history. Daily snapshots only when the score changed. It never blocks anyone; it feeds T3's At-risk draw rule (`atRiskMemberships` now finds real people).
- [x] T6.5 Disputes — 2026-10-28 — `ajo-dispute.service.ts`. Raised by the member or an officer on one instalment (claim of at least 10 characters, optional private evidence), one open per instalment. A different person decides UPHELD or NOT_UPHELD with a reason; decisions are immutable. While a payment is being verified or a dispute is open, fees, overdue reminders and default cases pause for that member, and a fee cannot land before one clear day after the check ends. A dispute never creates money; disputed instalments are left out of the trust score until decided.

## Phase T7 — Web
- [x] T7.1 Group admin: list, create wizard, rules, settings — built 2026-10-28; **NOT yet checked in a browser** (no browser tool here; tsc and lint clean, formatters unit-checked, every API call it makes is tested). `/dashboard/ajo` (THO1 list), `/new` (THO2 six-step wizard: kind, money, collector, account, rules, review), `/[groupId]` (overview: open, start with the smaller-group confirmation, cancel with a reason, edit settings), `/[groupId]/rules` (versions in the server's own words, publish a new one).
- [x] T7.2 Members, approvals, guarantors, payout order — built 2026-10-28; **NOT yet checked in a browser** (no browser tool here; tsc and lint clean, formatters unit-checked, every API call it makes is tested). `/[groupId]/members` (roster, add from a member search, approve, shares, SocietyLedger verification per person, guarantor with the wording shown first), `/[groupId]/order` (the recorded draw and seed, swap requests, admin decisions, emergency changes with a reason).
- [x] T7.3 Collections, reconciliation, collector management — built 2026-10-28; **NOT yet checked in a browser** (no browser tool here; tsc and lint clean, formatters unit-checked, every API call it makes is tested). `/[groupId]/payments` (queue; confirm with the duplicate and own-payment follow-ups; turn down; reverse; see the slip), `/[groupId]/handovers` (count cash and tick what was there), `/[groupId]/collectors` (assign people and targets), `/dashboard/ajo/collect` (THC1–3 for a collector: today's list, record cash with an idempotency key, hand over).
- [x] T7.4 Payouts queue/approval, defaults and cases — built 2026-10-28; **NOT yet checked in a browser** (no browser tool here; tsc and lint clean, formatters unit-checked, every API call it makes is tested). `/[groupId]/payouts` (preview with shortfall names, prepare, approve with the **password dialog**, record paid with slip, fail, cancel, reverse with password, complete the group), `/[groupId]/arrears` (late fees: told, waive, undo; claims: decide; cases: open, notes, plans, close with password).
- [~] T7.5 Member self-service — **Step 1 built 2026-10-28, NOT yet checked in a browser** (tsc and lint clean, formatters unit-checked; no browser tool was available and no dev servers were running). Web: `/dashboard/my-thrift` (THM1 + THM9), `/[membershipId]` (THM2 schedule, payments, late fees, THM7 trust, THM8 claims), `/[membershipId]/pay` (THM3: group bank details on request, transfer report with slip and an Idempotency-Key), `/[membershipId]/rules` (THM4), `/payout-account` (THM5). Shared: `lib/ajo/{api,hooks,types,format}.ts`, `components/ajo/{AjoGate,AjoStates,Money,StatusBadge,MembershipCard,DisputeDialog,proof}`, sidebar entries in `MemberSidebar.tsx` and `AdminSidebar.tsx` (the two sidebars people actually get; `Sidebar.tsx` is only a fallback and was wrongly edited first, then reverted), web `PERMISSIONS` gains the `AJO_*` keys. **API additions:** `GET /api/ajo/me` (the signed-in person's own memberships with owed, next due, payout positions and what they are waiting for; the member screens cannot start without it) and `password` required on payout approve, payout reverse and case close (`ajo-reauth.ts`, the existing rate-limited `confirmPassword`; 5 wrong tries lock the person out for 15 minutes). **Not done in step 1:** THM6 guarantor link (officer records it, T7.2), receipts per payment (API exists: `GET /contributions/:id/receipts`), reveal of the full payout account number. **Heads-up for T8:** mobile's approve/reverse/close now also need `password` in the body, so a biometric-only prompt is not enough unless T8 asks for the password too.
- [~] T7.6 Standalone group sign-up & onboarding — built 2026-10-28; **NOT yet checked in a browser** (no browser tool here; tsc and lint clean, formatters unit-checked, every API call it makes is tested). **The Ajo-only checkout is proven** (`__tests__/billing/thrift-checkout.integration.test.ts`, 4 tests): a thrift group is offered only its free `thrift-group` plan and the Ajo tiers, is quoted and charged exactly the tier, and cannot buy an association plan. **Known behaviour:** an add-on on its own needs an active subscription (403 `NO_ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION`), so a group still in its trial subscribes on the free base plan plus a tier. Built: `/signup/thrift` (public; says plainly when sign-up is closed), a link to it from `/signup`, `/ajo/status` now returns `tenantKind`, a menu trimmed to Ajo for standalone groups, and `ThriftHomeRedirect` (home goes straight into Ajo). **Not verified:** the existing Subscription page in a browser for a thrift group, and the whole sign-up and approval flow end to end. Still open: the legal-opinion gate on standalone groups. Original notes follow. **Build last in T7.** A standalone group never pays for SocietyLedger itself: its base plan `thrift-group` has no price and is hidden from associations, so it pays only for an Ajo tier (14-day trial with Ajo Starter free). T1 built the API only (`POST /api/associations/thrift-signup`, off unless `ajoStandaloneSignup` is on); there is no web page yet. **First step of T7.6: prove a `THRIFT_GROUP` can buy an Ajo-only tier through checkout with the zero-price base plan and is never asked to choose or pay for an association plan; if it cannot, fix billing before building the page.** Also needed: a trimmed dashboard that opens straight into Ajo with no empty association modules in the menu. Keep the page hidden until the switch is on; the legal-opinion gate on standalone groups (spec) is still open.

## Phase T9 — Admin console
- [x] T9.1 Ajo platform settings — built 2026-10-30, **NOT yet looked at in a browser**. The levy controls are on the admin Settings page (`settings/ajo-levy-section.tsx`): cycle and participant rate (shown as a percent), minimum and maximum in full naira, and which Ajo tiers pay no levy. The API already validated the owner's ceilings (₦5,000.00 per cycle, ₦1,000.00 per participant), so the screen only shows them. The standalone sign-up switch (added early on 2026-10-28) is unchanged. **Also built: Ajo pricing** (`/ajo/pricing`): the Ajo tiers as a list (price, size, groups on it, draft or unpublished) with "Edit price" going into the existing Plan Builder add-on editor, so a price change keeps its draft, preview and publish safeguards and current holders stay on the version they bought.
- [x] T9.2 Groups & payouts oversight — built 2026-10-30, **NOT yet looked at in a browser**. API `/api/admin/ajo-oversight` (`ajo-oversight.service.ts`, `.routes.ts`): the cross-tenant list (filter by owner kind, type, status, name), the group detail (people, payouts, default cases, levy, tier), tier preview, **Change tier** and **cancel a payout**. Support, compliance, finance and super admins can look; only SUPER_ADMIN and FINANCE_ADMIN can override. Admin screens `/ajo/groups` and `/ajo/groups/[id]`. Each override asks for a reason of at least 10 characters (`ConfirmDestructive` or the tier dialog) and writes a row to the Action log (`AJO_PAYOUT_VOIDED_BY_STAFF`, `AJO_TIER_CHANGED`, `AJO_TIER_DOWNGRADE_SCHEDULED`, `AJO_TIER_DOWNGRADE_CANCELLED`). Differences from the plan: (1) **suspend and reactivate apply to the owner** (association or standalone group), not to one circle, because suspension belongs to the owner; the group page has "Suspend X…" and "Reactivate X…" for SUPER_ADMIN only, through the platform's one suspend path, with a reason (review fix, 2026-10-30); (2) a payout already recorded as paid is never touched here (the group admin reverses it, because the money has moved); (3) the trial tier cannot be a target; (4) "make the excess read-only" is the existing capacity rule (nobody new can join while over the limit; nobody already in loses anything), now **shown**: an "Over tier limit" badge on the list and a warning on the group page.
  - **Tier change rules** (`services/billing/tier-change.service.ts`, billing owns the tables): an upgrade applies now with no charge (the new tier runs to the end of the period, the price is paid at renewal); a downgrade of a **paid** tier waits for the end of the paid period (`tenant_addons.pending_addon_id`, migration `20261121_tenant_addon_pending_tier`, applied to the dev database; the renewal quote prices the lower tier in full and ends the old one); a downgrade of a tier **never paid for** (trial, complimentary, granted) applies now; a downgrade is **refused** when the group has more active contributors than the lower tier allows, with how many to remove, and staff cannot force it; picking the tier the group already has cancels a waiting downgrade.
- [x] T9.3 Add-on analytics — built 2026-10-30, **NOT yet looked at in a browser**. `/ajo/analytics` over `GET /api/admin/ajo-oversight/analytics` (SUPER_ADMIN and FINANCE_ADMIN only, it shows revenue): groups holding each tier (paying, standalone), circles by status and type, **active contributors** (distinct people with a confirmed, not-reversed contribution in the last 30 days, against the 30 days before), tier subscriptions each month (paying groups only, annual spread over 12 months) and the platform levy by status with a 6-month series. No chart library: month bars are plain progress bars. Also shows what groups **paid** for tiers each month (read from the verified payments' stored quotes, net of the credit for a replaced tier) and the monthly subscription of the average paying group (review fix, 2026-10-30). The page shows "Access Denied" to roles that cannot see revenue instead of a raw error.
- [x] T9.4 SocietyLedger verification review queue — built 2026-10-30, **NOT yet looked at in a browser**. `/ajo/verifications` (Waiting, Verified, Rejected) and `/ajo/verifications/[id]` over the existing `/api/admin/ajo/verifications` routes: what the person submitted, the three-point checklist (Verify unlocks only when all three pass), Reject with a reason the group admin sees (at least 10 characters), earlier reviews, and a warning when there is no payout account yet. Only SUPER_ADMIN and COMPLIANCE_ADMIN see the buttons; others can look.
- **T9 review fixes (2026-10-30):** (1) the role lookup now uses `GET /api/admin/ajo-oversight/whoami`, because `admin/me` is SUPER_ADMIN-only and compliance and finance staff read as having no role, so their Verify and override buttons never appeared; (2) a tier change now runs in **one transaction behind a per-owner lock**, with its Action log row inside it (two staff acting at once take turns; a failure leaves neither the change nor a half-written log), and writes **one** row (`AJO_TIER_CHANGED`), not that plus `ADDON_GRANTED`; (3) a renewal whose scheduled lower tier can no longer be sold **keeps the tier the group has** instead of silently dropping it; (4) staff cancelling a payout is now tested against a real database; (5) `ConfirmDestructive` keeps the typed reason when the action fails; (6) saved levy-exempt codes for a tier that is not on sale still show; (7) an unpaid downgrade's dialog says it applies at once. The admin Playwright spec `e2e/ajo.spec.ts` (7 tests) was **run in a real browser against the dev API and passes**: login, every page, the tier dialog preview, no page errors. The existing `smoke.spec.ts` has 3 old failures that are not from T9: it looks for a `data-stat-card` attribute the stat card never had, it asserts no `unsafe-eval` in the CSP (dev mode needs it), and it needs the web app on port 3000.
- **T9 tests:** pure tier rules; tier change against a real database (upgrade, scheduled downgrade and its renewal quote, unpaid downgrade, blocked downgrade, cancelling a waiting one, reason and running subscription required); oversight routes (who can reach what, reasons, audit rows, a blocked downgrade as a clear 409); oversight reads against a real database (list filters, JSON-safe detail); analytics (a person counted once, reversed, old and unconfirmed payments left out). 235 pass on `sl_ajo_test` across billing, oversight, analytics, levy and routes (the whole billing and Ajo oversight set; `default-plans.integration.test.ts` fails to compile with the older `checkout` suite because both declare the same top-level names, which was already so). The admin app typechecks (the 2 `packages/shared` Set-iteration errors are old). The group page was opened in the browser against the dev database (2 groups, both on the trial tier); **a group with a paid tier, a scheduled downgrade, or the compliance and finance roles were not seen in a browser** (no such accounts or data exist locally).
- **Test-database trap:** a suite that imports a service at the top of the file loads Prisma before `process.env.DATABASE_URL` is set, and silently runs against the dev database. The tier-change suite did this once and left test rows in `societyledger`; they were removed by their run ids, and the suite now sets the URL first and refuses to run unless `current_database()` contains "test".

## Phase T10 — Reports & exports
- [x] T10.1 The eight reports (API) — built 2026-10-30. `apps/api/src/modules/ajo/reports/`: contributions, member ledger, collector performance, payouts, defaults and arrears, group financial summary, fees and revenue, audit and activity. Routes `GET /api/ajo/reports` (list, with what is locked and why), `GET /api/ajo/reports/:name` (figures for every circle plus one page of rows) and `GET /api/ajo/reports/:name/export?format=csv|pdf`. One report core (`report-context.ts`: filters, the circle's own timezone, who may see which rows, **one** ledger query and **one** status function shared by every report), eight report modules, one registry (`report-registry.ts`: name, tier, rule, filters), CSV and PDF writers (`report-export.ts`).
  - **Decisions kept:** files are streamed in the response and never stored; each export writes an `AJO_REPORT_EXPORTED` row to the audit log, and the audit report lists it. Max 12 months, 100,000 rows to view or as a CSV and 5,000 rows in a PDF (`RANGE_TOO_LONG`, `REPORT_TOO_LARGE`, `REPORT_TOO_LARGE_FOR_PDF` say how to fix it). Money is counted on the ledger day in the circle's timezone; expected, outstanding and overdue on the due date; only CONFIRMED, not-reversed money is received. Basic reports (contributions, member ledger, payouts, financial summary) come with every tier; advanced (collector performance, defaults and arrears, fees and revenue) need `ajo.advanced_reports`; the audit report needs the **new** feature `ajo.audit_reports` (Business and Enterprise). Officers (manage, reconcile or approve) see everything; anyone else holding `ajo.reports` is a collector and sees only contributions, member ledger and collector performance, and only their assigned people. Circles are never blended: totals are added only across circles of the same kind. No bank account number, email, address, proof link, guarantor detail or note reaches any report or file (tested on all eight, JSON and CSV); phone numbers appear only on defaults and arrears, for chasing.
  - **Differences from what we agreed, on purpose:** (1) "Overdue" is **two** figures, as the board already has them: *Overdue, in grace* and *Missed, past grace*; the single "overdue = past grace" wording would have disagreed with the board. (2) Rows are one flat paged table with a Circle column (figures stay per circle, plus the same-kind totals); per-circle row blocks could not be paged. (3) While the add-on is expired it keeps the reports **its own tier included** (read from the tier that lapsed; with no record of a tier nothing is taken away), so a lapsed Starter does not gain advanced reports. (4) CSV money is a plain number in naira under a "(₦)" heading, so a column adds up in Excel; the PDF writes "NGN 5,000.00" everywhere, including inside text the server wrote (the built-in fonts print ₦ as a stray mark; found by looking at a rendered PDF). (5) The audit report leaves out routine payment records (they are all in the member ledger) and free-text notes, and shows confirmations, reversals, payout steps, waivers, rule and order changes, default-case steps, cash counts and exports. (6) The range check answers 400, not 413 (the shared error type has no 413).
  - **Seeds, run them on every database:** `platform-modules.seed.ts` adds `ajo.audit_reports`; `ajo-addons.seed.ts` now gives it to Business and Enterprise and, because that seed never touches an existing add-on, also adds it to the **published** Business and Enterprise versions already there (`ensureAjoAuditFeature`, safe to repeat). **Run on the development database on 2026-10-30:** the feature was added and given to the 2 published Business and Enterprise versions there. Other databases still need both seeds.
  - **Tests:** 20 on a real database (`ajo-reports.integration`: a reported payment is never counted as received; contributions, balance, member ledger and financial summary agree with the group ledger to the kobo after a reversal and a payout; a payment at 23:30 and one at 00:30 in Lagos land in the right months; tier locks; collector scope; another association's circle is never reachable; the leak test; export audit row; CSV and PDF; paging; fees, defaults, payouts and audit contents), 9 on the routes, 5 on the seed step. All pass on `sl_ajo_test`. The whole Ajo and billing run: 904 passed, 55 skipped, 1 failed: `ajo-cycles.integration` "asks each saver once", which passes (16 of 16) on its own, so it is the cycle-end job reading groups other suites left in the shared scratch database when suites run together, not this work.
- [x] T10.2 Web report screens + CSV/PDF exports — built 2026-10-30. `/dashboard/ajo/reports` (hub, locked reports shown with why) and `/dashboard/ajo/reports/[name]` (the counting rule, filters for circle, dates, person and collector, figures per circle and same-kind totals, the rows paged 25 at a time, CSV and PDF buttons); a "Thrift Reports" menu entry for anyone with `ajo.reports`. **Excel-compatible = the CSV** (UTF-8 with a byte-order mark, numbers as numbers, text that cannot run as a formula); there is no separate .xlsx. **Checked in a real browser (Chromium via Playwright) with the API answered by canned replies** (26 checks: all eight reports listed, the audit report locked and explained, rule printed, money in full, a dash for a figure that does not apply, paging to the last page, filters sent to the API and back to page 1, CSV download with the API's file name and filters, a locked report by address, no sideways scroll at 390px). That run found and fixed three things outside this feature's files: the API only exposed `Set-Cookie` to browsers, so **every web download had been using its fallback file name** (now `Content-Disposition` is exposed); the dashboard `<main>` lacked `min-w-0`, so any wide table stretched the whole page (the existing thrift circles list was 504px wide on a 390px screen); and the breadcrumb trail did not wrap. **Not checked:** the real API with a real sign-in (the server side is covered by the API tests), light theme, and the PDF in the browser's own download. Tests: 13 new in `apps/web/__tests__/ajo/reports.test.tsx`.
- [x] T10.3 Mobile reports — built 2026-10-30, **NOT run on a device or emulator** (type-check, lint and tests only). Owner decision: **full parity with the web**, not just summaries. `manage/thrift/reports` (hub) and `manage/thrift/reports/[name]`: the rule, Circle and Dates dropdowns (This month, Last month, Last 3 months, This year; "This month" sends no dates so the API's default applies, as on the web), Person and Collector where they apply, figures per circle as ledger rows, and **every row as a card that keeps every column of the web table** with "Show more" 25 at a time; Share PDF and Share CSV through the existing share sheet (`saveAndShareFile`). The audit report is on the phone too. A "Reports" row on the Thrift circles screen. Tests: 21 (formatting, the row cards, the dates a quick choice means, the query the API receives). The 6 remaining type errors in the mobile app are in files this work did not touch (animation typings in `Passbook` and `ImageViewerModal`).
- **T10 review fixes (2026-10-30), all 15 findings:**
  1. *Row limit and paging.* A report may have 100,000 rows to view or as a CSV (was 20,000, which also blocked viewing); a PDF holds 5,000 and says to use the CSV above that (`REPORT_TOO_LARGE_FOR_PDF`). "Next page" reuses the computed report for 30 seconds (page 1, a changed filter and every file are always fresh; the cache is per person, so a collector's report is never served to an officer; at most 5 reports, none over 50,000 rows).
  2. *Untested figures.* Real-data tests now cover collector performance (cash recorded, confirmed, awaiting, handed in, counted, missing, unreconciled days, and a collector's own view), every owed, pending-payout, late-fee, cash and awaiting line of the financial summary, and a PDF of all eight reports.
  3. *Export audit row* now records the person and collector filter as well as the circle and dates.
  4. *Expired add-on* now keeps only the reports its own tier included (`getLapsedFeatureCodes` in the billing service reads the lapsed tier's features).
  5. *Seed edits to published versions* are now written to the staff Action log (`ADDON_VERSION_FEATURE_ADDED`, with the reason); the 2 rows missing for the development database were written afterwards. Entitlements are cached for only 20 seconds, so a change shows within that.
  6. *Shadow mode:* the tier lock follows `FEATURE_FLAG_MODE=shadow` like every other gate; a role rule is still enforced.
  7. *`AJO_FAMILY`* now lives in `ajo-access.ts` (the oversight module re-exports it), so the tenant reports no longer import from the staff oversight module.
  8. *Refused requests:* web and phone no longer ask for the collector list (needs `ajo.manage`) or the people list (needs an officer permission) for someone without it.
  9. *Dark theme:* figure tiles use `border-line-strong` and `bg-surface-muted`, so they read as panels (checked in the browser).
  10. *Scheduled payouts:* a round whose instalments were all waived keeps its due date, so later rounds no longer shift to the wrong date and beneficiary (tested).
  11. *A collector, or a person filter, sees only the circles those people are in*; no other circle's name or empty figures.
  12. *PDF rows* show all their text (a row is as tall as its tallest cell); a PDF is limited to 5,000 rows so building one cannot hold up the server for long.
  13. *A refused download* now shows the API's own words: on the web the blob body is read back into the error, on the phone an arraybuffer body is decoded (`decodedBody`, tested).
  14. *Stale figures:* while a new choice loads, the web page dims the old figures and says "Updating for your new choice…".
  15. *Excel and phone numbers:* a phone number is written as `="0803…"` so the leading zero survives; it is the only cell written that way, and a name or note that begins with = is still neutralised (tested).
  - **Tests after the fixes:** 44 for the reports on a real database and routes (all pass), 7 on the seed step, 39 on the web, 34 on the phone's report and error files. The whole Ajo and billing run: 924 passed, 55 skipped, 1 failed: the same `ajo-cycles.integration` "asks each saver once", which passes alone (shared scratch database). The web screens were rechecked in the browser (26 checks pass).
  - **Still not verified:** the real API with a real sign-in, light theme, the PDF through the browser's own download, and the phone screens on a device.

Every page follows the task/concept template: plain-language intro, Before you start, numbered steps with the exact UI labels, a naira worked example where money moves (always in full, e.g. ₦5,000.00), What happens next, Related.
- [x] T11.2 Joining and approval, staying safe, troubleshooting & FAQ — joining-and-accepting-the-rules, adding-and-approving-people, staying-safe, raising-a-query-about-a-payment, leaving-a-circle, and an Ajo FAQ (15 Q&As). Billing FAQ, Add-ons page and the Member guide now point at Ajo.
- [x] T11.3 Reference updates & screenshots — reference pages (permissions, statuses, modules-by-plan, payment-methods) regenerated with Ajo (the generator now follows spreads and top-level const references). A repeatable demo seed (`apps/api/prisma/seeds/demo-ajo.seed.ts`, with `_demo-stubs.ts` to silence email/SMS/push) and an Ajo capture flow (`scripts/screenshots/flows/ajo-thrift.ts`, 6 roles) produced 42 web screenshots (every task page now shows at least one).
  - **Checks:** `check:frontmatter` 249 pages, 0 failed; `check:links` 619 links, 0 broken; `coverage` Thrift (Ajo) 31/31 published, 167/167 web routes; docs `build` passes; every image the pages reference exists.
  - **Review fixes (2026-10-30):** (1) every task page now has a screenshot (added guarantor dialog, hand-over dialog, Leave dialog, saver Statements and cash-trail tabs, claims tab, Add-ons page, circles list); (2) Leave steps corrected — a reason of 5+ characters is required and the confirm button is "Leave the circle"; (3) "I already paid this" is shown only for an unpaid, past-due day with write access, and the saver's "Ask about this payment" is on **Where my cash is** (not Payments) — pages corrected; (4) Leave outcomes reworded to what the code does (officers settle what is owed); (5) "Under review" now says "not charged a late fee or chased" (what the officer screen says), not the unproven default-case claim; (6) mobile labels corrected: "Share PDF / Share CSV / Share circle statement", menu path Money → My thrift; (7) two older pages re-dated; (8) seed-demo.md documents the Ajo seed; (9) the alt text of a screenshot broke the MDX build (unescaped quotes) — fixed, build passes; (10) 390px check on 7 pages: no horizontal overflow of the page.
  - **Not verified:** the phone steps are written from the code and were **not run on a device** (the Payout account route on the phone is an inference); the pages were checked at 390px for overflow only, not in the dark theme; no screenshot of the member "I already paid this" dialog (every demo member is paid up); screenshots were taken from the demo tenant on the seed day (statuses are computed against the real clock, so re-seed with `--reset` before recapturing).
  - **Incident to know about:** while the demo seed ran, your own API (port 4001, real email key, Ajo hourly job on) touched the demo data and created 2 officer digests (in-app, possibly email to the demo officers). Set `AJO_JOBS_DISABLED=true` in dev to avoid this.
  - **Never run the demo seed on production.**
