### Phase T13 — Transparency & member dashboards (owner-approved 2026-10-29; spec §16)
- [~] T13.1 Phase A: Circle board for rotating pots — built 2026-10-29, **NOT yet run in a browser or on a device**. API, web and mobile done and tested; see the Latest line. Differences from the plan: (1) the activity log is built from the same records officers use (confirmed payments, payouts, rule versions, order changes) rather than from audit-log entries, so no free-text audit JSON can reach a member; (2) a position's scheduled payout date is its round's due date; (3) "this round" is the nearest round not yet past its due date, so a round still inside grace is shown as Overdue, not as the current round; (4) officers always see names, whatever the circle's setting, and can open the board when it is off. Not built in A (belong to D): the circle statement PDF, the transparency notifications, officer accountability screens, the docs page.
- [~] T13.2 Phase B: collector-thrift cycles — built 2026-10-29, **NOT yet run in a browser or on a device**. Not built: a CSV "amount" column for bulk upload (savers added in bulk get the group's smallest and can change it until the cycle begins), and an arrears carry-over (a continuing saver's unpaid installments from the finished cycle stay as arrears in that cycle; they are not added to the next one). The collector's Today list and the trust score were not changed: both read installments, which are per saver and per cycle, and the existing collector tests still pass.
- [~] T13.3 Phase C: the saver's own view — built 2026-10-29, **NOT yet run in a browser or on a device**. Differences from the plan: (1) the collector's **collection days and time window**, and an **office contact**, are not shown because nothing in the data records them yet (the collector card shows name, phone, since when, daily target and last cash taken); (2) the statement is for the **running cycle** only: earlier cycles show in "Past cycles" with their totals but cannot be downloaded yet; (3) PDFs write money as "NGN 5,000.00" because the built-in PDF fonts have no naira sign (the CSV and every screen use ₦); (4) a saver's **late fees** still show on the circle page, not on the new page, which links to them; (5) a streak ignores a payment still inside its grace (it has not been decided yet); (6) "ask about this payment" opens the existing dispute form for the payment's instalment, so a query is a dispute an officer decides. Not built: the "cash confirmed by office" and "payout recorded" saver notifications (Phase D).
- [~] T13.4 Phase D: statement PDF, notifications, officer accountability, docs page — built 2026-10-29, **NOT yet run in a browser or on a device**; the docs page was written from the code, **not performed step by step on staging** as the Docs Definition of Done asks. Differences from the plan: (1) the statement PDF writes money as "NGN 5,000.00" (the built-in PDF fonts have no naira sign) and shows the latest 36 rounds if there are more; (2) the "payout recorded" notice and the "round fully paid" notice go to every member of the circle with the board on, and name the recipient only if they accepted the board rule; (3) a circle that existed before notices began is not told about rounds more than 7 days old; (4) the "cash confirmed by office" notice for savers is the existing confirmation receipt, so no new notice was added; (5) officer accountability is for the officers of the group and is a separate tab from the members' board; (6) the weekly summary is sent on Mondays in the circle's timezone. Not built: a Docs page for collector savers' day-to-day steps beyond what this page covers.
- **Milestone TL:** Ajo add-on live.

## Track T open questions
Product questions only. None blocks T1.

| ID | Question | Gates | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| F1 | Trust-score formula | T0.2, T6.4 | resolved 2026-10-27 — on-time percentage over 12 months, bands Excellent/Good/Fair/At risk |
| F2 | Collector tools are online only; weak connectivity in markets is a real risk | T8.3, T12.3 | accepted for the first release, revisit after the pilot |
| F3 | Platform-levy formula (up to ₦5,000 per cycle + up to ₦1,000 per participant): caps, interaction with the collector fee, who pays; billed on the add-on invoice | T0.1, T5.4 | resolved 2026-10-27 — 0.5% cycle (₦500–₦5,000) + 0.25% per participant (₦50–₦1,000), Starter exempt |
| F4 | Existing billing lockout blocks a whole tenant on expiry; an expired Ajo add-on must leave history readable | T1.2 | decided 2026-10-27 — built as read-only mode in T1.2 |
| F5 | Payout order rules (fixed, recorded random draw, admin-set for need) | T0.2, T3.7 | resolved 2026-10-27 — hybrid |
| F6 | Which Ajo features each tier includes. Built as: Starter = rotating pots + guarantors; Growth and above add collector management, reminders and advanced reports (spec §9 lists "collector management" under Growth). This puts the collector tools, the commonest market model, behind ₦8,000.00 a month | T1.2 | **owner to confirm** — change in the Plan Builder, no code change |
| F7 | Standalone thrift-group sign-up ships **closed** (`ajoStandaloneSignup` off) until the owner switches it on after the pilot | T1.3 | **owner to confirm** the default |
| F8 | Enterprise tier is not on the public price list (`isPublic` off, ₦40,000.00 a month placeholder, quota unlimited); staff grant it by agreement | T1.2 | **owner to confirm** |

## Backend gaps (API needs that block or limit a screen)
Log here instead of inventing endpoints. Each: what, which screen, proposed fix, status (open / approved / done / won't do).

| # | Gap | Screens | Proposed fix | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | Paystack `initialize` accepts one `transactionId` only — no multi-item checkout | M2, M3 | Add optional `transactionIds[]` creating one gateway transaction covering several items, or keep sequential single-item checkout | **declined for v1** — sequential single-item checkout is the accepted v1 design, not a gap awaiting a fix |
| G2 | `packages/shared` types are stale (`User.id: string`, API returns numbers) | all | F1.3 adds verified types; decide whether web uses the old ones before changing them | **resolved 2026-10-20 (F1.3)** — confirmed `apps/web` doesn't import `User`/`Member`/`PaginatedResponse` from `@societyledger/shared` either (grepped; its 2 real uses of the package are unrelated), so mobile's fix is simply never importing the stale types: `src/types/api/*.ts` (11 domain files) typed directly from `docs/mobile/api-contract.md`, verified per-endpoint rather than one generic `PaginatedResponse<T>` (api-contract.md §10 found 4 incompatible real pagination shapes). `packages/shared` itself deliberately left untouched — out of scope per the contract doc's own conclusion, which overrides architecture.md §2's "or fix the existing ones" alternative |
| G3 | `MEETING_MINUTES` alias differs: API `members.view_own`, web `members.edit` | GV5 | Owner to choose; mobile uses stricter `members.edit` meanwhile | **superseded by G26 (2026-10-13) — live-confirmed this is a real authorization gap, not just a naming mismatch** |
| G4 | Subscription/RBAC/tenant errors identified by message text only | all | F0.5 adds `code` fields | **resolved 2026-10-19 (F0.5)** — `code` added to every real 403/400 exit point; see F0.5's progress-tracker entry for the full list, including 3 RBAC functions (`requirePermission`'s "Permission denied", `requireAnyPermission`, `requireAssociationRole`'s "Access denied") architecture.md's own text didn't name explicitly but which are the same semantic NoPermission condition |
| G5 | No public endpoint to list a member's associations by email | A3 | Not needed for code entry; revisit if users struggle | won't do (v1) |
| G6 | Existing DB-backed docs CMS (`/admin/documentation`, `/api/docs`, `DocPage`/`DocFeedback`) overlaps the new docs site | web `/documentation` | Keep it, unlinked, after D6.3 redirects; owner decides later whether to retire it | **resolved 2026-10-14 — D6.3 shipped the `/documentation*` redirect to the docs site; the CMS code itself is untouched and still unlinked from public nav, per the proposed fix** |
| G8 | Platform routes are guarded by `SUPER_ADMIN` only; `SUPPORT_AGENT`, `FINANCE_ADMIN`, `COMPLIANCE_ADMIN` exist but can't use any admin section | admin X4–X21 | Approve the role matrix in project-overview §10.7, then widen `requirePlatformRole([...])` per route | open |
| G9 | No API to create/manage platform staff accounts or their platform roles | admin | Keep seed/script for v1 (`pnpm -F @societyledger/api …`); add endpoints + X-screen later if wanted | open — script in v1 |
| G10 | Web currently sets the auth cookie with `COOKIE_DOMAIN` (shared across subdomains) for everyone, including platform users | web/admin | Admin uses its own host-only cookie (§16.3); platform users stop getting tenant cookies at A3.1 (B2). Consider host-only cookies for tenants too (separate decision) | open |
| G11 | No reserved-subdomain list — an association could register `admin`, `docs`, `api`… | API, web signup, mobile A3 | B3 in A1.2 | approved (A1.2) |
| G12 | ~~Unknown: how Dokploy builds `api`/`web` today~~ — resolved: `apps/api/Dockerfile`, `apps/web/Dockerfile` and `docker-compose.dokploy.yml` already exist on `main` (the "Migrate SocietyLedger from Vercel to Dokploy" commit, b61d388) and are the real source of truth — **one Dokploy Compose application**, not one Dokploy Application per app. `architecture.md §17.1`/§17.2/§17.3/§17.4 and §1 corrected to match (2026-10-12). `API_DOMAIN` confirmed by owner: `https://api.societyledger.com.ng`. `library-docs.md`'s "each app = one Dokploy Application" wording still predates this — owner explicitly deferred correcting it to D1.7 (when `docs` is actually added as a compose service) rather than now | deploy | None — reconcile `library-docs.md`'s wording at D1.7 | resolved (architecture.md); `library-docs.md` wording deferred to D1.7 by owner |
| G7 | Docs page feedback has no backend (the API's `DocFeedback` is tied to DB doc page ids) | docs Feedback | GA4 events only in v1; add a slug-based endpoint later if needed | open — GA4 in v1 |
| G13 | `requireModule()` middleware (module/plan gating) defaults to `shadow` mode — logs but never blocks — and `FEATURE_FLAG_MODE=enforce` isn't set in any env file, including `docker-compose.dokploy.yml`. The frontend also has zero handling of the `MODULE_DISABLED` error code. Found at D2.2 while verifying "what not on your plan looks like" for `plans-and-modules.mdx`. **Re-confirmed and sharpened at D5.3**: the demo association had **no subscription record at all** (`GET /subscriptions/current` → "No active subscription found") yet retained full, working access to Accounting and Cooperatives, both Enterprise-exclusive modules — proof the shadow-mode gap isn't theoretical, it's the live, current behaviour for an account with zero billing relationship to the platform | all module-gated screens; plan/subscription status generally | Decide whether/when to flip to `enforce` in production, and build the frontend's locked/upgrade state before doing so | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R7.1)** — frontend `moduleLockStore`/`ModuleLockedModal` + both interceptors built; three backend default-fallback/`upgradeRequired` bugs fixed in the same pass; `FEATURE_FLAG_MODE=enforce` flipped locally only (production stays shadow, explicit decision); demo association upgraded to Enterprise to match its seeded data. Live-verified end-to-end: Enterprise tier allows welfare, Starter tier correctly 403s with `upgradeRequired: true` |
| G14 | Notification preferences (`notifications.controller.ts`'s `getPreferences`/`updatePreferences`) are stored in an in-memory `Map` (`preferencesStore`), not a database table or Redis — lost on every server restart/redeploy, not shared across instances if the API ever scales horizontally. Found at D2.2 while verifying `notifications-and-channels.mdx` | notification preferences screen | Add a real `NotificationPreference` Prisma model | **resolved 2026-10-15 (R3.6)** — real `NotificationPreference` model added (one row per user per association, replacing the in-memory `Map`). New `services/notification-preferences.service.ts` centralizes preference resolution and dispatch across all three real channels — email, in-app, and a newly-built SMS channel (reusing `smsService.sendQuickSMS()`, consuming real credits). Wired into every real notification sender found: `notifications.controller.ts`'s `create()`/`createAnnouncement()`, and `jobs/meeting-jobs.ts`'s reminder/cancellation notifications. `createAnnouncement()` also corrected from `type: 'system'` to `type: 'announcement'` — it was never using the dedicated `announcementEmail/SMS/InApp` preference fields that already existed for exactly this category. Live-verified: disabling a user's `announcementEmail` preference correctly skipped them (and only them) when broadcasting a real announcement; the in-app notification still used the real `'announcement'` type for all recipients |
| G15 | `/dashboard/member/my-profile` fires a real "Member profile not found" error toast (twice, on every load) for at least the `member@demo.com` seeded account — some unrelated fetch on page load 404s even though the member record and profile both clearly exist and load fine otherwise. The page's own actual features (view profile, change password) work correctly regardless. Found at D2.3 while verifying `changing-your-password.mdx`; not root-caused (many `members`/`member-finance`/`cooperatives` controllers return this exact message, so the specific failing call wasn't isolated) | `/dashboard/member/my-profile` | Root-cause which fetch is 404ing and why | **resolved 2026-10-15 (while building R2.7)** — finally root-caused: `members.controller.ts`'s `getMe()`/`updateMe()` matched `Member.email === User.email`, but `Member.email` is frequently `null`/different from the login email (confirmed live: `member@demo.com`'s own `Member` row has `email: null`). Fixed to match on the real relation, `Member.userId === req.user.id`. Live-verified: `/members/me` now returns the real member record instead of 404ing |
| G16 | **The `member` role has no self-service permission for voting, welfare or cooperatives anywhere in the system** — `/api/voting/active`, `/api/welfare/dashboard`, `/api/cooperatives/my-profile` all require the same broad, officer-level keys (`members.view`, `ledger.view`, `ledger.create`) used to view *all* members/ledgers, with no `_own`-scoped equivalent of the kind that already exists for finance (`ledger.view_own`, `payments.view_own`). Found at D3.1 verifying `savings-and-loans.mdx`/`welfare.mdx`/`voting.mdx` — confirmed by direct `403` responses from a real `member` token. Worked around *for the demo association only* (3 `RolePermission` rows added directly, `rbac-seed.ts` untouched) so these pages could be documented against real, working screens — not shipped anywhere else | voting, welfare, cooperatives self-service | Design proper `_own`-scoped permissions for these 3 modules and widen the real `member` role template with them — a product decision, not a docs fix | **partially resolved 2026-10-15** — the voting third implemented ahead of schedule inside R2 (real `elections.view_own`/`nominate_own`/`vote_own` and `polls.view_own`/`vote_own` added, `ELECTION_VIEW`/`ELECTION_NOMINATE`/`ELECTION_VOTE`/`POLL_VIEW`/`POLL_VOTE` repointed to them from `members.view`, assigned to all 4 roles). Live-verified: a plain member can now self-nominate and vote for real, in any association, not just the demo-patched one. **Welfare and cooperatives still open** — not touched by Track R yet; whichever phase next touches those modules should implement the same pattern. **The Guarantor Requests crash and the broken accept/decline action it referenced are now fixed, at D4.4 — see the session notes** |
| G17 | **Members have no way to pay via real Paystack checkout or submit a bank-transfer proof for officer verification on the web today.** `/dashboard/my-finances/pay`'s payment-method dropdown (Bank Transfer/Cash/Card/POS) is cosmetic — `POST /api/members/me/pay` marks the transaction `paid` immediately regardless of method, with no Paystack redirect, no card charge, no proof attachment, and no officer confirmation step; it's a self-declaration, confirmed instantly by the member themselves. The real Paystack `initialize`/`verify` and the bank-transfer-proof endpoints both require officer-only permissions (`payments.view`/`payments.manual_create`), unreachable from a `member` token. Confirmed by reading `member-finance.controller.ts`'s `processPayment` and by a real live payment (`PAY000647`) that completed with zero verification. Found and confirmed with the owner at D3.1 — `paying-online.mdx`/`paying-by-bank-transfer.mdx` document this real behaviour with a warning callout rather than the Paystack/proof-upload flow the original build-plan description assumed; true Paystack checkout and proof upload are mobile-track features (F2.8/F2.9), not yet built | member payment pages (web) | Owner's call whether the unverified self-declare flow is acceptable long-term for web, or should be gated the same way the mobile flows will be | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R7.2)** — full two-step proof-upload + officer-approval flow built per owner's explicit spec: bank-transfer requires receipt file upload; Cash/Card/POS requires a written declaration note (who paid whom, circumstances). Officer sees both proof types in `/dashboard/payments/verification`. Also fixed in the same pass: both officer verification pages were broken (G92) and the member upload endpoint was unreachable by real member tokens (G93). All three proof paths (cash-declare+approve, cash-declare+reject, bank-transfer-file+approve) live-verified end-to-end |
| G18 | **The member financial ledger isn't kept current automatically — confirmed system-wide at D3.3, with a real but partial officer fix.** First found at D3.1 (one member's `/dashboard/my-finances/ledger` showed "No transactions yet" despite real payments). At D3.3, confirmed system-wide: the officer-wide `/dashboard/finance/member-ledger` list showed ₦0/BALANCED for all 61 members despite ₦3.1M+ in real collected payments. Selecting **Sync All Ledgers** there is a real, working fix for the *summary balance* (confirmed before/after — both the officer list and the affected member's own Current Balance/Total Debits/Credits became correct). But even after syncing, the itemised entry list (Total Entries, All Entries/Statement tabs) stays empty — the sync fixes the aggregate number, not the line-item history. Whatever writes `Transaction`/`Payment` rows never posts to the per-entry ledger table, and the sync action doesn't backfill it either | `/dashboard/my-finances/ledger`, `/dashboard/finance/member-ledger`, `your-statement.mdx`, `member-ledgers.mdx` | Root-cause whether per-entry ledger rows are ever written by anything, or backfill them as part of the sync action | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6.2) — re-verified live, stale.** 1,440 real itemised ledger entries exist for the demo association (confirmed directly in the DB), and both `GET /member-ledger/ledger/:memberId/entries` and `GET /member-ledger/my-ledger/entries` return them correctly — the frontend's own client functions already point at the right paths. Whatever caused the original "stays empty" finding no longer reproduces; no code change was needed |
| G20 | Member IDs are formatted inconsistently between the demo seed and the real application code: `demo-association.seed.ts` generates `MEM-0001` (with a dash), but `members.controller.ts`'s `create`/`bulkImport` generate `` `MEM${count}` `` → `MEM0061` (no dash) for every member added through the real UI/API. Cosmetic only, found at D3.2 creating a live test member; not fixed | member ID display everywhere | Decide on one format and make the seed match the real code (or vice versa) | **resolved 2026-10-17 (R4.5)** — `demo-association.seed.ts` changed to the no-dash `MEM####` format all 4 real generation sites already used (`members.controller.ts` ×2, `member-invites.service.ts`, `member-finance.controller.ts`); same fix applied to the two docs pages' `MEM-0001` examples. Live-verified: a new member created via the real API got `MEM0062` |
| G21 | **Waiving a penalty is unreachable from the UI** — `PenaltyWaiver`, the `/api/finance/advanced/penalties/:id/waive` endpoint, and a full waiver modal (`handleWaivePenalty`, `showWaiverModal` state) all exist in `apps/web/.../finance/penalties/page.tsx`, but nothing in that file ever calls `setSelectedTransaction` with a real value or `setShowWaiverModal(true)` — there's no button anywhere that opens it. Confirmed by reading the whole component, not assumed. Found at D3.3 writing `waiving-a-penalty.mdx` | Penalties page, Waivers tab | Add the missing trigger (likely a "Waive" action per applied penalty, probably needing a new "Applied Penalties" list the page doesn't currently have) | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6.3) — bigger than described: the waiver modal itself was never rendered anywhere either**, not just untriggered (`showWaiverModal` was dead state, no `<Modal>`/`modals.open()` call referenced it). Built a real "Applied Penalties" tab (fetches `GET /finance/transactions?type=penalty`) with a Waive action per outstanding penalty, plus the actual waiver form modal. Live-verified: waived a real penalty transaction, confirmed it flipped to `status: waived`, test waiver reverted afterward |

Confirmed by reading the whole file, then confirmed live (the dropdown never becomes interactive no matter how long you wait). | **G25** | **⚠️ SECURITY — none of the export endpoints enforce RBAC.** `apps/api/src/modules/exports/exports.routes.ts` applies only `authMiddleware` to every route — `financial-pdf`, `financial-excel`, `member-statement/:memberId`, `members-excel`, `custom-members-excel`, `custom-report-excel`, `history`, `download/:id` all have zero `requirePermission` call. Confirmed live at D3.5: a `member@demo.com` token (no `exports.*` permission in `rbac-seed.ts`'s `member` array) successfully called `POST /exports/members-excel` and received a real completed export — the full member list (names, emails, balances) that only Admins/Treasurers/Financial Secretaries are supposed to be able to pull. The `exports.financial`/`exports.members`/`exports.view_history` permissions exist and are seeded correctly for officer roles — they're simply never checked against. **Not documented in the published docs** (would be irresponsible to publicize) — flagged here and directly to the owner instead | every `/api/exports/*` route; any member of any association today | Add `requirePermission`/`requireAnyPermission` checks matching the existing `exports.*` permission keys to every route in `exports.routes.ts`, same pattern already used in `reports.routes.ts` | **resolved 2026-10-15 (R1.2)** — all 8 routes gated on `exports.financial`/`exports.members`/`exports.view_history`; live-confirmed `member@demo.com` now gets `403 Permission denied` on `POST /exports/members-excel`. **Additional fix beyond the original finding**: `member` already held `exports.view_history` (not absent, as first described), which let any member list and download *every* export in the association via `/history`+`/download/:id`, including officer-generated financial/member-list dumps — `getExportHistory`/`downloadExport` now scope to the caller's own exports unless they also hold `exports.financial`/`exports.members`; live-confirmed an admin's export is invisible to a member in `/history` and returns `403` on direct `/download/:id` |
| G19 | `/dashboard/member/my-meetings` and `/dashboard/member/my-voting` are **static placeholder pages** — no `useEffect`, no fetch, no API call of any kind; "No upcoming meetings scheduled", "No attendance records yet", "No active elections", and every stat ("0") are hardcoded JSX, not conditionally rendered. Confirmed by reading both page components directly. Real meeting/election data exists and is reachable via the API (`/api/meetings/upcoming` returns a real scheduled meeting) — these two screens simply were never wired up to it. Found at D3.1; `meetings-and-attendance.mdx`/`voting.mdx` changed from `type: task` to `type: concept` and describe the intended behaviour with a "still rolling out" callout, since there are no real steps to perform yet | `/dashboard/member/my-meetings`, `/dashboard/member/my-voting` | Wire both pages to their real endpoints (`/api/meetings/upcoming`, `/api/voting/active`, plus an attendance-history and voting-history endpoint if those don't exist yet) | **resolved 2026-10-15 (R2.7)** — both endpoints already existed (`/meetings/attendance-history`, `/voting/elections/:id/voter-status`), no new backend work needed. Wiring this up surfaced that `membersApi.getMe()` — needed to know "my own memberId" — was broken for this exact account; fixed as G15 (see its own row). Live-verified both pages against a real member token after that fix |
| **G26** | **⚠️ SECURITY — meeting minutes and attendance have almost no real backend authorization; this is a default-role gap in every association, not demo-scoped.** `apps/api/src/lib/permissions.ts` maps `MEETING_VIEW`, `MEETING_ATTENDANCE` and `MEETING_MINUTES` to `members.view_own` — the baseline permission every authenticated user already has (`rbac-seed.ts`'s `member` array includes it by default, with no demo-only change involved). Confirmed live at D4.1 with a plain `member@demo.com` token: it successfully (1) created and self-approved official minutes for a real AGM via `POST /meetings/:id/minutes` and `POST /meetings/:id/minutes/approve`, and (2) initialized and marked attendance for a meeting via `POST /meetings/:id/attendance/initialize` and `POST /meetings/:id/attendance`. The web UI only ever shows these controls on the officer-facing `/dashboard/governance/meetings/[id]` page and performs no further role check — the backend is the only thing standing between a plain member and these actions, and right now it isn't. Supersedes G3 (which only flagged the API/web permission-constant mismatch, not that the API side is this permissive). Test data created during verification was deleted immediately after confirming, not left in the database. **Not disclosed in the published docs** — `minutes.mdx`/`taking-attendance.mdx` describe the intended Administrator/secretary workflow as normal, same precedent as G25 | `POST /meetings/:id/minutes`, `/minutes/approve`, `/attendance/initialize`, `/attendance`, `/attendance/bulk` — every association today | Give meetings its own real officer-scoped permissions (e.g. `meetings.manage_minutes`, `meetings.manage_attendance`) instead of reusing `members.view_own`, and update `rbac-seed.ts`'s `admin`/`treasurer`/`financial_secretary` arrays accordingly | **resolved 2026-10-15 (R1.3)** — added real `meetings.manage_minutes`/`meetings.manage_attendance` permissions to `rbac-seed.ts`, assigned to `admin`/`treasurer`/`financial_secretary`, and repointed the two constants in `permissions.ts` (route guards already called `requirePermission` on these constants — no route-level changes needed). `pnpm seed:rbac` re-run; live-confirmed `member@demo.com` now gets `403 Permission denied, requiredPermission: meetings.manage_minutes` on `POST /meetings/1/minutes` |
| G27 | **A meeting can never be edited, and never leaves "Scheduled," through the interface.** (1) No `/dashboard/governance/meetings/[id]/edit` route exists anywhere in `apps/web` — the **Edit** button on a scheduled meeting points at a URL that 404s; live-tested twice, once the click hung the page for several minutes, once it silently did nothing, both consistent with the same missing-route root cause. (2) `meetings.service.ts` never sets a meeting to `IN_PROGRESS`, and the only button that sets `COMPLETED` (**Complete Meeting**) only renders when status is already `IN_PROGRESS` — so neither state is ever reachable through the UI; a meeting stays **Scheduled** until cancelled. (3) Agenda items can only be set at creation time — the meeting detail page's Agenda panel is read-only with no add/edit/delete control, even though the backend's `/meetings/:id/agenda` endpoints work. Found at D4.1 while verifying `scheduling-a-meeting.mdx`/`building-an-agenda.mdx`/`cancelling-or-postponing.mdx`, which describe the real, narrower capability instead of inventing edit/in-progress/postpone steps that don't exist | `/dashboard/governance/meetings/[id]` (Edit button), meeting lifecycle generally, agenda editing | Build the missing edit page (or repurpose the create form for edit-in-place), add a UI trigger for `IN_PROGRESS`/`COMPLETED` transitions, and add agenda add/edit/delete controls to the detail page | **resolved 2026-10-15 (R2.4)** — built `meetings/[id]/edit/page.tsx` (SCHEDULED/POSTPONED only — `updateMeeting()` itself has no status guard, this is a UI-only restriction); added a **Start Meeting** button (SCHEDULED → IN_PROGRESS — the real missing trigger; **Complete Meeting** already existed and worked once reachable); added agenda Add/Edit/Delete controls to the detail page. **While building this, found a cross-tenant IDOR** in the backing service methods — see new gap G77, fixed for agenda, not yet for attendance. Live-verified full lifecycle (schedule → start → complete) and agenda CRUD with the ownership check |
| **G28** | **🔴 TOP PRIORITY — there is no UI path to ever start voting on an election, for any election, in any association.** `voting.service.ts`'s `closeNominations()` intentionally sets the election's status back to `DRAFT` (`// Back to draft until voting starts`, a real code comment confirming this is deliberate), and `startVoting()` correctly accepts a `DRAFT` election. But `elections/[id]/page.tsx`'s action buttons only render **Start Voting** when `status === 'NOMINATIONS_CLOSED'` — a status the real backend never actually produces — so a `DRAFT` election (post-nominations) only ever offers **Edit** / **Open Nominations** / **Cancel**, and reopening nominations leads back to `NOMINATIONS_OPEN`, which has no **Start Voting** button either. A genuine, confirmed dead end. Found and confirmed live (repeatedly) at D4.2; worked around for documentation purposes only via a direct `POST /elections/:id/start-voting` call — a real, valid, working endpoint with no reachable UI trigger | `/dashboard/governance/elections/[id]` action buttons; every election, every association | Either have `closeNominations()` set `NOMINATIONS_CLOSED` (matching the enum that already exists for exactly this purpose), or have the frontend also show **Start Voting** for a `DRAFT` election that has already had nominations opened once | **resolved 2026-10-15 (R2.1) — re-root-caused: `NOMINATIONS_CLOSED` doesn't exist anywhere in the schema or codebase, frontend or backend (confirmed by a whole-repo search); the proposed fix's first option was never possible.** Fixed via the second option — `elections/[id]/page.tsx` now shows **Start Voting** whenever `status === 'DRAFT'` (or `NOMINATIONS_OPEN`) and at least one candidate is `APPROVED`, matching `startVoting()`'s own real precondition. Live-verified full cycle: create → open nominations → self-nominate → approve → close nominations (→ `DRAFT`) → Start Voting → `VOTING_OPEN` → vote → close → publish → results, with no direct API call at any step |
| G29 | **No candidate-management screen exists anywhere in the app.** Zero files anywhere are named anything like "candidate" (confirmed by a whole-repo search). The **Add Candidate** link on an election's page points at `/elections/:id/candidates/add`, which 404s — live-tested twice with the same two outcomes as G27's Edit button (once hung, once silently no-op'd). `selfNominate`, `approveCandidate` and `withdrawCandidate` are all real, fully implemented endpoints with matching API client functions in `lib/api/voting.ts`, but **none of the three is ever called from any component in the entire web app** (confirmed by a whole-codebase grep for each name). The only way real candidates exist today — the seed data, and this session's own documentation screenshots — is a direct, valid admin API call. Added `bulkSyncWelfareContributions`/`bulkSyncWelfarePayouts`/`bulkSyncSavingsTransactions`/`bulkSyncLoans`/`bulkSyncLoanRepayments` (mirroring `bulkSyncTransactions`/`bulkSyncPayments` exactly) plus matching "Sync Now" buttons on the settings page. | G47 | **Accounting periods (open/close/lock) are completely unimplemented beyond the Prisma schema.** `AccountingPeriod` has zero references anywhere in `apps/api/src` outside the schema itself — no routes, no service functions, no frontend screen. Every journal entry can be dated into any period, closed or not, because nothing enforces a period's status. Found at D5.1 writing `accounting-periods.mdx`, which documents the concept plainly as unavailable rather than inventing steps | Accounting periods generally | Build the period open/close/lock workflow (routes, service, screen), and have journal entry creation check a period's status before accepting a backdated entry | **resolved 2026-10-17 (R5.2)** — new `accounting-periods.service.ts`/`.controller.ts`, `/api/accounting/periods*` routes (create/close/reopen/lock, Administrator-only via `FINANCE_APPROVE`), `/dashboard/accounting/periods` screen. Three real tiers: OPEN (free), CLOSED (reversible), LOCKED (permanent, no reopen — enforced server-side, not just UI). A new period's date range is rejected if it overlaps an existing one. Manual journal entry create/update (`accountingService.createJournalEntry`/`updateJournalEntry`) hard-blocks a CLOSED/LOCKED period date with a real 400; the auto-sync path (`createAndPostJournalEntry`) shares the identical check but — since every real-time call site already wraps the call in its own non-critical try/catch — a blocked period there just fails the sync and logs it, never blocking the underlying Finance/Welfare/Cooperatives action itself. Live-verified end to end: overlap rejection, close→lock→reopen-rejected, hard-block on a backdated manual entry, and normal posting outside the period all confirmed against the demo association (test period and test entry deleted afterward) |

`enablePartialPayments` rejected for real. **Payment**: `enabledChannels` rejects a disallowed method; `receiptAutoGenerate` gates a previously-orphaned `payment-receipt` email template that nothing had ever called. **Notification**: `paymentReminderDays`/`overdueReminderFrequency`/`emailEnabled`/`senderName` now drive R3.4's cron job per-association (it was one hardcoded global schedule); `senderName` wired into `sendEmail()` as a real display-name swap (Resend only requires the domain verified, not the display name). **Members & Access**: `defaultRole` (self-signup), `allowMultipleRoles` (role assignment retires other active roles when off — live-verified both branches), `inviteExpiryHours` (replaces the hardcoded `INVITE_TOKEN_EXPIRY_HOURS` constant), `memberIdPrefix` (all 4 real generation sites) all wired; `memberIdAutoGenerate` **not** enforceable — no manual-entry UI exists to fall back to, logged as G80. **Integrations**: `paystackEnabled`/`nombaEnabled` gate the member-facing dues-payment initialize endpoints (deliberately **not** subscription billing, which is between the association and the platform and stays always-available); `smsProvider: 'none'` blocks SMS sending for real at both call sites; `apiAccessEnabled` **not** enforceable — no API-key-based access mechanism exists anywhere in the codebase to gate, logged as G81. **Data & Privacy** (`dataRetentionMonths`, `anonymizeInactiveMembers`) deliberately deferred — these need new background jobs (purge/anonymization), not a wiring fix, logged as G83. `defaultPaymentMethod` also not wired to any payment-recording UI's pre-selected value — minor/cosmetic, logged as G82. All live-verified against the dev stack |
| G54 | **A plan "downgrade" is not actually deferred — it applies immediately, despite the API's own success message.** `subscription.controller.ts`'s `changePlan` returns `"Plan downgrade scheduled for [period end date]"` for a downgrade, but the handler updates `subscription.planId`/`billingCycle` in the very same request — there's no `pendingPlanId` or equivalent field anywhere on the `Subscription` model to defer it. Confirmed live: downgraded a real subscription from Enterprise to Starter, the success message said the change was scheduled for 14 days out, and a `GET /subscriptions/current` called immediately afterward already showed `planId` as Starter — visible in `billing-history-1.webp`, which shows the plan as "Starter" the same session this was tested. Low practical impact today only because of G13 (module access doesn't change with plan either way yet) — becomes a real billing-fairness issue the moment G13 is fixed, since a downgrade would then revoke paid-for access before the period it was paid for ends. Found at D5.3 | `POST /subscriptions/change-plan` (downgrade path) | Add a real deferred-change mechanism (a `pendingPlanId`/`pendingBillingCycle` applied by a cron job at `currentPeriodEnd`), or change the message to say the new plan applies immediately | **resolved 2026-10-17 (R4.1)** — added `pendingPlanId`/`pendingBillingCycle` to `Subscription`; downgrade now sets these instead of applying immediately; new `applyPendingPlanChanges` cron job (daily, 1:45 AM) applies them once `currentPeriodEnd` passes. Requesting the current plan again while a change is pending now cancels it instead of erroring (a real consequence of making deferral real — otherwise no way back). `getCurrentSubscription` surfaces `pendingPlan` for the UI; `/dashboard/subscription` shows a "switching to X on [date]" banner. Live-verified end to end: downgrade left `planId` unchanged with `pendingPlanId` set, cancel-pending-change cleared it, and simulating `currentPeriodEnd` passing correctly applied the swap |
| G55 | **`PAST_DUE` is a dead subscription status — nothing in the codebase ever sets it, so there's no real grace period.** `subscription-jobs.ts`'s `checkPastDueSubscriptions` job (runs every 6 hours) looks for subscriptions already in `PAST_DUE` status to expire them after 3 days — but grepping the entire API for `PAST_DUE` found only that one read; nothing anywhere ever transitions a subscription into it. `checkSubscriptionRenewal` instead jumps a subscription straight from `ACTIVE` to `EXPIRED` the moment `currentPeriodEnd` passes, with no past-due/grace window in between, despite the schema, the status enum and this cron job all being built for one. Found at D5.3 reading `subscription-jobs.ts` | Subscription renewal/expiry generally | Either have the renewal check set `PAST_DUE` first (with `checkPastDueSubscriptions` doing its intended job of expiring it after 3 days), or remove the unreachable status and job if no grace period is actually intended | **resolved 2026-10-17 (R4.2)** — `checkSubscriptionRenewal` now sets `PAST_DUE` (with a new `subscription-past-due` email) instead of jumping straight to `EXPIRED`; `checkPastDueSubscriptions` was already correctly built to expire `PAST_DUE` subscriptions after 3 more days, it just never had anything to find — now does, and sends the `subscription-expired` email it never sent before either. Live-verified the full state machine: `ACTIVE` → (period ends) → `PAST_DUE` → (3 more days) → `EXPIRED`, each transition checked directly against the DB |

| G67 | `RBACService`'s in-memory permission cache (`apps/api/src/services/rbac.service.ts`, 5-minute TTL) is only invalidated on `clearUserCache`/`clearAllCache`, both called when a user's role *assignment* changes — nothing invalidates it when a *role's own permission set* changes. Confirmed at D5.4: after fixing the G66 seed-ordering issue, `help.manage` showed correctly in `GET /rbac/roles` immediately, but `requirePermission('help.manage')` kept rejecting requests for several more minutes until the stale cache entry expired. Folded in from Known Issues at Track R's creation (2026-10-14) | Any role-permission edit or re-seed | Invalidate the relevant cache entries whenever a role's permission set changes, not only on assignment changes | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6.15) — re-verified live, stale for the live API.** `rbac.controller.ts`'s `updateRole` already calls `RBACService.clearUserCache` for every affected user (`UserRole.findMany` + a loop) when a role's permission set changes via the real endpoint; `assignRole`/`removeRole` do the same on assignment changes. The original symptom only happens when a seed script writes directly to the DB from a separate Node process — that process's in-memory cache can never know about it, an inherent limitation of this cache design (would need Redis + pub/sub to fix for real), not a missed invalidation call |

| G73 | **Minor, latent — restoring a deleted transaction bypasses accounting sync.** `finance.controller.ts`'s undo-delete paths (single restore ~line 1498, bulk restore ~line 1558) `prisma.transaction.create()` directly with zero call to `accountingIntegrationService`, unlike every other transaction-creation path fixed at R1.5. Deliberately left unfixed in that pass: the *original* transaction's journal entry (if any) isn't un-posted when the transaction is deleted, so naively syncing the restored row risks a double-post of the kind G48 already describes, and that needs its own real investigation (does delete already reverse the journal entry? should restore re-link to it instead of creating a new one?) rather than a copy-paste of the other fixes. Found at R1.5 (2026-10-15) while auditing every `prisma.transaction.create` call site | Transaction restore-from-audit-log (both single and bulk) | Investigate what happens to a transaction's journal entry on delete, then either re-link on restore or sync fresh with a dedupe check — don't just add the same hook call blindly | **investigated 2026-10-18 (R6, leftover cluster) — found bigger than scoped, deliberately not fixed here.** `deleteTransaction` never touches `JournalEntry` at all — it deletes the `Transaction`/`Payment` rows but leaves any already-posted journal entry completely orphaned and un-reversed, independent of whether the transaction is ever restored. This overstates the association's books for as long as the entry sits there. A real fix needs a reversal/void mechanism, not a quick hook call — deserves its own dedicated pass rather than a rushed addition to an already-large hygiene phase. Still open |
| G76 | **`apps/web`'s `lib/api/voting.ts` had never been reconciled against the real backend contract — systemic, not isolated typos.** Found and fixed at R2 (2026-10-15) while building R2.1–R2.3: `ElectionType` (`BY_ELECTION`/`REFERENDUM` vs real `EXECUTIVE`/`COMMITTEE`/`SPECIAL`) and `PollType` (`RANKED_CHOICE` vs real `RATING`) meant creating 2 of 4 election types and 1 of 4 poll types failed validation outright — confirmed live before the fix. `approveCandidate()` sent `{approved, rejectedReason}` where the real endpoint expects `{status, rejectionReason}` — it had never been callable correctly, confirming G29's "never called" finding had a second cause beyond just no UI. `castVote()`'s `isAbstain` (real: `isAbstention`) was masked by `Election.allowAbstain` (real: `allowAbstention`) always being `undefined`, so the Abstain button never rendered to expose the second bug. `ElectionPosition.minCandidates` doesn't exist on the backend at all; `displayOrder` is really `orderNumber`. `ElectionResults`/`VoterStatus` interfaces didn't match `getElectionResults()`/`getVoterStatus()`'s real shapes at all. `Poll.allowMultipleResponses`/`maxSelections` are really `allowMultipleVotes`/`maxChoices`; `showResultsBeforeEnd` was missing entirely. `addPollOption()` sent `{text, displayOrder}` where the real endpoint expects `{optionText, orderNumber}` | Election/poll creation, candidate approval, voting (abstain path), election results, positions | Match every type and function in `lib/api/voting.ts` to `voting.validation.ts`'s real Zod schemas and `schema.prisma`'s real enums | **resolved 2026-10-15** — all live-verified via direct API calls matching the corrected payload shapes (election creation with `EXECUTIVE` type, self-nominate, approve, full status-transition cycle, vote with `isAbstention`, voter-status, election-results all confirmed working end to end) |
| G77 | **⚠️ SECURITY — meeting agenda and attendance sub-resources had zero `associationId` scoping, a cross-tenant IDOR in the same class as G25/G26.** `addAgendaItem`/`updateAgendaItem`/`deleteAgendaItem`/`reorderAgendaItems` and `markAttendance`/`bulkMarkAttendance`/`getAttendanceStats`/`getMeetingAttendance` in `meetings.service.ts` all trusted a bare `meetingId`/`agendaId` from the URL with no check that it belonged to the caller's own association — any authenticated officer in any association could read/write another association's meeting sub-resources by guessing/iterating IDs. (`addAgendaItem`'s controller happened to check meeting ownership already; `updateAgendaItem`/`deleteAgendaItem`/`reorderAgendaItems` did not.) Found at R2.4 (2026-10-15) while building agenda editing UI | Agenda items (fixed) — `updateAgendaItem`, `deleteAgendaItem`, `reorderAgendaItems`, plus **attendance marking/stats, still open** (`markAttendance`, `bulkMarkAttendance`, `getAttendanceStats`, `getMeetingAttendance`) | Thread `associationId` through every one of these methods and verify the meeting belongs to it before any read/write, same pattern as `getMeetingById`/`updateMeeting` already use | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6, found via pre-build audit, not in R6's original list) — re-verified live before hardening: the attendance half was never actually exploitable.** Crafted a cross-tenant JWT for a real second association and called all 4 attendance routes (`mark`, `bulk mark`, `initialize`, `get`) against a meeting belonging to a different association — every one correctly 404'd, because the controller already calls `getMeetingById(associationId, meetingId)` before reaching any of these service methods. Hardened anyway for defense-in-depth consistency with the agenda fix: threaded `associationId` through `markAttendance`/`bulkMarkAttendance`/`getAttendanceStats`/`getMeetingAttendance`/`initializeAttendance` at the service layer itself, so a future caller that skips the controller can't reintroduce the gap |
| G78 | **`apps/web`'s `lib/api/sms.ts` had never been reconciled against the real backend contract — same systemic-drift class as G76.** Found and fixed at R3 (2026-10-15) while building R3.1–R3.3: `SMSDashboardStats` fields were entirely different (`sent`/`delivered`/`totalCredits`/`creditsUsedThisMonth` vs real `totalMessagesSent`/`totalMessagesDelivered`/`creditBalance`); `SMSCreditBalance` fields were entirely different (`totalCredits`/`creditsUsed`/`creditsRemaining`/`lastTopupAt` vs real `balance`/`totalPurchased`/`totalUsed`/`lastTopUpAt` — the dashboard's own "Top Up Credits" button pointed at `/dashboard/communications/reports`, not a real purchase flow, and the reports page's own "Top Up" form posted to `/credits/topup`, the endpoint deleted in R1.4/G41); `SMSTemplate.message`/`.variables` should be `.content`/`.placeholders`; `SMSCampaign.recipientCount`/`.scheduledFor` should be `.totalRecipients`/`.scheduledAt`; `SMSMessage.recipient` should be `.recipientPhone`, and `.campaignId`/`.templateId` don't exist (only a nested `.campaign` object); every status/type enum was lowercase where the real ones are uppercase. Also found: `TemplateEditor`'s own variable-insert button used single-brace `{var}` while its own extraction regex (and the real backend's `applyMessageTemplate()`) both use double-brace `{{var}}` — inserting a "quick variable" produced text the backend would never substitute | Every page under `communications/*`, plus `SMSBalanceCard`/`CampaignStatusBadge`/`MessageDetailModal`/`SMSComposer`/`TemplateEditor` components | Match every type and function in `lib/api/sms.ts` to `sms.types.ts`/`sms.validation.ts`/`schema.prisma`'s real shapes and enums | **resolved 2026-10-15** — all consuming pages and components fixed in the same pass; live-verified via direct API calls (dashboard stats, template creation, campaign creation with a real `recipientFilter`) |
| G74 | **Minor, hygiene only — `apps/api`'s test suite is non-functional.** `pnpm -F @societyledger/api test` fails on every test file with `TS2582`/`TS2304` ("Cannot find name 'it'/'expect'/'describe'") — `@types/jest` is listed in `package.json` but isn't actually present in `node_modules/@types` for this package. Confirmed pre-existing, unrelated to any R1 change (the failing files — `__tests__/multi-tenancy/subdomain.test.ts` and one other — don't touch anything R1 edited). Same tooling-hygiene class as G60 (`apps/web` has no ESLint config) and G69 (now fixed) — and while fixing G69, `pnpm -F @societyledger/api lint` was also tried and found to have **no ESLint config at all** either, the same gap as G60 but for this package. Found at R1 (2026-10-15) while running the Remediation DoD's "tests pass" check | `apps/api`'s test and lint tooling generally | Reinstall/hoist `@types/jest` correctly for a working `test` script; write an `.eslintrc` for `apps/api` same as G60 proposes for `apps/web` | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6.13) — real root cause was different from the original diagnosis.** `@types/jest` was never actually missing (`require.resolve` found it fine in `apps/api/node_modules/@types/jest`) — the real cause is `tsconfig.base.json`'s `typeRoots: ["./node_modules/@types"]`, which resolves relative to *that file's own location* (the monorepo root), so it only ever sees root-hoisted `@types/*` packages, never a package's own local ones. Fixed via a new `apps/api/tsconfig.test.json` (widens `include` to cover `__tests__/` too, which the main `tsconfig.json` never did either, and overrides `typeRoots` to include both paths) wired into `jest.config.ts`. Once tests could actually run, they surfaced real, separate content staleness (stale Prisma field names — `password` should be `passwordHash`, `membershipNumber` should be `memberId` — and a stale `X-Subdomain` header the current tenant middleware doesn't read) — fixed those too so the suite type-checks and executes, but several assertions still fail against today's actual API behavior; a full rewrite of these 2 integration test files is real, separate work, not tooling hygiene — see G91. `.eslintrc.json` written for `apps/api` too (`@typescript-eslint` added as a devDependency) — runs non-interactively (103 pre-existing errors/warnings found, same "separate debt" treatment as G60) |
| G79 | **Latent, pre-existing — a subscription can be re-trialed for free by switching plans after expiry.** `initializePayment`'s "does a subscription already exist" check (and now R4.3's `startTrial`, deliberately matching it) only looks for `status: {in: ['ACTIVE','TRIALING','PAST_DUE']}` — an `EXPIRED`/`CANCELLED` association can start a brand-new 14-day `TRIALING` subscription by picking a **different** plan than the one that expired (picking the *same* plan hits the `@@unique([associationId, planId])` constraint and fails, which is what currently masks this for anyone who'd naturally re-select their old plan). Not introduced this session — found at R4.1 while designing G54's `pendingPlanId` fix and re-reading `initializePayment` closely. Not fixed: real fix is a product decision (one trial ever per association, tracked some other way than per-plan `Subscription` rows) | `POST /subscriptions/initialize`, `POST /subscriptions/start-trial` | Track "has this association ever had a trial" independently of which `Subscription` row/plan it was against (e.g. a boolean on `Association`), and check that instead of scoping to currently-active statuses | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6, leftover cluster)** — added `Association.hasEverTrialed`; both `initializePayment` and `startTrial` now check it in addition to the active-status scoping, and set it the first time a real trial starts |
| G80 | **Members & Access Settings' `memberIdAutoGenerate` toggle can't be enforced — there's no manual-entry alternative.** All 4 real member-ID-generation call sites (`members.controller.ts` ×2, `member-invites.service.ts`, `member-finance.controller.ts`) always auto-generate; `createMemberSchema` doesn't even accept a `memberId` field in the request body, and no frontend member-create form has an ID input. Turning this setting off today would have to either silently do nothing or break member creation outright. Found at R4.6.4 (2026-10-17) while wiring `memberIdPrefix` (which **was** wired — this is the sibling field) | `/dashboard/settings/members-access`, member creation generally | Add a manual Member ID input to the create-member form (shown only when `memberIdAutoGenerate` is off) and accept it in `createMemberSchema`, validating uniqueness within the association | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6, leftover cluster)** — `createMemberSchema` now accepts an optional `memberId`, enforced (required + unique within the association) only when `memberIdAutoGenerate` is off; the create-member form shows the field conditionally. `memberId` deliberately excluded from the update schema's actual DB write — it's create-only. Live-verified: blocked with no ID when the setting is off, rejected a real duplicate, accepted a real unique one |
| G81 | **Integrations Settings' `apiAccessEnabled` toggle has nothing to enforce — no API-key-based access mechanism exists anywhere in the codebase.** Confirmed by grep: the only `apiKey`-shaped fields anywhere in `apps/api/src` belong to Paystack/Nomba's own provider credentials, not a SocietyLedger-issued key, and no middleware checks any kind of API key for external access. Found at R4.6.6 (2026-10-17) while wiring the rest of the Integrations category (`paystackEnabled`/`nombaEnabled`/`smsProvider`, all real) | `/dashboard/settings/integrations` | Either build a real API-key issuance + auth-middleware system and gate it on this flag, or remove the toggle until one exists | **declined/deferred 2026-10-18 (R6)** — confirmed again, still no API-key mechanism anywhere to gate. This is a real new feature (issuance + auth middleware), not R6-scoped hygiene — left open for a dedicated pass, same reasoning as originally scoped at R4.6.6 |
| G82 | **Minor, cosmetic — Payment Settings' `defaultPaymentMethod` is saved but never read by any payment-recording UI.** Officers and members still have to manually pick a payment method on every payment every time; the configured default never pre-selects anything. Lower priority than the other Payment Settings fields fixed at R4.6.2 (`enabledChannels`/`receiptAutoGenerate`) since it's a convenience, not a correctness or security gap. Found at R4.6.2 (2026-10-17) | Payment-recording forms in `apps/web` | Read `payments.defaultPaymentMethod` when rendering a payment-method selector and pre-select it | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6, leftover cluster)** — both the bulk-pay and single-transaction payment forms on `/dashboard/finance/transactions` now fetch and pre-select the configured default |
| G83 | **Data & Privacy Settings (`dataRetentionMonths`, `anonymizeInactiveMembers`) deliberately left unenforced — real enforcement needs new background jobs, not a wiring fix.** A real data-retention purge job and an inactive-member anonymization job are both new scheduled-job features with their own real-deletion/anonymization semantics that deserve a dedicated design pass (what counts as "inactive," what "anonymize" actually touches, irreversibility safeguards) rather than being bolted onto R4.6's existing-code-path-only scope. Owner-scoped out of R4.6 explicitly (see Decisions log). `gdprCompliant` is a declarative flag with no obvious enforcement action regardless. Found/scoped at R4.6.5 (2026-10-17) | `/dashboard/settings/data-privacy` | Design and build the two background jobs as their own phase/pass, including a dry-run/preview mode given the irreversibility of both actions | **declined/deferred 2026-10-18 (R6)** — still real new feature work (two background jobs with irreversible-deletion semantics), not R6-scoped hygiene — re-confirmed the original R4.6.5 scoping decision stands |
| G84 | **`apps/web/src/types/subscription.ts`'s `Subscription` interface has never been reconciled against the real `Subscription` Prisma model — same systemic-drift class as G76/G78.** `cancelledAt`, `trialStart`, `paystackCustomerCode`, `paystackSubscriptionCode` don't exist on the real model (real fields are `trialEndsAt` only, no cancelled-at/customer-code tracking at all); conversely the real `renewalDate`/`pendingPlanId`/`pendingBillingCycle` (the latter two new at R4.1) weren't in the frontend type until this session added only the two it needed. Found at R4.1 (2026-10-17) while adding `pendingPlanId`/`pendingBillingCycle` to this same file — not fully fixed, only the fields R4.1 needed were added, the pre-existing drift in the other 4 fields was left as found (same triage bar as G58, a known-adjacent drift noticed but out of this phase's actual scope) | Any `apps/web` code reading `Subscription`/`SubscriptionWithPlan` | Full field-by-field reconciliation against `schema.prisma`'s real `Subscription` model, same methodology as G76/G78 | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6, leftover cluster)** — removed `cancelledAt`/`trialStart`/`paystackCustomerCode`/`paystackSubscriptionCode` (none exist on the real model), renamed `trialEnd`→`trialEndsAt`, added `renewalDate`. Found a real masked bug along the way: `/dashboard/subscription/page.tsx` read `subscription.trialEnd` (always `undefined`, wrong field name) — never visibly wrong because the fallback (`currentPeriodEnd`) happened to equal the real trial-end date during an actual trial. Fixed the usage site too |
| G85 | **`member-finance.controller.ts`'s `paymentMethod` validator (G58) blocks real settings enforcement from being extendable to member self-service payments.** R4.6.2 wired `payments.enabledChannels` into `finance.controller.ts`'s officer-recorded `recordPayment` (real lowercase 6-value set, matches the setting), but couldn't extend the same check to `member-finance.controller.ts`'s `processPayment` because that validator still uses G58's wrong uppercase 5-value set (`BANK_TRANSFER`/`CASH`/`CARD`/`POS`/`MOBILE_MONEY`) — comparing it against `enabledChannels`' lowercase values would never match anything. Found at R4.6.2 (2026-10-17) confirming G58 is still open and now directly blocking something else | Member self-service payments (`member-finance.controller.ts`) | Fix G58 first (normalize to the same six lowercase values everywhere), then extend R4.6.2's `enabledChannels` check to `processPayment` the same way it was added to `recordPayment` | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6.11)** — G58 fixed first in the same pass, then `processPayment` got the identical `enabledChannels` rejection `recordPayment` already had |
| G86 | **A plan "upgrade" via `changePlan` never actually applied the new plan — pre-existing, found while building R4.1's downgrade-deferral fix.** `changePlan`'s upgrade branch sends `{type: 'plan_change', newPlanId, billingCycle}` as Paystack metadata, but `verifyPaystackPayment`/`verifyNombaPayment` never read it back — the subscription update there only ever extended `currentPeriodStart`/`currentPeriodEnd`, never touched `planId`/`billingCycle`. A user who "upgraded" and paid stayed on their old plan, charged the prorated amount for nothing. Compounded by a second defect introduced in the same R4.1 work and caught by `/review` before being shipped as "resolved": the new `pendingPlanId`/`pendingBillingCycle` fields were never cleared by a completed upgrade either, so a stale pending downgrade could later silently overwrite a plan the association just paid to upgrade to. Found at R4.1 (2026-10-17) | `POST /subscriptions/change-plan` (upgrade path), `GET /subscriptions/verify/:reference` | Read the echoed-back `plan_change` metadata in both verify handlers and apply `newPlanId`/`billingCycle` for real; clear any pending deferred change at the same time (but only for an actual plan-change confirmation — a plain renewal must leave a pending downgrade untouched) | **resolved 2026-10-17 (R4.1 review follow-up)** — both verify handlers now read the metadata, apply the real plan/cycle on a `plan_change` confirmation, and clear `pendingPlanId`/`pendingBillingCycle` only in that case. Live-verified via direct simulation of both branches: a plain renewal left a pending downgrade untouched (`planId` stayed 3, `pendingPlanId` stayed 1); a plan-change confirmation applied the new plan for real and cleared the pending field (`planId` → 2, `pendingPlanId` → null) |
| G87 | **Minor — two settings fields with the identical name (`enablePartialPayments`) exist in both the Financial and Payment Settings categories; only the Financial one is wired.** G53's original text only ever listed `enablePartialPayments` under Financial Settings, and that's the one R4.6.1 wired into `recordPayment`. `payments.enablePartialPayments` remains exactly as unenforced as before — not a missed instruction, but a duplicate the original gap's own text never accounted for. Found at `/review` of R4.6 (2026-10-17) | Payment Settings page, `payments.enablePartialPayments` | Decide whether the duplicate is intentional (two independently-toggleable contexts) or a copy-paste leftover that should be removed from one category | **resolved 2026-10-18 (R6, leftover cluster)** — treated as intentionally independent rather than a copy-paste leftover: `recordPayment` now rejects a partial payment if *either* the Financial or Payment Settings toggle is off |
| G88 | **Minor — the two new settings-gated SMS rejections (R4.6.6) returned HTTP 500 instead of 400.** `sms.service.ts`'s `sendQuickSMS`/`startCampaign` throw a plain `Error` on `smsProvider: 'none'`, which `sms.controller.ts`'s pre-existing generic catch-all (shared with "insufficient credits" and every other business-rule rejection in that function) turns into a 500 with a `console.error`. Found at `/review` of R4.6 (2026-10-17) | `POST /sms/send`, `POST /sms/campaigns/:id/start` | **resolved 2026-10-17** — both HTTP controller methods now check `smsProvider` directly and return a real 400 before ever calling the service, same pattern already used for the Paystack/Nomba checks; the service-layer check is left in place as a backstop for the non-HTTP `dispatchNotification` SMS-channel caller, which already handles a thrown error gracefully. The underlying pre-existing pattern (every other thrown business-rule error in this function, e.g. "insufficient credits", still becomes a 500) was left alone — out of scope, affects code this session didn't touch |
| G89 | **`logSyncFailure`'s own audit-log write has silently failed on every single call, ever.** It hardcodes `userId: 0` on the `AuditLog` row it writes, but `AuditLog.userId` is a required foreign key to `User` and no user with id `0` exists — every call throws `P2003` inside `logSyncFailure`'s own inner try/catch, which swallows it with a `console.error` only. Net effect: the "Recent Sync Errors" panel on `/dashboard/settings/accounting-integration` has never shown a single real failure, for any association, ever — not a regression, a bug present since the feature's original build (predates D5.1). Also compounded by a second, separate pre-existing defect found alongside it: every `onX...` sync hook (`onTransactionCreated`, `onPaymentRecorded`, and the 6 Welfare/Cooperatives equivalents) swallowed its own internal error instead of rethrowing, so `bulkSyncTransactions`/`bulkSyncPayments` (and this session's new welfare/cooperative bulk-sync endpoints) always reported "0 failed" even when every single record silently failed — reproduced live: bulk-syncing 100 genuinely-unsynced transactions reported "Synced 100, 0 failed" while the real synced count didn't move at all. Found 2026-10-17 while live-verifying R5.1/R5.2 (a deliberately-created, deliberately-locked test period caused real sync failures that the UI and error panel both silently hid) | `/dashboard/settings/accounting-integration` entirely — its sync-rate numbers, "Sync Now" results, and error panel were all untrustworthy on a failure | Pass the real `userId` through to `logSyncFailure`; make each `onX...` hook rethrow instead of swallow (every real-time call site already wraps the call in its own non-critical try/catch, so this is safe) | **resolved 2026-10-17** — `logSyncFailure` now takes and uses the real `userId`; all 8 `onX...` hooks now rethrow. Live-verified: a real sync failure now produces a real, readable `AuditLog` row, and a bulk-sync call now correctly reports "0 synced, 100 failed" instead of a false "100 synced, 0 failed" |
| G90 | **`generateEntryNumber` uses a row count, not the highest existing number — any deleted journal entry permanently breaks future numbering for that association/year.** It computes the next entry number as `count(existing entries this year) + 1`; deleting any entry (a void/cleanup, or any gap for any reason) drops the count without removing the highest number already issued, so the next "generated" number collides with one that still exists, throwing a Postgres unique-constraint error on every subsequent attempt to create a journal entry for that association that year. Found 2026-10-17 — triggered by deleting a test journal entry while verifying R5.2, which then blocked every subsequent sync for the demo association until root-caused | All journal entry creation (manual and auto-sync), for any association where an entry is ever deleted | Base the next number on the highest existing suffix for the year, not a count | **resolved 2026-10-17** — `generateEntryNumber` now reads the highest existing `entryNumber` for the association/year and increments its numeric suffix, instead of counting rows. Live-verified: recreated the exact collision, confirmed the fix resolves it, and all 5 welfare/cooperative record types reached 100% sync rate afterward with no further numbering conflicts |
| G91 | **`apps/api`'s two multi-tenancy integration test files (`__tests__/multi-tenancy/{subdomain,isolation}.test.ts`) have drifted badly from the current API — not just the `@types/jest` tooling issue G74 fixed.** Once that tooling fix let the suite actually compile and run, it surfaced real content staleness on at least 3 independent dimensions: stale Prisma field names (`User.password` → real `passwordHash`; `Member.membershipNumber` → real `memberId`; `Member.status: 'ACTIVE'` → real lowercase `'active'`; `User` also never had `firstName`/`lastName` fields at all — those live on `Member`), a stale tenant-resolution header (`X-Subdomain`, which the current `tenant.middleware.ts` never reads — only `x-tenant`/`x-tenant-id`), and at least one outright route/behavior change (`GET /associations/check-subdomain` and the public signup endpoint no longer return the status codes the tests expect). Found 2026-10-18 while fixing G74 | `apps/api`'s only 2 integration test files | A full rewrite against the current API — tenant resolution, schema, and whatever else surfaces — not a field-rename patch | open — the field-name/header issues found so far were fixed in the same pass as G74, but the suite still doesn't fully pass; deliberately not pursued further here, this is real test-authoring work, not tooling hygiene. **Update 2026-10-19 (F0.2):** found two more, earlier-stage blockers in the same two files, both load-time (fail before any test body runs, so G91's content staleness is still unreached). (1) `packages/shared` had never actually been built (no `dist/`) — `apps/api`'s own `tsx src/server.ts` failed to even boot with the same `Cannot find module '@societyledger/shared/dist/index.js'` error, not just these tests, so this is a real pre-existing environment gap, not test-only; fixed by running `pnpm -F @societyledger/shared build` (safe, no source change, just the missing compile step — should probably be a `turbo.json` `build` dependency so it's never missing again, not done here, out of scope). (2) Once that's fixed, both suites still fail to load — `otplib`'s `@otplib/plugin-base32-scure` subdependency (pulled in transitively via `server.ts` → `admin-auth.routes.ts` → `admin-totp.controller.ts` → `totp.service.ts`, added at A1.4) ships ESM-only source Jest's default config can't transform from `node_modules`. Confirmed via `git stash` that both these blockers, and G91's original content-staleness issues, all predate this session — F0.2 touches neither `packages/shared` nor `otplib`/TOTP, just fixed the former in passing and surfaced the latter. Fixing the Jest/ESM transform config was not attempted here — real tooling work, same character as G91 itself, left for whoever eventually does the full rewrite. |
| G92 | **Two parallel officer payment-proof-verification pages existed (`/dashboard/payments/verification` and `/dashboard/payments/nigerian/verification`), both broken against the real backend and both unreachable from any navigation in the app.** Both called `verifyPaymentProof` with a stale `{verified: boolean}` payload instead of the real `{status: 'APPROVED'\|'REJECTED'}`; both unwrapped the list response incorrectly (expected `response.data`/`response.total` instead of the real `response.proofs`/`response.pagination.totalCount`); both checked a non-existent status value `'VERIFIED'` instead of the real `'APPROVED'`; both rendered non-existent fields `fileName`/`fileUrl` instead of the real `filename`/`filepath`. Neither page was linked from `Sidebar.tsx` or anywhere else — confirmed via repo-wide grep. Found 2026-10-18 while building R7.2 (G17), whose member-side proof-upload flow is only complete end-to-end if an officer can actually review and approve what gets uploaded | `/dashboard/payments/verification`, `/dashboard/payments/nigerian/verification` | Fix the richer of the two (`/dashboard/payments/verification` — has a Proofs+Bank Transfers tabbed UI) and link it from the Sidebar; leave the simpler duplicate (`/dashboard/payments/nigerian/verification`) fixed for correctness but still unlinked, flagged here as a known orphaned duplicate for the owner to retire or merge | **resolved 2026-10-18** — fixed both pages' call shape, response unwrapping, status values, and field names (verified via a clean `apps/web` `tsc --noEmit`); added a "Payment Verification" entry to the Sidebar's Finance section (gated on `PAYMENT_CONFIRM`) pointing at `/dashboard/payments/verification`. `/dashboard/payments/nigerian/verification` remains fixed-but-unlinked; owner should decide whether to retire it |
| G93 | **`POST /nigerian-payments/proofs` (member proof upload) was gated by officer-level `PAYMENT_CREATE`/`PAYMENT_VIEW` only — the real `member` role has neither, only `payments.view_own`, making the endpoint unreachable by any actual member despite its own code comment calling it a "member action".** Found 2026-10-18 while live-verifying R7.2 (G17) with a crafted member JWT | `POST /api/nigerian-payments/proofs` | Add `PERMISSIONS.PAYMENTS_VIEW_OWN` to the route's `requireAnyPermission` gate | **resolved 2026-10-18** — route now accepts `PAYMENT_CREATE`, `PAYMENT_VIEW`, or `PAYMENTS_VIEW_OWN`; live-verified a member-role JWT can now reach the endpoint |
| G94 | **`apps/web/src/lib/permissions.ts`'s `MEETING_MINUTES`/`MEETING_ATTENDANCE` aliases are stale — `apps/api/src/lib/permissions.ts` was updated during R1.3/G26 to the real, dedicated, seeded keys (`meetings.manage_minutes`/`meetings.manage_attendance`, confirmed enforced server-side by `meetings.routes.ts`'s `requirePermission(PERMISSIONS.MEETING_*)` calls), but web's own copy was never updated and still has the old `members.edit`/`members.view_own` values that project-overview.md §6.9's drift note describes. This means web's UI gates the Minutes/Attendance buttons on the wrong permission — a user with `meetings.manage_minutes` but not `members.edit` would see the button hidden (or the reverse: `members.edit` without `meetings.manage_minutes` shows a button that then 403s).** Found 2026-10-20 while building mobile's F1.6 `lib/permissions.ts` (copying the API's real alias map surfaced the live divergence from web's copy) | `apps/web/src/lib/permissions.ts`'s `MEETING_MINUTES`/`MEETING_ATTENDANCE` entries; any web screen gating the Minutes/Attendance buttons on them (e.g. meeting detail) | Update both values in `apps/web/src/lib/permissions.ts` to match the API's current real keys | open — out of mobile's scope to fix (architecture.md rule #14: web changes limited to §9.5/§15.3/§16.8); mobile's own `lib/permissions.ts` uses the correct, current, server-enforced values directly from the API source, so mobile itself isn't affected |

## Decisions log
| Date | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-15 | **R0.1 (G13):** build the frontend's `MODULE_DISABLED`/`FEATURE_DISABLED` locked/upgrade state first, then flip `FEATURE_FLAG_MODE=enforce` | Enforcement code is already fully built (`module-feature.middleware.ts`); flipping first risks a tenant's screen breaking on a raw, unhandled 403 the moment it ships |
| 2026-10-15 | **R0.2 (G16):** design real `_own`-scoped permissions (`voting.vote_own`, `welfare.view_own`, `cooperatives.view_own`) for the member role template, matching the existing `ledger.view_own`/`payments.view_own`/`members.view_own` pattern | Closes a real over-permissioning gap rather than leaving every member on the same broad key that also grants officer-level visibility into every other member's records |
| 2026-10-15 | **R0.3 (G17):** gate the self-declare payment flow with a two-step proof-upload + officer-approval model — member uploads a screenshot/bank receipt, transaction stays `pending` until an officer confirms it; officer-recorded payments are unaffected. Reuses the Nigerian-payments module's existing `multer`/`proofs`/`verify` infrastructure | Closes the financial-integrity gap now rather than waiting on mobile's F2.8/F2.9, without building throwaway web-only Paystack/upload plumbing mobile will supersede |
| 2026-10-15 | **R0.4 (G53):** wire real enforcement for all 6 behavioral settings categories, as its own block (**R4.6**, after R4's existing 5 items) rather than folded into R4 | Scope is substantially larger than R4 currently budgets; a dedicated block keeps it trackable without inflating R4's existing DoD |
| 2026-10-15 | **R0.5 (G38):** rewrite the linked `/dashboard/communications/*` SMS pages to match the real API (porting in the orphaned set's working Paystack top-up, fixing G39/G40 in the same pass), then delete the orphaned `/dashboard/communication/sms/*` set | Same total engineering effort as deleting-then-rebuilding, but ends with one correct implementation and zero duplicate code instead of two still-diverging ones |

| 2026-10-11 | Fixed pre-existing `apps/web` build break found while verifying D1.1: `src/app/dashboard/member/settings/page.tsx` was the **only** file in `apps/web` using `import React, { useState } from 'react'` (a default + named import); every other file imports `{ useState }` only. That default `React` binding made `React.ReactNode` resolve structurally differently from the `ReactNode` Mantine 7's compiled `.d.ts` files reference, so every Mantine component in that file's JSX tree failed `TS2786`. Fixed by switching to `import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'` (matching every other file) and removing `useEffect`. Also removed `"ignoreDeprecations": "6.0"` from `apps/web/tsconfig.json` (current TypeScript rejects that value; a second, independently-broken line masked behind the first). Several false leads ruled out along the way, worth not re-trying: pinning `typescript` down to 5.4.5 (no effect — error is independent of the TS minor version), pinning `@types/react`/`@types/react-dom` down to 18.2.x (no effect — `Key = string \| number \| bigint` has been in `@types/react` since at least 18.2.79, nothing to do with a recent bump) | Caught by the D1.1 DoD's "web still passes" check; file was apparently never type-checked after being written (a `next build`/`tsc` run was not part of the original workflow) |

Revisit if something ever behaves differently on the real Dokploy staging URL |

Expect this pattern to repeat for other W+M pages until D7 |

| 2026-10-20 | **Mobile Phase 1 retro review** (`/review`, before starting Phase 2): audited all of F1.1–F1.8 against build-plan.md/architecture.md/ui-rules.md/ui-tokens.md/code-standards.md in three parallel passes (scaffold+theme+types; UI kit+forms+feedback; lib+stores+api+navigation). Zero layer-boundary violations, zero hardcoded-hex/raw-Tailwind-class violations, zero doc-vs-code drift found across ~28 registered components. Found and fixed 6 issues, all same-session: (1) **Important** — `BottomSheet`'s close (X) button only fired the optional `onDismiss` callback, never actually called `.dismiss()` on the sheet — pressing it did nothing in `SelectSheet`/`FileAttachField` (neither passes `onDismiss`) and didn't close `ConfirmSheet` either; fixed with an internal ref the button calls directly, forwarded to callers via `useImperativeHandle` so existing `ref.current?.dismiss()` call sites are unaffected. (2) `FileAttachField`'s camera/photos/files pickers had no try/catch and silently no-op'd on a denied permission — now surface through the field's existing helper-text slot. (3) `Checkbox`/`Switch` row tap targets were under the 44dp minimum — `Checkbox` got `hitSlop={8}` (IconButton's own convention), `Switch`'s whole row is now pressable (RN's touch responder gives the inner native `Switch` exclusive capture of direct taps, so this doesn't double-toggle). (4) `DateField`/`SelectSheet`/`FileAttachField`'s trigger `Pressable`s got explicit `accessibilityRole="button"` + `accessibilityLabel`.
