| 2026-10-27 | **`Plan.audience` (ASSOCIATION or THRIFT_GROUP) and the base plan `thrift-group`** (core modules only, no price). A standalone group is a real tenant with a real subscription on that plan plus an Ajo tier; its trial includes a complimentary Ajo Starter add-on | A public no-price plan would otherwise appear on every association's pricing page. The only branches on tenant kind are sign-up, the entitlements limit and the plan audience. |
| 2026-10-27 | **Ajo payout accounts are encrypted at rest with a key derived (HKDF) from the existing `TOTP_ENCRYPTION_KEY`**, bound to the member so a copied ciphertext will not decrypt; a full number is produced only by an audited `reveal` | Owner chose encryption at rest. No second secret to manage; the two uses stay cryptographically separate. Rotating `TOTP_ENCRYPTION_KEY` would make stored accounts unreadable, so it needs a re-encrypt step first (a person can simply re-enter theirs). |
| 2026-10-27 | **Payout cool-off is the platform setting `ajoPayoutCooloffHours` (default 72, 0 to 336), applied to the first entry as well as every change.** It blocks approving or recording a payout, not contributing or joining | Owner confirmed. A fake account is set up the same way as a redirected one. |
| 2026-10-27 | **Phone code:** 6 digits, 10 minutes, 5 wrong tries, one resend a minute, 5 codes a day per person, one SMS credit from the association; hashed and bound to the phone it was sent to; no credits means no code and no bypass | Owner confirmed the limits. The binding to the number stops a confirmation landing on a different number than the one texted. |
| 2026-10-27 | **Verification is decided by SUPER_ADMIN and COMPLIANCE_ADMIN; SUPPORT_AGENT can only view.** Reviewers decide on what is on file now, a rejection needs a reason of 10+ characters shown to the group admin, and a payout-account change is the only thing that resets a verified person | Owner confirmed. |
| 2026-10-27 | **Built beyond the plan (small):** viewing a full account number is itself audited; nobody can approve their own membership; the verification queue can be filtered by association; `RuleError` allows 429 | Safety and usability follow-ons from the same decisions. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Rule changes bind a member only once they accept the new version.** A new version takes effect at least 7 days after publishing; the version in force for a member is the latest one they accepted; not accepting never blocks contributing | Owner confirmed. A fee can never apply to someone who never saw it, and T6/T8 can defend any fee by pointing at the acceptance. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Acceptance and guarantor consent can be recorded by the member (app) or by an officer on their behalf (officer, with a required note).** A guarantor is officer-recorded only. An officer cannot record an acceptance for themselves | Owner confirmed. Many contributors have no login. The note, the audit entry and the visible channel limit abuse, but software cannot verify a verbal or paper agreement. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Invites stay email-only; no SMS invite.** A contributor with no email is recorded by an officer and invited once they have one | Owner decision: someone serious about joining can open an email. The existing member-invite service is unchanged. |
| 2026-10-28 | **A rotating pot is frozen when it starts; a collector-thrift group takes late joiners** (per-group switch, default on). A late joiner owes only from the day they joined, and the cycle ends on the same day for everyone | Owner confirmed. Rounds equal shares, so adding people after the start would break the order and the round dates. |
| 2026-10-28 | **A rotating-pot participant can hold several shares** (limit per group, default 2, set by the admin, fixed at start). Each share is a payout slot and a person pays shares × the contribution in every round; the plan quota still counts people | Owner addition. Needed a new `AjoPayoutSlot` table and a `shares` column; the single `payoutPosition` was dropped. |
| 2026-10-28 | **The draw is an admin action and cannot be re-rolled** unless the roster or shares changed since the last draw. Swaps need admin approval and are only possible before the first paid payout; after it, only an admin's emergency exception (reason of 10+ characters) that moves unpaid slots | Owner confirmed. Keeps the draw trustworthy and paid positions immutable. |
| 2026-10-28 | **The group's own bank account is encrypted at rest and shown in full only to its members and officers**; masked in lists, audit entries and notifications; changing it notifies every member (no cool-off) | Owner confirmed. Contributors need the number to pay, so it cannot be masked for them, but it should not sit in plain text. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Built beyond the plan (small):** the admin cannot judge a swap that involves their own slot; a person who left can be re-added; cancelling is refused while confirmed money is on the books; the group's bank account is required to open (not just to start) | Safety and usability follow-ons from the same decisions. |
| 2026-10-28 | **A payment is split across instalments at confirmation, oldest first, and the split is stored** (`AjoContributionAllocation`). Nobody picks an instalment; a reversal restores exactly that split; a payment above everything still owed is refused | Owner confirmed. Arrears are cleared first, nobody can bury an old instalment by paying newer ones, and a reversal cannot land on different instalments than the original. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Cash is reconciled line by line.** The officer ticks the payments received and states the count; the two must agree. Unticked payments stay "cash received", flagged missing, never counted, carried to the collector's next handover or rejected with a reason | Owner confirmed. One missing ₦2,000.00 neither confirms the whole day wrongly nor blocks twelve people's payments, and the contributor is not penalised (their receipt says it is awaiting confirmation). |
| 2026-10-28 | **Transfers: duplicates are flagged when reported and enforced when confirming**; amounts are never edited (a wrong report is rejected with a reason); the recorder cannot confirm their own entry unless they are the only reconciler, with a note | Owner confirmed. Honest people re-upload things, so a flag is not a block; but one bank payment cannot pay twice. Mirrors the single-admin rule for payouts. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Receipts are one per moment, by channel.** Login: in-app, push, email by preference. No login: SMS when recorded and when something goes wrong, not for routine confirmation. No credits means queued (never dropped), officers told once, sent when credits arrive | Owner confirmed. Halves the SMS spent on a daily-thrift group and stops an empty balance from losing receipts. |
| 2026-10-28 | **"Expected" in the collector's day is today's instalments only**; arrears are shown separately and the assigned daily target beside it; the figures are frozen on the handover | Owner confirmed. A day does not look like a failure because of old arrears, and expected never feeds reconciliation. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Built beyond the plan (small):** cash taken after a handover is closed to that day; a collector cannot hand over more than was recorded; a payment cannot be confirmed through the wrong route (cash through the handover only); Jest timeout raised for the database suites | Safety and test-stability follow-ons from the same decisions. |
| 2026-10-29 | **How often a circle collects is: daily, weekly, monthly, quarterly, annually, or custom ("every N days, weeks or months", N up to 60).** Quarterly is every 3 months and annually every 12, counted from the start day and clamped to month ends. Twice a month is not a separate option: choose 15 days or 2 weeks. A group that collects more often than weekly gets one overdue reminder a week | Owner request 2026-10-29 (onboarding); approved with the Custom hint. A frequency cannot be changed once the group has started. |
| 2026-10-29 | **Thrift onboarding is its own five-step setup, decided D1 to D5 as recommended:** trial includes Growth features (cap 30), step 4 optional, approval and on-behalf rule acceptance both offered, login links by email only, a new bulk endpoint. People can be staged in a draft circle and activate when it opens | Owner approved the full plan with CSV on 2026-10-29. The plan had been missing from the specs ("onboarding" was named but never defined). |
| 2026-10-28 | **A standalone group lands in Ajo and sees a menu trimmed to Ajo.** `/ajo/status` reports `tenantKind`; for `THRIFT_GROUP` the sidebars keep only Thrift Groups, Collect, Members, Notifications, Subscription, Settings and Help, and the dashboard home redirects into Ajo | Follows the owner's concern that a standalone group should not see (or pay for) the rest of SocietyLedger. Entitlements already cut the other modules off; this stops the menu showing them. |
| 2026-10-28 | **T7 web structure: member/staff split, as the app does today.** One sidebar, items gated by permission; a person holding both kinds sees both. Member side `/dashboard/my-thrift/*` (my groups, pay with proof, rules, payout account, trust, disputes); staff side `/dashboard/ajo/*` (groups, members, collections, payouts, cases); a "Collect" group shown to anyone holding `ajo.collect` (THC1–3), with the server limiting them to their assigned contributors. No separate role switch | Owner confirmed (T7 architect session). Note: the API has an `ajo.collect` permission (a collector must hold it to be assigned), so no assignment lookup is needed in the sidebar. |
| 2026-10-28 | **T7 build order, with a stop after each step:** (1) foundation + member side T7.5, (2) group admin T7.1, (3) members and payout order T7.2, (4) collections and collectors T7.3, (5) payouts and cases T7.4, (6) standalone sign-up T7.6 last. Each step is run in a browser against the real API (loading, empty, error, forbidden, locked, read-only states), the tracker and `ui-registry.md` are updated, then a short report | Owner confirmed. Member side first because proof upload, masked account numbers and rules acceptance are the risky parts. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Web payout safety = password re-entry** on payout approve, payout reverse and case close (the web has no biometrics). The dialog shows who is paid, how much, the masked account and any shortfall; the sole-approver extra confirmation and note stay. Recording paid and routine reconcile use a normal confirmation only. **API addition needed:** those three routes accept `password` in the body and check it with the existing rate-limited `confirmPassword` (`lib/reauth.ts`, used by expenses/accounting/finance) | Owner confirmed. Mirrors mobile's `useReauth`. |
| 2026-10-28 | **A standalone thrift group pays only for Ajo, never for SocietyLedger.** Association: plan + Ajo tier. Standalone: hidden zero-price `thrift-group` base plan + Ajo tier, core + Ajo modules only. Owner raised the double-payment worry; the model already avoids it, but the web checkout path for it is unproven | Owner concern, 2026-10-28 (T7 architect session). Logged so T7.6 starts by testing the Ajo-only purchase before any page is built. |
| 2026-10-28 | **A late fee is assessed once per overdue instalment, from the rule version the member accepted, and is owed only after they have been told and 48 hours have passed.** It is paid after instalments as a separate FEE credit, is the group's income, and can be waived with a reason (undone only by a recorded reversal) | Owner confirmed. A fee nobody was told about is not a fee; the notice window and the accepted-version rule are what make it fair. |
| 2026-10-28 | **A pending payment check or an open dispute pauses fees, overdue reminders and defaults for that member, with one clear day after the check ends.** A dispute never creates money and is decided by a different person | Owner confirmed. Stops anyone being charged for the time it takes the group to look at their claim. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Defaults apply only to a rotating-pot member who was paid out and then stopped paying; the procedure they accepted decides the action, and a plan is a timing promise.** Nothing is taken from others or guarantors and membership is never changed automatically | Owner confirmed. Keeps recovery a conversation for the group, with a record, rather than something the system does to people. |
| 2026-10-28 | **The trust score is per person across their Ajo groups, never blocks anyone, and excludes disputed instalments until decided** | Owner confirmed. A new group has no history, so the draw's At-risk rule needs the person's record. |
| 2026-10-28 | **A payout is calculated from the confirmed money, the gap is always shown, and late money is a supplementary payout.** Gross is what was scheduled; shortfall (with names) is what is unconfirmed; net is confirmed less the collector fee; the fee is worked out on the cumulative total and taken once | Owner confirmed. The recipient is never promised money the group does not have, one late payer cannot hold the recipient up, and every naira paid out traces to a round. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Payout timing and order:** a rotating-pot round opens when its date has come and the round before was paid; collector thrift pays after the last due date; the payout guard runs at prepare, approve and record; a slot with an open payout is held still | Owner confirmed. Keeps the promise of the draw, and closes the gap in which an approved payout could be redirected by a swap. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Three roles on a payout:** officer prepares, admin approves (never the preparer or recipient), a reconciler records (never the recipient); a sole approver or reconciler may do their own with an explicit confirmation and a note, flagged and shown to officers; a paid payout is undone only by an admin reversal | Owner confirmed. Mirrors the single-admin rule; the collector-disappears-with-the-money risk is what the product exists to prevent. |
| 2026-10-28 | **The platform levy is billed two ways: recorded and reported for staff (A), and as a separate line on the next payment-taking checkout (B).** A levy paid twice becomes a credit; unpaid levy over 60 days blocks starting a NEW group and nothing else | Owner chose A and B and the 60-day rule. The billing system has no invoice object, so the levy rides on the quote; the levy is SocietyLedger's money, not the group's, so it never enters the group ledger or a payout. |
| 2026-10-28 | **Built beyond the plan (small):** the Ajo module asks billing which add-on a tenant holds (`held-addon.service.ts`) so the money-boundary guard stays intact; money reversed after a payout was prepared stops it; the same payment can never settle a levy twice or credit itself | Safety follow-ons from the same decisions. |
| 2026-10-27 | **Admin Plan Builder: limits are generic, and an add-on-only quota is offered only on add-ons that include the Ajo module** | The editor bound every limit input to the member limit, and an empty add-on quota would have saved as "unlimited Ajo" for any add-on. |
| 2026-10-27 | **New roles `ajo_collector` and `ajo_officer`; only `admin` can approve payouts** | Separation of duties (spec §4). Treasurer and Financial Secretary get no Ajo rights by default; assign Ajo Officer. |

New known issues that aren't yet worth a full gap write-up can still be logged here; fold them into the gaps table once they're actionable._

## Session notes
_(Short notes for the next session: what was in progress, anything half-done. Clear when resolved.)_
- **2026-10-19:** F1.1/F1.2 shipped, verified only via `type-check`/`lint`/`test`/`expo export -p web` — never launched on an actual device or emulator (no Android SDK/`adb`, no Mac, owner's explicit choice for this session). Whoever picks up F1.3+ should do a real dev-client smoke test on at least one device/emulator before building much further on top, per `build-plan.md`'s own manual-check step. Mobile Phase 0 (F0.1–F0.6, backend enablement) is still entirely unstarted — F1.3 (API types & enums) needs `docs/mobile/api-contract.md` from F0.1 to exist for real, so F0.1 is the actual next blocking dependency even though F1.3 is next in the Phase 1 checklist order.

- **2026-10-26 F7.5 AI assistant:** API (`modules/ai/*`: settings, credits, privacy, context, assistant, admin usage; migration `20261026_add_ai_credits`), admin console Settings → AI section (model, credit price, minimum purchase, free requests by plan, request costs, cost vs credits sold), mobile Manage → AI assistant (chat, reminders, report, buy credits), web credits page and usage link, docs (new "Buying AI credits", others updated). Free requests per day: Starter 10, Pro 30, Enterprise 100; question 1, reminders 1, report 5; ₦20/credit, ₦3,000 minimum — all editable in the admin console. Webhook credits `AIC-` (and `SMS-`) purchases. **To do on deploy:** stop the API, `npx prisma generate`, apply the migration, set `OPENROUTER_MODEL` or leave the default `deepseek/deepseek-chat`. Not on credits yet: `ai-help.service.ts` and `ai-support.service.ts`. Verified: API 1105 tests, mobile 974, typechecks clean; not tried on a device or against live OpenRouter.
- **2026-10-26 Phase 7 review fixes:** (1) AI chat history is kept in memory only (never written to unencrypted phone storage); (2) AI permission gates aligned — mobile hides the report icon and screen without `reports.generate`, web "Buy AI Credits" needs `ledger.view`; (3) closing Paystack without paying no longer leaves a "Waiting" notice (checked once, quietly; a payment made before closing is still credited); (4) five AI mobile screens now have tests (`ai.screen.test.tsx`); (5) F7.4 ticked; deploy steps written in `docs/deployment/AI_CREDITS_DEPLOY.md`. Payments now return to the association's own address (`lib/tenant-url.ts`) for both AI and SMS.

## Billing — dynamic plans, entitlements and subscriptions (2026-10-26)
Branch `Plan-Builder`. Design agreed through `/architect`; every decision above in the Decisions log.

- [x] **Schema + migration** `20261104_plan_versions_addons_terms`: `PlanVersion` (+ modules/features), `Addon`/`AddonVersion`/`TenantAddon`, `SubscriptionTerm`, `PlanMigration`; `Subscription.planVersionId/source`; `PaymentHistory.expectedAmountKobo/quote/appliedAt/needsReview/purpose`. Backfills version 1 from each plan (launch prices applied only to the untouched old defaults 5,000/15,000/35,000; a customised plan keeps its price), copies modules/features, links every subscription, then drops the old columns/tables. Checked on a scratch database with sample old data and zero schema drift afterwards.
- [x] **Services** (`apps/api/src/services/billing/`): `money` (kobo, UTC months, proration), `catalog` (the one read path), `entitlements`, `quote` (pure price builder), `checkout`, `lifecycle` (onboarding, `activateFromPayment`, manual activate/extend/suspend/reactivate, grant add-on, deferred downgrades), `plan-builder` (drafts, publish with impact preview, archive, duplicate, add-ons), `migration` (preview/schedule/apply), `billing-notifications`, `revalidate`.
- [x] **API**: `/api/subscriptions` (plans, current, quote, initialize, change-plan, add-ons, verify), `/api/public/pricing` (no auth), `/api/admin/billing/*` (Super Admin only), validated platform settings (`onboardingPolicy`, `onboardingPlanCode`, `gracePeriodDays`, `expiryReminderDays`, `defaultTrialDays` = onboarding duration).
- [x] **Seeds**: `plans.seed.ts` creates Starter/Pro/Enterprise at 8,000/80,000, 10,000/100,000, 15,000/150,000 only when a plan is absent and never changes an existing one (verified: owner edits survive a re-run). `platform-modules.seed.ts` no longer assigns anything to plans. Entrypoint order: modules, then plans.
- [x] **Web**: shared `PlanGrid`/`BillingToggle`/`PlanComparison`/`PublicPlans` drive the landing page, homepage, pricing page and in-app chooser; checkout re-quotes on the server and lists only server-enabled providers; add-ons page; expired screen with **Resubscribe Now**; grace/expiry banners; `/api/revalidate` for instant refresh. Stale hardcoded prices and "14-day trial" copy removed from FAQ, features, testimonials, signup.
- [x] **Admin**: Plans (plans, add-ons, pricing preview, migrations), plan/add-on editor with draft, impact preview and publish; Subscriptions (all, expired, renewals in 7/30 days, payments needing attention) and a per-association page with audited actions; Settings gains onboarding policy/plan, grace period and reminder days.
- [x] **Mobile**: blocked screen shows the end date. **Docs**: plan-billing pages rewritten, new Add-ons page, Modules-by-plan reference now lists the catalogue only; 218 pages pass the frontmatter check.
- **Tests**: 135 pass: money maths (11), the quote/pricing rules (19), web pricing helpers plus a guard that fails if a plan or price is hardcoded on a pricing surface (32), access/grace/onboarding rules (24 + 6 existing approval tests), the plan-service override (4), and 39 database-backed tests (29 for versioning, onboarding, concurrent activation, amount mismatch, lockout, migrations, add-ons, manual actions and authorization; 10 for checkout over the controller with Paystack stubbed). The database suites need `BILLING_TEST_DATABASE_URL` (a disposable database whose name contains "test"); without it they skip.
- **To run**: `pnpm -F @societyledger/api exec prisma migrate deploy` (applies the migration; the container does this on start), then the entrypoint seeds. Optional env: `WEB_REVALIDATE_URL` + `REVALIDATE_SECRET` (same secret on API and web) for instant pricing refresh, `SUBSCRIPTION_NOMBA_ENABLED`, `FEATURE_FLAG_MODE`.
- **Not done / known**: docs screenshots of the changed pages are the old ones and need recapturing; member limit is not enforced on invite acceptance or the onboarding import; `__tests__/multi-tenancy/*` cannot run (they import the whole server and Jest fails on the ESM `otplib` dependency; unrelated to this work); the docs generator's permissions page fails on a spread in `ROLE_PERMISSION_MAPPING` (already failing before this work); nothing was exercised on a real Paystack account or in a browser.
- **2026-10-26 Plan allocation + real gates:** default plans rebalanced so each card tells the truth. Starter = members, ledger, payments, notifications, reports, meetings (+ members.export, members.bulk_import, ledger.export); Pro adds welfare, voting, SMS, audit and PDF/Excel exports, bulk transactions, advanced penalties, custom roles; Enterprise adds accounting, cooperatives, AI and custom report exports. Every listed feature is enforced by a route (`requireFeature`/`requireModule`); `__tests__/billing/plan-gates.test.ts` fails if a plan promises something no route enforces. Retired unenforceable features and the `integrations` module. Fixed the gate failing open on routers without `tenantId` (`tenantOf(req)`). Existing databases: run `apps/api/scripts/rebalance-default-plans.ts` (dry run by default; `--apply`, `--migrate`) because seeds never change existing plans. 163 billing/access tests pass.
- **2026-10-26 Billing review fixes:** member limit now enforced on every member-creating path (`assertMemberCapacity` in sign-up by link, setup-wizard team invites, self-created profile; `__tests__/billing/member-capacity.test.ts`); receipts for confirmed subscription payments (`GET /api/subscriptions/payments/:id/receipt`, page `/dashboard/subscription/receipt/[id]`); removed dead `calculateSubscriptionAmount`; admin 404 page uses Mantine colour variables; billing pricing components use the `primary-*` scale instead of raw `teal-*`; architecture.md and the docs (billing history, deploy checklist in `docs/billing/PLAN_BUILDER.md`) updated.
- **2026-10-26 Mobile biometric lockout fix:** signing out locked the app but left app lock on, and a password sign-in never unlocked, so a phone whose biometric prompt failed was shut out until reinstall. Now `useLogin` unlocks after a password sign-in; the unlock screen waits for the app to be in the foreground (max 2s), shows the failure code, and turns app lock off (with a message) when the device reports `not_enrolled`/`passcode_not_set`/`not_available`. Tests: `app/(lock)/unlock.test.tsx`, `features/auth/mutations.test.tsx`. Native module needs a fresh dev/APK build (`expo-local-authentication` is not in older builds).
- **2026-10-26 Mobile About legal links:** the About screen pointed at `societyledger.com` (wrong domain) and a `/licenses` page that did not exist. It now uses `EXPO_PUBLIC_WEB_URL` (default `https://societyledger.com.ng`) for `/privacy`, `/terms` and `/licenses`; new web page `apps/web/src/app/licenses` (open-source licences, linked from the footer and sitemap). Needs a new mobile build to take effect.
- **2026-10-26 Signed-in devices fix:** the API had no `DELETE /api/auth/sessions/:familyId`, so "Sign out device" always failed; added it (`revokeSessionForUser`, scoped to the signed-in user, 404 if not theirs). The mobile app never sent `X-Device-Name`/`X-Device-Platform`, so every session was "Unknown device"; `src/lib/device.ts` now sends the phone's brand and model on every request and the API stores it at sign-in. Sessions created before this fix stay unnamed. Needs an API deploy and a new mobile build.
- **2026-10-26 Member profile photos:** optional photo per member. API: `Member.photoKey/photoThumbKey/photoUpdatedAt` (migration `20261105_member_photos`), `services/member-photo.service.ts` (sharp: validates real format, straightens, crops to 512px square + 128px thumb, strips GPS/device data, replaces and deletes old files), stable six-hour signed links (`getStablePresignedUrl`) so photos cache, routes `PUT/DELETE /api/members/me/photo` (own) and `/api/members/:id/photo` (members.edit). `photoUrl`/`thumbUrl` returned by members list, detail, `/members/me`, `/auth/me`, login and payment proofs. Mobile: `Avatar` shows photos, `EditableAvatar` + `useProfilePhoto` (camera/library, square crop, remove) on Profile and the officer's member detail, thumbs in the directory, home and proof rows. Web: `MemberAvatar`, `ProfilePhotoEditor` (preview then save), `SidebarAvatar` in the three sidebars via `useMyPhoto`, My Profile, member detail, members table. Docs updated. Not yet showing photos: approvals, invitable list, role users, cooperatives guarantors, meetings, elections, announcements, comments (add `presentMemberPhoto` to each endpoint and pass `imageUrl` to its avatar). Needs `prisma generate` with the API stopped, an API deploy, a mobile rebuild, and MinIO configured (`MINIO_*`, `MINIO_PUBLIC_ENDPOINT` in production).

## Mobile (T8) decisions

| 2026-10-29 | **Mobile parity with the web is the rule for Ajo (T8).** Everything that works on the web works on the phone unless the owner removed it or it cannot be done properly, and anything that cannot must be raised with the owner. Removed by the owner: the guarantor's consent screen (the link opens in the browser for everyone, no native deep link), CSV bulk upload of contributors (typed in one at a time on mobile), and offline collection. | The owner asked for equal parity on 2026-10-29. |
| 2026-10-29 | **Navigation: no new tab.** Contributors get "My thrift" on Home and Money; staff get "Thrift" under Manage; a collector also gets "Today's collection" there. A standalone thrift group hides Home and Community and relabels Money as Thrift; Home and Money redirect into Ajo. | The owner confirmed this arrangement 2026-10-29. |
| 2026-10-29 | **Collectors are online only, with no offline queue at all.** TanStack Query pauses a mutation made offline and replays it later by default; every Ajo mutation sets `networkMode: 'always'` so it fails at once with "You're offline", and the collector screens disable their buttons and show a banner. | Money is never queued for later. |
| 2026-10-29 | **Standalone thrift groups get a trimmed mobile app, matching the web allow-list.** First build trimmed only the tab bar and the Money and Manage screens still showed the whole association app (found by the owner on a device). Fixed: Money shows My thrift, Manage keeps Members, Thrift and Settings; screens wait for the account kind. Changed: `money/index.tsx`, `manage/index.tsx`, `useAjoNav` (`resolving`), `useAjoNav.test.tsx`. **Not re-checked on a device.** | Owner correction, parity rule. |
| 2026-10-29 | **Transparency (spec §16).** Rotating pots get a Circle board (members see each other by first name and initial, payment status and payout position; never contact or bank details or trust score). Visibility is `FULL` or `OFF` per rule version, accepted by each member; "status only" dropped. Collector thrift: each saver chooses one amount for the whole cycle, then withdraws or continues (no answer means withdraw), collector fee is one contribution per saver per cycle. Mobile: a Circle tab for a standalone rotating pot, a Circle board button on the circle page for association members. Built in four phases A to D. | Owner decisions in the /architect session. |
| 2026-10-29 | **Collector-thrift cycles: how it works.** A saver's amount is fixed for the cycle and can change only before the cycle begins for them, or when they continue. A cycle is the group's `cycleLength` periods; the next cycle starts one period after the last due date (or today if the admin is late). A saver who withdraws is paid (as today) and leaves the group when the next cycle starts; a saver who continues has the fee settled to the ledger and carries the rest. The question opens three days before the last due date. | Follows the owner's decisions in the /architect session. |
| 2026-10-29 | **Transparency Phase D: what is shared and with whom.** The circle statement is for members and officers of a circle with the board on and shows only what the board shows; its verification page is public to anyone with the unguessable code and shows totals only. Circle notifications default on except the weekly summary; they never replace the individual overdue notice, which goes only to the member and officers. Accountability is for officers only. | Follows the owner's approved spec §16. |
| 2026-10-29 | **Mobile password re-entry uses a password sheet, not `useReauth`.** The API checks a password for payout approve, payout reverse and case close, and a device fingerprint or passcode cannot supply one. `useReauth` is also documented as failing on phones with no screen lock. The build-plan line "`useReauth` before approving payouts" is therefore met by the server-checked password sheet; adding a device check on top is a later choice. | Matches the web and the server. |
| 2026-10-29 | **A sheet never opens on top of another sheet (ui-rules).** Inside Ajo sheets, choices (collector, outcome, payout position) are tick lists, not `SelectSheet`s. | `SelectSheet` opens its own bottom sheet. |
